# MKG: changes noted during the day, to be applied when the MKG is updated at the end of the day

The code and the schemas change all day. The MKG is not rebuilt for each change: the changes are noted here, and the MKG is
rebuilt and published once, at the end of the day (the author, 2026/10/11).

## 2026/10/11
- **PAF-CRX becomes PAF-VCR** (Videoconference Client Receiver, as VCT is the Transmitter), applied to the L2 from the author's
  figure of 2026/10/11 (fourth; "AV" became "MM"): four sub-AIMs, PDX -> VSC -> **MSC** -> RSR. **PAF-AVC (Audio-Visual Scene
  Creation) is now PAF-MSC (Multimodal Scene Creation)** - the acronym MSC is mine, to be confirmed; file renamed to
  MultimodalSceneCreation.json. VSC takes and gives the whole Multimodal Scene (OSD-BMS), because RSR needs the full MM scene, not
  only the visual part (its output was OSD-BVS). Inputs of VCR: Portable Avatar, Point of View (to RSR). Outputs: Point of View
  (RSR passes it on), Speaking Avatars (XRV-SAV), Multimodal Scene Descr. (OSD-BMS), Output Text (from PDX). PDX -> VSC: MM Scene
  Descr., Avatars, Spatial Attitudes. VSC -> MSC: MM Scene Descr., Avatars, Mouth Spatial Attitudes. MSC -> RSR: MM Scene
  Descriptors, Avatars. PDX -> RSR directly: Personal Statuses, Input Speeches. L2 changes: VSC lost its Personal Status input and
  output; MSC has no speech or personal-status input; RSR gained an optional OutputPointOfView (OSD-OPV); the port labels
  "AVSceneDescriptors"/"AudioVisualSceneDescriptors" became "MultimodalSceneDescriptors" (PDX, VSC, MSC)..
  Sixth figure (same day): "Avatars - IDs": the Avatar ID (OSD-IID) travels with the avatar PDX -> VSC -> MSC -> RSR (no longer PDX ->
  MSC directly) and comes out with the Speaking Avatars (new VCR output OutputAvatarID). VSC and MSC gain an OutputAvatarID output; RSR an
  optional AvatarID input and OutputAvatarID output. This closes the Avatar ID assumption.
  Fifth figure (same day): the avatars' Spatial Attitudes (OSD-OSA) also travel PDX -> VSC -> MSC -> RSR, beside the mouth's, so that RSR
  can put each into its Speaking Avatar. VSC has two OSA outputs (1 Mouth, 2 Spatial Attitude); MSC two OSA inputs (1 Mouth, 2 Spatial
  Attitude) and an OSA output; RSR an optional OSA input. Ports of the same Data Type are told by Port Number.
  Files: PAF/V1.6/AIMs VideoconferenceClientReceiver, VisualSceneCreation, MultimodalSceneCreation (was AudioVisualSceneCreation),
  PortableAvatarDemultiplexing, ResponseAndSceneRendering; Test/Expected/l2-topology.json, schema.json. MetadataTests Schema,
  L2Topology, Conformance and Connections pass. The old PAF-CRX V1.6 is published on schemas.mpai.community (2026/10/09-10): to be
  replaced, and PAF-AVC V1.6 (AudioVisualSceneCreation.json) removed there if it was published.
  Seventh figure (same day): the Input Speeches go PDX -> **MSC** (not RSR); MSC adds each avatar's speech at its face and gives the
  Speaking Avatars with their IDs (XRV-SAV + OSD-IID) instead of the avatar models; RSR takes the Speaking Avatars (new optional input
  InputSpeakingAvatars), adds the Personal Statuses (EPS) to the avatars' speech, face and body, and gives the Speaking Avatars - IDs, the
  MM Scene Descr. and the Point of View. VSC gives the Spatial Attitude of the avatar's mouth - of a characteristic point of its face, from which the model gives the mouth (port label MouthSpatialAttitude, the author's name).
  The Avatar is no longer only a model to animate: it is an animated and speaking avatar (a model with its animation information); RSR is
  substantially enhanced - its GSD adds the Speech PS to the speech, GFD the Face PS to the face, GBD the Body PS to the body. Its L2 and
  its sub-AIM topology are still those of the response-rendering RSR: to be reworked. No registration exists: an ABV User Agent is to be
  defined (a web page where participants register with a nickname and an avatar), and the User Agents of the ABV Modules.
  Author's answers (same day): the animation of an avatar is its Face and Body Descriptors; the Entity State is not used in the ABV, so the
  Portable Avatar (PAF-PAV) loses EntityState and gains FaceDescriptorsObject, BodyDescriptorsObject and EntityPersonalStatus (schema edited);
  a Module has access to the data of an ID (model-or-ID: no change); the nickname IS the Avatar ID; avatars are introduced at start time and then
  referenced - a transmitted Speaking Avatar is the Avatar ID + Face and Body Descriptors + Speech; the AVS User Agent places the avatars around the
  table at start time, in the order of arrival; the VCT and the VCR run in each client; the VMS is also part of the meeting; at least 3-4 avatars.
  "Avatars - IDs" on the VCR arrows = the PAF-AVT (the avatar model with its animation - Face and Body Descriptors - and its ID), not OSD-B3O (author):
  PDX output Avatar (was AvatarModel, OSD-B3O) is PAF-AVT; VSC input Avatar and output OutputAvatar, MSC input Avatar, and the four VCR edges
  carry PAF-AVT. PDX still gives FDO and BDO on their own ports.
  EIGHTH FIGURE (same day): **Visual Scene Creation (PAF-VSC) is removed** (author: its function was minimal): the VCR is PDX -> MSC -> RSR. MSC takes from PDX the
  MM Scene Descr., the Avatars - IDs (PAF-AVT, OSD-IID), the Spatial Attitudes and the Input Speeches; gives the MM Scene Descr., the Speaking Avatars - IDs (XRV-SAV, OSD-IID)
  and the Spatial Attitudes to RSR. The face/mouth point is a standard 1.6 m above the avatar's Spatial Attitude (author), no output for it. The avatar's ID is the nickname; its
  placement is its Spatial Attitude; the model is not in the scene because there is nobody yet to display it. VSC's L2 (VisualSceneCreation.json), L3 and code are deleted.
  NINTH FIGURE (same day): the MM Scene Descr. goes MSC -> RSR -> boundary (not MSC -> boundary); the Point of View goes to the RSR (and, as before, round it); MSC -> RSR
  carries "Speeches" (OSD-BSO). Author, same message: the Body Descriptors should simply be the OSA of the nodes between joints (a change to the content of PAF-BDO / its
  TFA qualifier: not made, to be confirmed).
  Body Descriptors Object (author: it is an Object, select a format and add a Qualifier): a new content format in TFA/V1.5/formats/BodyDescriptorsContentFormats.json -
  "OSA (Spatial Attitudes of the nodes between joints, OSD-OSA-V1.5)" - the Data a JSON array of OSD-OSA objects, the TFA-BDQ Qualifier naming the format; C#
  BodyDescriptorsObject.NodeAttitudes and FromNodeAttitudes. GBD still gives BVH (Thalia's avatar body uses it): not changed.
  CAE-UCM (User Command): new field UserCommandData.RevokedUserCommandID - the ID of an earlier User Command to revoke; the AIM that executed it undoes it if it can; the report
  says Done / Failed / NotSupported under the action RevokedUserCommandID. Nothing else changes (no new AIM, no AIF change); see docs/ASM-Commands-and-Revocation.md.
  AOE, ASE, SOE, SSE and OSD-BME now undo a revoked User Command (UndoJournal in AIMs/Core; the last command first); no AIM Metadata change.
  RSR and SAF (author's figures of 2026/10/11, same as the L2s: RSR = MMC-PDX + MMC-TTS + PAF-SAF; SAF = GFD + GBD + SAS): the pass-throughs (Point of View, Spatial
  Attitude, MM Scene Descr.) are external to the RSR - in the VCR the Point of View goes boundary to boundary and the scene from the MSC straight to the boundary. The VCR now feeds
  the RSR with the Avatar (PAF-AVT, its Avatar Model input), the speech (its Input Speech), the Avatar ID, the Spatial Attitude and the Personal Status; the RSR's SAS forms the
  Speaking Avatar. MSC gives therefore the Avatar, the speech, the ID and the Spatial Attitude, not an XRV-SAV. RSR L2: only AvatarID and SpatialAttitude inputs and an
  OutputAvatarID output added to the committed one.
  VCR code (AIMs/PAF/V1.6/VCR, Mpai.Paf.Vcr, continuous AIMs, each with an L3 and tests): PDX (1PAF-PDX-V1.6-I01) and MSC (1PAF-MSC-V1.6-I01: places each
  avatar in the BMS at its Spatial Attitude, adds its speech at the mouth, gives the Speaking Avatar = an Avatar (PAF-AVT) + speech, author 2026/10/11). The
  Spatial Attitude is not inside the SAV: it travels on its own port. Not yet: the RSR enhancement, the VCR composite L3, a Controller-level run.
  Head model HDM (OSD-HDM-V1.5, in OSD - the standard is to be confirmed): a parametric head to begin with (author) - a sphere (radius 8.75 cm), the mouth
  in front of the characteristic point of the face, the speech radiating as a dipole (weight, power): schema OSD/V1.5/data/HeadModel.json.
  PAF-PDX (the VCR's) is now code: AIMs/PAF/V1.6/PDX (Mpai.Paf.Pdx), a continuous AIM, L3 1PAF-PDX-V1.6-I01, six tests (PdxTests); the ID of each
  object of an avatar carries the Avatar ID before a '#'.
  To do: the 3OD/SOD Units: the SOD needs a head model for the propagation of each avatar's speech (author); each avatar must carry
  its nickname as given by the participant (where: open); the MKG's Units list (units.json) to be updated for the 3OD (Speaking Avatars, BMS) and SOD.
- **PAF-FPE is a leaf; PAF-BPE is new (a leaf)** (author, 2026/10/11): Face and Body Personal Status Extraction, used by MMC-PSE. FPE L2: no
  Sub-AIMs (it had EFD and PFI), FaceObject and optional FaceDescriptors in, FacePersonalStatus out. BPE L2 (BodyPersonalStatusExtraction.json):
  BodyObject (OSD-VIO) and optional BodyDescriptors in, BodyPersonalStatus (MMC-BPS) out; no L3 and no code (there is no body-emotion
  model). MMC-PSE lists FPE and BPE as Sub-AIMs in place of EFD, PFI, EBD and PBI (EFD, EBD, PFI and PBI stay as AIMs). The RSR needs GFD and
  GBD, not FPE and BPE. Test/Expected re-recorded; all 11 MetadataTests pass.
- **XRV-SAV (Speaking Avatar)** gains the Spatial Attitude of the avatar in the scene (author's edit of SpeakingAvatar.json,
  not yet committed): there is no longer one Speaking Avatar but several, each carrying its OSA, so that the 3OD places each in
  the scene. The 3OD Unit takes the Speaking Avatar (and the BMS): its ports in units.json to be updated accordingly.
  (The edit writes the property "Spatial Attitude" and the $ref ".../Spatial Attitude.json", with a space: SpatialAttitude?)

- All DT semantics tables rebuilt from the schemas (31 pages). Fixed schemas: REParticipantStatus (nesting), AudioBehaviour (typo), Event (AVSceneDescriptors).

- 2026-10-12: CAE-ASM-V1.0 L2 (schemas/CAE3/V1.0/AIMs/AudioSceneManager.json) now includes the voice interface: SubAIMs MMC-ASR, MMC-EDP, MMC-TTS; new boundary ports VoiceRequest (BSO:2 in), VoiceLanguage (SEL in), VoiceReply (BSO:2 out), VoiceCommand (UCM:6 out), VoiceReplyText (BTO out); existing BSO boundary ports now numbered 1.

## 2026-10-14 ASM, ABV and the AIF spec (for the end-of-day MKG rebuild; the entries of 2026-10-11 and 2026-10-12 on PDX, FPE and the ASM ports are SUPERSEDED by this one)
**ASM (pushed, mpai-cav 4446648)**
- CAE-ASM-V1.0 L2 and AMD: voice inside the Module (SubAIMs MMC-ASR, MMC-EDP, MMC-TTS). Boundary Ports: VoiceRequest BSO:2 in, TypedRequest BTO:1 in, TextToSpeak BTO:2 in, VoiceScene BAS:2 in, AudioObjectFromOutside AUO in; VoiceReply BSO:2 out, VoiceCommand UCM:6 out, HeardText BTO:1 out, VoiceReplyText BTO:2 out, TextForUA TFU out, MadeAudioObject AUO out. The existing BSO and BAS boundary Ports are numbered 1. VoiceLanguage (SEL in) is in the L2 only: the ASR AMD has no language input.
- CAE-AOE L3/AMD: AUO in and AUO out. User Command semantics: AddedObjects sent to Audio Object Editing composes an Audio Object (report ResultID); MovedObjects/RemovedObjects may name the Object by its PoV alone (no identifier). EDP remembers the last Scene it was told; ASE ignores a Scene id the storage does not hold.
- The ASM UA runs Apps/ASM/ASM.orch with the workflow interpreter (one device per Port); no AIM, no storage in the UA.
- **AIF spec - to be applied to mpai.community by the author:** Workflow Description Table 1 gains `ask Controller to keep storage in "<where>"` (calls MPAI_AIFU_SharedStorage_Init); one sentence each in Basic API 3.5 and Storage (Reaching Storage). Text and reasons: Downloads\Spec-Updates-WDL-keep-storage.html. The site is NOT yet changed.

**ABV (merged with the parallel work, pushed)**
- PAF-PDX (L2, AMD, code Mpai.Paf.Pdx): the Avatar is the Avatar data type PAF-AVT (author's ruling 2026/10/14), with its ID, 3D model, Face/Body Descriptors and, where it speaks, its speech; PMX still takes the Avatar Model (OSD-B3O). The '#' part IDs and Mpai.Paf.Vcr's own PDX are gone.
- PAF-VCR L2 as slide 27: PDX - MSC - RSR, Continuous, Portable Avatars and Summary External; PAF-AVT flows kept. PAF-MSC (new AIM, schema + AMD + code Mpai.Paf.Vcr): takes the data of one Portable Avatar together (ID, Avatar, Spatial Attitude). VSC and AVC no longer used; schemas AudioVisualSceneCreation.json and VisualSceneCreation.json deleted (slides 3 and 31 removed by the author).
- Portable Avatar: EntityState kept (it holds only the Personal Status: Cognitive State, Emotion, Social Attitude), FaceDescriptorsObject, BodyDescriptorsObject added; no PersonalStatus field. MMC EntityState.json: only Header and EntityStateID required, plus at least one element (anyOf) - a change to a data type schema.
- PAF-FPE stays a leaf (author's ruling); PAF-BPE new leaf; VCT needs Body/Face Personal Statuses.

## 2026-10-14 - the Portable Avatar replaces the Speaking Avatar in PAF (slides of 2026-10-14)
- AIM renames: PAF-SAF "Portable Avatar Formation", PAF-SAS "Portable Avatar Synthesis" (acronyms kept); files PortableAvatarFormation.json, PortableAvatarSynthesis.json. XRV-SAV is no longer an Output or Input of any PAF/MMC/CAV/MAS AIM; the XRV standard's own SpeakingAvatar.json stays.
- Avatar Model input of SAS, SAF, RSR and of the composites that use them is OSD-B3O-V1.5 (was PAF-PAV); outputs are PAF-PAV-V1.6 (PortableAvatar). SAR takes a Portable Avatar. RSR composite per slide 17 (PoV through; Avatar Sp. Att. and MM Scene Descr. into SAF). SAF/SAS get optional Avatar Spatial Attitude (OSD-OSA) and AV Scene Descriptors (OSD-BMS) inputs (the RSR slide feeds them; the SAF slides do not show them). PAF-VCR is one basic AIM (slide 23). PAF-PMX: Speech Object optional. MMC-AMQ = ASR + TIQ + PMX (slide 3), output Portable Avatar.
- Decks: "Speaking Avatar" -> "Portable Avatar" in AIH, CUI, CVE, GME, MPAI AIWs and AIMs2, PGM (31 places; originals in the session scratchpad pptx-backup). Those decks still call PAF-SAR "Portable Avatar Rendering" (the PAF deck: Scene and Avatar Rendering).

## 2026-10-15 (night) - ABV aligned to the slides
- PAF-MSC retired; **PAF-VCR is one AIM** (L2 VideoconferenceClientReceiver.json, L3 1PAF-VCR-V1.6-I01, code Mpai.Paf.Vcr.VideoconferenceClientReceiver; the author: "VCR adds speech to Avatars, one AIM"). It holds each avatar's state by ID, merges updates, seats avatars at the Spatial Attitude of the Portable Avatar, adds the speech at the mouth (1.6 m), shows a default avatar (setting DefaultAvatar) when no model was ever sent; PoV and Text pass through. MultimodalSceneCreation.json, 1PAF-MSC-V1.6-I01 deleted.
- PAF-AVS L2 (slide 3): two Portable Avatar inputs, PAF-PAV-V1.6 Port 1 (straight to PMX, not acted upon) and Port 2 (to PDX, translation); the AVS adds room (AV Scene Descriptors) and seat (Spatial Attitude) to the participants' Portable Avatars.
- PAF-VMS L2/L3 (slide 25): boundary input MachineID (string) into PSD (the secretary's own ID, PMX requires the ID).
- MMC-SCM L2/L3: PersonalStatus input optional (a speaker without Personal Status is still minuted). L3 1MMC-EDP-V2.5-I02: PersonalStatus optional (as the L2).
- PAF-PDX: ID always, everything else only when the Portable Avatar has it - no placeholders, no neutral Personal Status. PAF-PMX: where it starts from an Input Portable Avatar, the Model already there is kept (the server's PMX adds the ID).
- Meaning (MMC-MEA) is discontinued: read Text Descriptors Object (MMC-TDO); Text Descriptors (MMC-TXD) discontinued; Intention is MMC-INT-V2.5 (D:\AI Intention.json: qtopic, qfocus, qLAT, qSAT, qdomain). The L2s of TTT, QAM, AQM, NLU, SCM still to align.
- WDL interpreter (UserAgent/Rca): a literal may also be an ID (`offer MachineID (string:1) = "Secretary"`).
- OPEN (author's ruling needed): PMX "updates on any arrival, ID the only required input" does not fit the Controller's firing (one Message per required input per firing): inside PSD the model, speech, face and body come from different AIMs without the ID. VmsTests.SecretarySpeaks and App are skipped for this reason (see the comment in VmsTests.cs). Options: PMX continuous (state by ID) or a retained ID input.
- Proposal for the author: Downloads\AIF-V3.0-Proposal-Link-on-Request.md (COM-9a, Basic API 3.10.4-6, WDL steps link/unlink) for the private message between participants.
- Access folder: D:\DI\Access\Avatars\RocketboxAvatars (moved from Models\Rocketbox), Access/ in .gitignore.
- Rebuild the MKG for: PAF-VCR, PAF-AVS, PAF-VMS, MMC-SCM, MMC-EDP L3.

## 2026-10-15 (day) - PMX/ID resolved, CAV launcher
- **PAF-PMX-V1.6-I02 (new L3, continuous)** resolves the OPEN firing point above: PMX I01 fired on the ID alone, before the content. I02 runs on its own Ports (IAimRunner, code Mpai.Paf.Pmx.ContinuousPmx), holds the Avatar ID (still required, as in the L2), sends an update on each arrival - the Model once, then what changed; a state unchanged is not sent again; what arrives before the ID waits for it; what arrives together goes out as one update. Input Ports Depth 8. The PSD L3 (1PAF-PSD-V1.6-I01) and the VMS provider use I02; the ABV demo server still uses I01.
- WDL/App: Apps/VMS/PAF-VMS.orch - the Secretary's ID comes from the Secretary device (`stream MachineID (string:1) from device "Secretary"`), once; `offer` only queues data until a `give`. Mpai.Rca SecretaryDevice gives the ID. VmsTests SecretarySpeaks and App pass.
- CAV: launcher CAV/Cav.bat (seven use cases live; animations page CAV/Demos); the live test gains the junction modes junction-route, junction-lights, junction-nothing; the viewer draws a crossing road, turned vehicles and indicators. Planned tonight: overtaking a car that turns off at a junction (Downloads\CAV-Overtaking-at-a-Junction.md).
- ABV demo (ABV/): server, browser client, protocol, avatars (Rocketbox, MIT) pushed; the Secretary types the minutes.
- Rebuild the MKG for: PAF-PMX I02, PAF-PSD L3, PAF-VMS (device ID).

## 2026-10-15 (evening) - MMM: a meeting as services (Phases 1-6)
- MMM implementation (D:\DI\MMM): axes x east, y north, z up; Orientation yaw, pitch, roll (yaw 0 north, provisional); MM-Capture (simplified scene); first-person client in the viewer; a meeting as services - room Location with seat Locations, AVS Service (Process: convening, invitations, seats, rights), Secretary (machine Process with its own Persona, minutes), ASR Service (Process, Whisper), Speech Objects with their own duration (OSD-BSO), minutes for the participants only. Doc: docs/MMM-Meeting.md.
- Rules from the author: a User may have many Personae, all its own (it brings one to an event); the places of a meeting are assigned by the AVS Service; who is in is a human decision; no meeting code.
- Not in MMM-TEC (flagged): GET processes/{id}/inbox; Discover not implemented; a removed participant's Persona stays in its seat.
- Rebuild the MKG for: nothing new in the standards; the MMM implementation notes only.

## 2026-10-16 - MMM: hearing (Phase 7)
- A User's speech is placed at its seat as a Speech Object (MM-Add; the AVS Service grants the right with the seat); MM-Capture returns it (kind Speech); the participants read its data and play it from the seat; the Secretary hears the room by capturing it (no second copy sent). MM-Add can make a Speech Object where it is added. MM-Capture is not in the Activity Data. Whisper noise-word filter of ABV ported. Doc: docs/MMM-Meeting.md.
- Rebuild the MKG for: nothing new in the standards.

## 2026-10-16 (later) - MMM: streamed Speech Objects (Phase 8)
- A Speech Object is declared when speech starts (time declared, not redefined until over) with its Speech Qualifier TFA-SPQ-V1.5 (PCM; StreamFormat WebSocket) and a DataURI (a WebSocket address); the data goes out as it is captured; whoever has chosen to hear that User (the receiver decides: from A, not from C) opens the address and receives it from the start, played from the seat. The Secretary takes it when it is over. First sound about 0.1-0.3 s after the Speech Object began (localhost, warm).
- Schema: "WebSocket" added to TFA/V1.5/formats/SpeechStreamFormats.json (the Speech Qualifier's stream formats) in D:\DI\schemas, to be published on schemas.mpai.community by the author.
- Rebuild the MKG for: nothing new in the standards.

## 2026-10-17 AIH-HSP V1.1 schemas (EI branch ei-restructure)
- schemas/AIH1/V1.1/data/AIHData.json: SmartContractID and DeIDAndAnonym are no longer required in AIHData (the Front End makes AIH Data before the Back End makes the Smart Contract); the author's decision.
- New data type AIH-TPU-V1.1, Third-Party User Profile (schemas/AIH1/V1.1/data/ThirdPartyUserProfile.json): an organisation (CompanyName, RegistrationCode, Address, Country, Email) and its Entitlements in the terms of the AIH Taxonomies and the Model Licence (UserTypes = DataUser, DataClasses = DataClass, DataStatus, ProcessTypes = AIH Data Processing Type, AnomalyTypes = DataAnomaly, UsageTypes = DataUsage). The Third-Party User is a class of user next to the End User.
- schemas/AIH1/V1.1/data/Register.json: the Request carries either a UserProfile (End User) or a ThirdPartyUserProfile (Third-Party User), exactly one of the two.
- Earlier AIH schema corrections (2026-10-14), not yet noted here: Audit.json (Request and Response could not appear: outer additionalProperties removed), DeIDAndAnonym.json (alternatives made exclusive, enum keyword, required fields), EHRObject.json (required fields).
- 2026-10-18 NN Model wrapped by an AIM (author: "do it, no far-range impacts"): what the Back End uploads to the MPAI Store and the Front Ends download is an AIM that wraps the NN Model. schemas/AIH1/V1.1/data/FederatedLearn.json (AIH-FDL-V1.1): optional PreLearnAIMID in the Request and optional PostLearnAIMID in the Response (identifier in the MPAI Store of the wrapping AIM); the model weights keep travelling as AIP-MLO between HFL and the Front Ends; no Port or other Data Type changed. New L2 schemas/AIH1/V1.1/AIMs/NeuralNetworkModel.json (AIM AIH-NNM-V1.1, "Neural Network Model": input AIH-AHD-V1.1, output AIH-ARD-V1.1, as Anomaly and Risk Alerting; the Store publishes the wrapping AIM only if its L3 conforms to it). Each wrapped model is an AIM of its own (AIMName 1AIH-NNM-V1.1-I01, -I02, ...), the model being a file the Store keeps and fingerprints (SHA-256). On the web: a row for AIH-NNM in the AIH-HSP AIMs page;  AIH-FDL row of the Data Types page and the HBE/HFE Operation text (the model is uploaded to and downloaded from the Store as an AIM).
- 2026-10-18 HFE L2 schemas/AIH1/V1.1/AIMs/HealthFrontEnd.json: AIH-NNM-V1.1 is a further Sub-AIM of the Health Front End (an L3 may leave it out), with two Topology lines: AIH Data from AIH Data Processing to it, ARA Data from it to AIH Data Processing, as for Anomaly and Risk Alerting. Reason: a Module has no AIM its Metadata does not declare (AIF CTR-1), so a Front End has the AIM that wraps a model when the Metadata of the Front End names it; the Back End publishes a new version of the Front End's L3 with it. On the web: the Health Front End page (Sub-AIMs and Operation).
- AIF V3.0 (not schemas): proposed text for the Controller's AIM repository and the AIM Licence is in Downloads (AIF-V3-AIM-repository-and-licence-proposed-text.md), with the redrawn Figure 1 (PNG and SVG). Nothing changed on the web.
- 2026-10-18 L1 schemas/AIF/V3.0/data/AIMMetadata.json: new optional top-level property AIMLicence, whose value is the AIM Licence Data Type (AIF-LIC, below) as the Implementer's OFFER: Licensor, Grant, Term (Duration), Updates, the price (Payment.Value, an MPAI-MMM Value), TermsURI; it has no Licensee and no Signature (the Metadata is published once, for everybody; the L1 schema refuses an AIMLicence that has either). Omitted means the AIM needs no AIM Licence. Inherited by every L2 and L3. On the web: the AIF V3.0 Metadata page (a row in the table of the AIM Metadata fields) and the MPAI Store page. Test: AimLicenceFieldTests.
- 2026-10-18 New AIF data type AIF-LIC, AIM Licence (schemas/AIF/V3.0/data/AIMLicence.json, Header AIF-LIC-V3.0), one Data Type in two states: the OFFER (the AIMLicence field of AIM Metadata: Licensor, Grant, Term Duration, Updates, Payment.Value, TermsURI) and the ISSUED AIM Licence the MPAI Store signs for a Controller that obtains the AIM (adds AIMLicenceID, AIMLicenceTime, AIM with the files covered and their approved fingerprints, Licensee, Term NotBefore, Status, Payment.Reference, Signature and KeyID - which come together with the Signature). Grant: Run, Keep, RunOnLicenseeHosts, ProvideService; limits; excluded purposes; Sub-AIMs covered. Price and payment are an MPAI-MMM Value (MMM-VAL-V2.2). On the web: a row in Table 1 of the AIF V3.0 Data Types page and a page AIM Licence (Definition, Functional Requirements, Syntax, Semantics; text in Downloads). Tests: AimLicenceSchemaTests. Decided by the author the same day: the Licensor is the MPAI Store (Licensor is issued only; the Implementer-Store relation is outside the AIM Licence); Grant.Rights gains RunOnThirdPartyHosts (whether the AIM may run on the AIM host of another party is part of the licence); usage reporting is a term of the licence (Obligations). And: the Licensee is the holder of a PTF Trust Anchor (TrustAnchorID; every Controller whose Instance Credential chains to it is covered; ControllerID optional) - not a Controller, which can be created and destroyed; Status gains MaxAge (ISO 8601 duration: how long the status may go unconfirmed, after which a Controller asks the Store or does not use the AIM); OrganisationID and Licensor.StoreID are gone. Then: the price and the payment are an MPAI-MMM Service Pricing Model (MMM-SPM-V2.2; offer: SPMStatus Model with the pricing model offered; issued: SPMStatus Final with Chosen and Settlement, whose Transaction MMM-TRA is the proof of acquisition) in place of Amount/Currency/Reference; Grant.SubAIMs is removed: an AIM - whatever its nature and hierarchy - is an AIM, and its AIM Licence is the licence of an AIM. Then (author, 2026-10-18): the concept of an AIM host is dropped from the architecture - an AIM executes under the control of a Controller, with it or apart from it as its Relation states; Grant.Rights is now Run (execution under the control of the licensee, wherever), Keep, RunUnderOtherControl, ProvideService (RunOnLicenseeHosts, RunOnThirdPartyHosts and Licensee.AIMHosts are gone). On the web: Section 8 of the Controller page and every other mention of AIM hosts (listed in C4 of M3271) and Figure 1 (the box AIM host removed; an AIM drawn executing apart).
- To upload on schemas.mpai.community: AIF/V3.0/data/AIMLicence.json, AIF/V3.0/data/AIMMetadata.json, HealthFrontEnd.json (AIMs), NeuralNetworkModel.json (AIMs), FederatedLearn.json, AIHData.json, ThirdPartyUserProfile.json, Register.json (and Audit.json, DeIDAndAnonym.json, EHRObject.json if not yet uploaded). On the web: the AIH-HSP V1.1 Data Types page needs the new AIH-TPU row and the changed Register and AIH Data descriptions.
- Rebuild the MKG for: AIH-HSP data types (new AIH-TPU; AIH-AHD and AIH-REG changed).

## 2026-10-17 ASM as three programs: how the Controller API is carried for a Continuous Module
- No schema changed by this entry. Apps/MAS/Server: for a Module whose L3 says Execution Continuous the Input and Output routes relay InputWrite and OutputRead (IContinuousModuleRunner); the Output GET accepts ?timeout=<ms>; other Modules are run per exchange as before. RemoteControllerApi sends ?timeout=. Status, StopAim, SharedStorageInit, Pause and Resume are still not carried (MAS Phase 15).
- AIF/PortData: JsonObjectCodec registered for OSD-BAO, OSD-BAS, OSD-BSS, OSD-BMS, OSD-AUO, OSD-OPV and CAE-UCM (the instance of the schema, the same inside and on the wire); PortDataSchema knows their schema files.
- Apps/CAE/ASM: Server (the Controller, no AIM), Host (the folder the AIM host runs the AIMs from), Start-Asm.ps1, README. AIF.AimHost pre-loads ONNX Runtime from the provider's runtimes folder (Windows has its own onnxruntime.dll in System32).
- Web: the AIF User Agent page may say that a Continuous Module is written and read, not run per exchange, over the routes; the ASM page loses the vocal interface (see the entry above).

## 2026-10-18 AIH-HSP V1.1 reference software, first slice (EI branch ei-restructure)
- L3s (AIMs/AMDs/1AIH-*-V1.1-I01.json): every input Port of HDP, STR, ADT, DIA, HFL, HFE and HBE is IsOptional (an AIM runs on whatever request reaches it; a composite's boundary inputs are independent requests); HFE and HBE declare Execution Continuous (their wiring has cycles - HDP.AHD -> ARA -> HDP - which a Module run per exchange cannot do). The L2s in schemas/AIH1/V1.1/AIMs say the same (IsOptional on 29 input Ports of AIHDataProcessing, Storage, Auditing, DeIdentificationAndAnonymisation, HealthFederatedLearning, HealthFrontEnd, HealthBackEnd), since an L3 may not make optional what its L2 requires; this follows the author's answer that HDP handles one request at a time if executable - TO BE CONFIRMED by the author (Downloads\AIH-HSP-questions-and-decisions.md). To upload on schemas.mpai.community: those seven L2 files; on the web, the Ports tables of those AIMs.
- No new Data Type beyond AIH-TPU. Conventions that the Data Types leave open (asked of the author): the requester of a Licence Confirm in DescrMetadata; the identifier of a de-identified copy (<id>-deid, <id>-anon); an audit that finds nothing is a Response with an empty AIHDataIDs (the Audit schema says minItems 1: to be corrected).
- Software: AIMs/AIH/V1.1 (ARA, MDL, STR, HDP, DIA, ADT, HFL, Provider, Chain), Apps/AIH/HSP (HFE.orch, HBE.orch, the User Agents of the Front End and the Back End, the security model of AIF with the Back End as Trust Authority). Tests: AihAimsTests, AihHdpTests, AihBlockchainTests, AihFrontEndModuleTests, AihBackEndModuleTests, AihTrustTests, AihFirstSliceTests.
- Rebuild the MKG for: AIH-HSP AIMs and the new software nodes (AIMs/AIH, Apps/AIH/HSP).
## 2026-10-18 AIH-HSP V1.1: the Storage AIM disappears (author: Private Storage is part of the AIF infrastructure; reference models in AIH Reference Models.pptx)
- schemas/AIH1/V1.1/AIMs/Storage.json removed (AIH-STR-V1.1 is no AIM). AIH Data Processing keeps and gives back AIH Data through the storage API of the Module (Secure Data Vault of a Front End, Secure Storage of the Back End).
- HealthFrontEnd.json and HealthBackEnd.json: Sub-AIM AIH-STR and its Topology lines removed; the boundary Ports of Storage - AIH Data Process Request #2/#3 in and Response out - removed (the AIH Data Process request and response of HDP serve retrieval too). HealthBackEnd.json: boundary AIH Data inputs #1 (to Auditing), #2 (to De-Identification and Anonymisation), #3 (to AIH Data Processing, to be kept) and AIH Data output #1 (the copy De-Identification made), as in the reference model of the Health Back End V1.1.
- On the web: the Front End, Back End and AIH Data Processing pages (Sub-AIMs, Operation), the Storage page, Table of AIMs; the AIH Data Process request/response no longer go to Storage.
- schemas/AIH1/V1.1/data/LicenceConfirm.json: Request Time is now a Simple Time (a change made in the worktree on 2026-10-18 14:50 by the author); the software follows.
- 2026-10-18 (answers of the author in M3272): DeIDAndAnonym.json: the Request carries the End User's Tokens (required: the service is granted if the Tokens grant De-ID and Anonym) and the Response carries NewAIHDataID, the identifier of the resulting copy. Audit.json: the Request carries the Tokens (required); the Response may be empty (AIHDataIDs without minItems: an audit that finds no violations) and has ProcessingState (false if the request was refused for its Tokens). The Tokens are checked by AIH Data Processing, where the End Users are registered; a Third-Party User, granted AIHDataProcessing only, is refused De-ID and Audit.
- 2026-10-18: HealthFrontEnd.json and HealthBackEnd.json: a boundary output ARA Data (AIH-ARD-V1.1; Anomaly and Risk Alerting's ARA Data goes to AIH Data Processing and to the boundary): the alert is the ARA Data, there is no alert response of AIH Data Processing. The AIM that wraps a model (AIH-NNM) gives its ARA Data to the boundary too. On the web: the Front End and Back End pages (Input/Output tables: ARA Data output; Operation: the alert), the De-ID and Anonym and Audit data types (Tokens, NewAIHDataID, ProcessingState).
- 2026-10-18 (answer of the author): schemas/AIH1/V1.1/data/LicenceConfirm.json: Request gets RequesterID (required): the End User or Third-Party User when the Back End asks for AIH Data Processing; the party whose operation is audited (the Back End) when it asks for Auditing; EndUserID stays the End User the request is made for. The software no longer carries the requester in DescrMetadata (RequestedBy= removed). Web: Licence Confirm Data Type page (Request table, JSON example).
- 2026-10-18: new software, no standard change: AIMs/AIH/V1.1/Chain/EthereumBlockchain.cs (IBlockchain over an Ethereum JSON-RPC node), Keccak256.cs, EvmAbi.cs, Contracts/AihLicence.sol (the Smart Contract of AIH-HSP for experiments, same rules as HashChainBlockchain), Contracts/README.md; test AihEthereumTests.cs. Rebuild the MKG for the new nodes.
- 2026-10-18: MPAI Store Service (reference software, no schema change): Store API additions not in the AIF V3.0 text - GET /MPAI/Store/Register (page), POST /MPAI/Store/Implementer (register, gives the Implementer ID), GET /MPAI/Store/Implementer/{id}; an L3 whose Implementer ID is not assigned is refused (403); the whole L3 is validated against the AIM Metadata schema (L1) as well as its L2; the SHA-256 of BinaryName.dll in a local package is recorded in approved/<AIMName>.json (STR-3). Store App asks for the Implementer ID and checks it with the Service. Web: MPAI Store page, 5 Store API (registration routes), 7 Workflow step 1.
- 2026-10-18 (answers of the author): MPAI_AIFS_VerifyImplementation: the Controller measures, the Store verifies; the fingerprint is never given out. Software: POST /MPAI/Store/L3/{id}/verify, GET /MPAI/Store/L3/{id}/implementations (names only); IAimSource.Verify; AmdStore.Verifier; the Trust check (ImplementationEvidence) asks the Store; the host measures and the Controller asks. Authentication of the submitter (STR-4): registration (page, code), key bound with the code, MPAI approves, credential issued by the Store (PTF Instance Credential), each L3 signed (MPAI-Signature); MPAI's own ID 1 by admin key. Web: Store page 4 (Verification: the Store verifies what the Controller measured), 5.2.3, 5 (check of an Implementer ID - registration outside AIF), 7 Workflow steps 1, 3, 8. Implementer ID 1 is MPAI.

- 2026-10-20 CAE-USC SOFTWARE, AUDIO RECORDING PRESERVATION started (author: yes to the video stand-in and to 'TAR only applies the correction'; start with Tape Audio Restoration): AIMs/CAE1/V2.5/ARP (Mpai.Cae.Usc.Arp): Tape (speed standards, playback equalisation time constants, AppliedSamplingFrequency, CorrectEqualisation by FFT) and CAE-TAR (TarAimProcessor: for each irregularity whose IrregularityProperties give reading and writing standards that differ, the section its TimeLabel (a Simple Time) covers is corrected - sampling frequency applied = rate x writing speed / reading speed; equalisation = the response it should have had over the one it had, taken at the frequency the sound then has - and put in the Restored Audio Files (BAO, one file per restoration, item ID = RestorationID); the Editing List (CAE-ELS) records Original* and Applied* for each; nothing to correct = no output). SPEC POINTS: (1) the Irregularity File has NO field for reading backwards, while the Editing List has ReadingBackwards: TAR sets it false and reverses nothing; (2) the IEC equalisation time constants in Tape.cs are FROM MEMORY and MUST be checked against IEC 60094; 'IEC' alone is taken as IEC1; (3) the meaning of the irregularity types (sp, b, sot, eot, da, di, m, s, wf, pps, ssv, esv, sb) is not on the page; (4) the Audio Qualifier and the Speech Qualifier name the same things differently (ContentFormat / ContentFormats, TransportFormat.FileFormats / TransportFormats.FileFormat); (5) the AIMs read 16-bit mono WAV only: a Preservation Master File must keep the digitised audio as it is (96 kHz, 24 bit, stereo are usual) - Packaging has to pass the bytes through.

- 2026-10-20 CAE-USC SOFTWARE, ARP: PACKAGING FOR AUDIO PRESERVATION (CAE-PAP, PapAimProcessor, ARP project): THE MASTER (output port 2, an OSD-BAO whose data items are FILES named by their ID) = the bytes of the Preservation Audio File untouched (any format, 24 bit / 96 kHz / stereo included: nothing is decoded), the Preservation Audio-Visual File bytes, irregularities.json, irregularity-image-N.<ext>, editing-list.json and manifest.json (name, bytes, SHA-256 of every file); nothing restored. THE ACCESS COPY (port 1) = the Preservation Audio File with each restored section (Editing List times hh:mm:ss.mmm, restored files by RestorationID) put in the place of the section it restores, the section brought to the file's sampling frequency (so it lasts as long as it should); nothing restored = a copy of the original bytes. Common: AudioObject.Files / FileFormatOf / MakeFiles. Tests CaeUscV25PapTests (4). SPEC POINTS: (1) the Preservation Master Files are typed OSD-BAO-V1.5 in the L2 but are a set of FILES of several kinds (film, images, JSON): carried as items with ID names, the Qualifier naming no format; (2) the manifest of hashes and the inclusion of the Editing List and the Irregularity File in the Master are MY additions (long-term preservation practice), not in the page; (3) 'Access Copy Files' is a set: this AIM gives one file. NOT YET: AAP, VAP (stand-in), TIC, L3s, provider, the ARP Module test.

- 2026-10-20 CAE-USC SOFTWARE, ARP: AUDIO ANALYSIS FOR PRESERVATION (CAE-AAP, AapAimProcessor + AudioAnalysis + Irregularities, ARP project; Common: AudioVisualObject): (1) OFFSET between the Preservation Audio File and the audio of the Audio-Visual File by cross-correlation (coarse at ~4 kHz over the first 2 minutes, fine at the full rate), in SAMPLES of the Preservation Audio File, tolerance 10 s (the page); (2) IRREGULARITIES of the audio: dropout (frames under 5 % of the median of their 2 s), click (a sample difference far above its neighbourhood's), step in level (more than 6 dB between the second before and the second after); close ones are one; each with a uuid unique on the tape, Source a, and its Audio File (the event with 1 s of margin on each side, item ID = IrregularityID, AudioFileURI urn:mpai:cae:aap:audio:<id>); (3) the loop with the Video Analysis: with the Preservation Audio File it gives the Irregularity File (Source a, with the Offset); when the Video Analysis's Irregularity File comes (times of the film) it puts them in the time of the recording (+ offset), merges (overlap within 0.5 s = ONE irregularity, Source b, keeping the audio's identifier) and gives the merged file and the audio files of all. THE VIDEO STAND-IN (author's yes): the Audio-Visual Object (OSD-BMO) carries a data item ID 'audio' (WAV) and one ID 'video-frames' (FRM8: width, height, fps, count, then grayscale frames). Tests CaeUscV25AapTests (6). SPEC POINTS: (a) the Offset of the Irregularity File is an integer >= 0 with NO unit and no sign: samples of the Preservation Audio File here; an Audio-Visual File that starts before the Preservation Audio File cannot be said (the AIM refuses); (b) IrregularityType is NOT set by the audio analysis: the meaning of the type codes is not on the page, Tape Irregularity Classification is the one that classifies; (c) the loop makes the AIM give its Irregularity File twice (audio only, then merged): the Topology sends it to Video Analysis and to Tape Irregularity Classification alike. NOT YET: VAP (stand-in), TIC, L3s, provider, the ARP Module test.

- 2026-10-20 CAE-USC SOFTWARE, ARP: VIDEO ANALYSIS FOR PRESERVATION (CAE-VAP, VapAimProcessor + VideoAnalysis, ARP project; Common: Png, VisualObject): on the video STAND-IN (frames of the Audio-Visual Object) it detects a change of brightness between two frames (>= 25 grey levels) or an abrupt change of the frame (mean difference >= 40); changes within 1.5 s are one irregularity (a splice changes the brightness twice); gives the Video Irregularity File (Source v, times of the FILM, uuid, ImageURI urn:mpai:cae:vap:image:<id>) and the Irregularity Images (an OSD-BVO whose items are PNG files of the frame, ID = IrregularityID, Visual Qualifier Formats.Content.2D.Static PNG). When the Audio Analysis's Irregularity File comes (times of the RECORDING, with the Offset) it gives the image of the film at (time - offset / sampling frequency of the film's audio) for each irregularity it has no image of; none where the film does not cover it. PNG is written and read with the framework only. Tests CaeUscV25VapTests (5). SPEC POINTS: (a) the Offset has no unit: the Video Analysis takes it at the sampling frequency of the film's audio, which has to be that of the recording; (b) the Video Irregularity File goes to the Audio Analysis AND to Tape Irregularity Classification (one port): it holds only the film's own irregularities, the merged file is the Audio Analysis's - the page says the Video Analysis 'sends the merged Irregularity to TIC'; (c) the three Qualifiers name formats three ways (Speech: Formats.TransportFormats.FileFormat; Audio: Formats.TransportFormat.FileFormats; Visual: Formats.Transport.FileFormat and Formats.Content.2D.Static): they should agree. NOT YET: TIC, L3s, provider, the ARP Module test.

- 2026-10-20 CAE-USC SOFTWARE, ARP, CORRECTIONS from the web spec (the Irregularity File page 44860: author 'always look at the web spec'): (1) the Offset is in MILLISECONDS (not samples as my AAP/VAP notes above said): AAP and VAP now give and read ms; the reference of every Time Label, the video ones included, is the Preservation Audio File: the Video Analysis puts its irregularities in the time of the recording itself (it gets the Offset from the Audio Analysis); (2) both the Audio Analysis and the Video Analysis give the MERGED file (the VAP page says it sends the merged Irregularity to TIC), merged by IrregularityFile.Union: same identifier = same irregularity, different identifiers that overlap within 0.5 s with different sources = one, b, keeping the audio's identifier; both give a file again only if it differs from the last (Signature), so the loop ends; (3) the type codes (page Tables 1 and 2): sp splice, b brands, sot start of tape, eot end of tape, da damaged tape, di dirt, m marks, s shadows, wf wow and flutter (the film or the audio), pps play/pause/stop, ssv speed standard variation, esv equalisation standard variation, sb signal backward (audio only). TAPE IRREGULARITY CLASSIFICATION (CAE-TIC, TicAimProcessor): classifies and selects: a type an irregularity has is kept with its properties; seen by the film: sot within 2 s of the film's start (the Offset says where), eot within 2 s of its end (the film's length is a parameter), else sp; heard only: by its Audio File a dropout >= 1 s is pps, a shorter dropout or a click is da; a step in level is NOT classified (it cannot say ssv/esv/sb) and so NOT SELECTED (not given). RULES ARE A STAND-IN for a classifier. The Irregularity record gained Type and Properties (IrregularityProperties kept through the chain, so an expert's ssv/esv with the speed and equalisation standards reaches Tape Audio Restoration: TIC -> TAR test). Tests: CaeUscV25TicTests (6, including AAP+VAP+TIC and Packaging together without a Controller), AAP (7), VAP (6); with TAR (7) and PAP (4) 30 ARP tests pass. SPEC POINTS: (a) nothing in the analysis can SET ssv, esv, sb, wf, di, m, s, b - the codes that need a real classifier or an operator; without them Tape Audio Restoration restores nothing in a Module run; (b) the AAP page says the audio analysis 'sends the merged Irregularity' while the Topology gives one output port with two destinations; (c) the TIC page's inputs are described with the names 'Audio Irregularity File' / 'Video Irregularity File' as files of 'Audio Analysis for Recording' - the page text (not the schemas) still has 'Recording' for 'Preservation' in places. NOT YET: L3s and provider entries for TAR, PAP, AAP, VAP, TIC and the ARP composite, the Module test.

- 2026-10-20 CAE-USC SOFTWARE, EAE (Enhanced Audioconference Experience; project AIMs/CAE1/V2.5/EAE; Exchange; 6 SubAIMs): AUDIO ANALYSIS TRANSFORM (AatAimProcessor: STFT, sine window, 50 % overlap, block size of the Microphone Array Geometry), AUDIO SYNTHESIS TRANSFORM (AstAimProcessor: one mono file "source-n" per channel), SOUND FIELD DESCRIPTION (SfdAimProcessor: spherical harmonics, real ACN, order from the number of microphones (N+1)^2 <= M, open-sphere radial functions 4 pi i^n j_n(kr), regularised least squares per bin), SPEECH DETECTION AND SEPARATION (SdsAimProcessor), NOISE CANCELLATION MODULE (NcmAimProcessor), AUDIO DESCRIPTORS MULTIPLEXING (AmxAimProcessor). New data forms in Common: TransformObject (blocks as float32 data items "block-k"; Audio Qualifier TransformSpace or SpherlHarmDecompos), Multichannel (MultiAudio, ArrayGeometry = CAE-MAG), SceneDescriptors (OSD-BAS: members with Space-Time (SpherPosition [1, azimuth, elevation]) and Simple Time segments of activity). L3s 1CAE-{AAT,SFD,SDS,NCM,AST,AMX,EAE}-V2.5-I01 (EAE Exchange, the topology of the L2); UscProvider builds the six. Tests: Transform (5), Sfd (4), Sds (4), NcmAmx (5), Module (1, AimHost apart; two sources + noise): 256 tests (CaeUsc, Metadata, Schema, Aih) pass. Expected files regenerated.
  SDS: finds the SOURCES (the page says speech OR non-speech: it does not tell them apart) from the direction of the intensity at each time-frequency point (a plane wave gives order-one coefficients sqrt(3) u times the order-zero one), histogram 5 degrees, greedy clusters; separation by beams that give a source itself and the others reduced (inverse of the harmonics at the directions); activity per block (within 20 dB of the loudest and over the noise), silence otherwise. A silent room is an ERROR ('no sound source').
  NCM: noise MEASURED in the blocks where no source is active (SDS leaves them silent), carried through the beam of each source (weights recomputed from the directions of the Geometry), power spectral subtraction (over 1.5, floor 0.05); no silent block = passthrough (the page allows it). REVERBERATION is NOT cancelled (the page says it should).
  AMX: each member of the Geometry gets its Audio Object (a BasicAudioObject) in place of its ID; no audio = ID kept; audio with no member = a member with no Space-Time; the Space-Time input (optional) becomes BASSpaceTime.
  SPEC POINTS: (1) the polynomial of the Audio Scene Reference (AngleSine/AngleCosine) has no stated meaning (the model OF what? of the source's directivity? of its level against angle?): read and passed on by SDS, NOT used; SDS can give it on (optional output) but the Topology routes it nowhere; (2) the Audio Scene Geometry is typed OSD-BAS (Basic Audio Scene Descriptors) in the L2s but the AMX page says it validates against 'Audio Basic Scene Geometry' (BasicAudioSceneGeometry does not exist; OSD-ASG-V1.5 AudioSceneGeometry does): the L2s say BAS and the Implementation follows them; (3) a BAS requires UserPoV: the centre of the array, no orientation; the distance of a source cannot be known from the sound field, so the Position is (1, azimuth, elevation) with the Position page not saying which of phi, theta is which; (4) the TWO Audio Objects of NCM share one Data Type (BAO#1 SHD, BAO#2 transform) and the Qualifier is what tells them apart: SpherlHarmDecompos against TransformSpace; (5) the radial functions of a rigid sphere (Eigenmike) differ from the open sphere used: the Microphone Array Geometry has no 'sphere type' field; the decomposition holds only while the wave number times the radius is below about one (spatial aliasing with few microphones: 6 microphones at 4.2 cm are wrong at 2 kHz, so the tests use 1.2 cm); (6) the CAE-EAE L2 AudioSceneReference input is required but nothing uses it.

- 2026-10-20 CAE-USC SOFTWARE, THE REAL SPEECH MODEL (project AIMs/CAE1/V2.5/Voice; setting "SpeechModel": "openvoice" for SMC and TTS, default "stand-in"): the Neural Network Speech Model = the TONE-COLOUR EMBEDDING (256 numbers) of OpenVoice V2 (MIT; source and converter checkpoint in D:\AI\openvoice, downloaded with the author's yes; runs in D:\AI\pyannote-venv, CPU torch, no new packages: openvoice_worker.py replaces librosa/wavmark by a stub/scipy). SMC (OpenVoiceSpeechTrainer) extracts it from the Speech Segments (mean); the TTS (VoiceCloningTtsAimProcessor) lets Piper say the texts and gives each piece the tone colour of the model, 16 kHz. A model that is not 256 numbers (the stand-in's) is REFUSED. Test CaeUscV25VoiceTests (Slow, 2): speech 'said' by speaker 1462 comes out nearer the embedding of speaker 1272 than the original was. LIMITS: it changes the TIMBRE only (prosody and language are Piper's); the model is loaded at each call (~8 s on a CPU); the embedding says nothing of the quality of the voice with few seconds of speech (SMC still reports under 1 s as reduced quality). SPEC POINT: the 'training' of the Basic API is here an extraction, no weights are fitted (the AIP Model_Update/Drift calls have nothing to update).

STANDARD/SOFTWARE CHANGE (inform): Rca/DeviceRegistry gained RegisterAcquireLabelled (a source for the datum a workflow calls by LABEL, so two OSD-BSO of one Module come from different places) and AcquireAsync(... label), and the interpreter passes the label; compatible with the existing clients. Tested by running each App on real data (Test/SrsApp, EesApp, ArpApp, EaeApp): SRS with the OpenVoice voice (5.93 s = 5.0 - 1.4 + 2.3), EES both pathways, ARP (dropout, click, master = tape byte for byte), EAE demo (both speakers found within 1 degree and their times). FOUND on the way: the Text To Speech of MPAI-MMC speaks the WHOLE Text Object as ONE utterance and in its own internal Speech Object form: SRS needs a piece for each text in the schema form (TextByTextTtsAimProcessor, Voice project, in the provider for Tts=piper). SPEC POINTS: (1) exchange Modules need the offers all made before the Output is asked for - a workflow that STREAMS to devices (as for continuous Modules) closed the exchange early and the Module degraded; (2) a failure of an AIM stops the Module (OnDegraded StopModule) and the stream-based session then hung instead of failing. - 2026-10-20 CAE-USC EAE SERVER (Apps/CAE/EAE/Server, EaeServer; user's idea: EAE architecture is like the avatar videoconference, there was no server, derive one from AVS taking away what is not needed): ASP.NET/Kestrel (HTTP 5190, HTTPS 5191, own certificate, EAE_LAN/EAE_CODE as ABV_LAN/ABV_CODE), WebSocket /ws, join as the ROOM (captures the microphone array: multichannel getUserMedia -> 16 kHz interleaved) or LISTENER; the room's audio in segments of 3 s through the EAE Module (the same ModuleHost + WorkflowRun as the EAE App; busy = segment dropped); listeners get the scene (speakers, azimuth/elevation, speaking) and the separated, de-noised audio of each speaker (0x02 frames), played through HRTF panners in their directions; top-view map. Removed from AVS: avatars/three.js, chat, minutes/Whisper, PDX/PMX/VCR. EAE_DEMO=1 = a virtual room (no array needed). Tried: 3 s of room in ~280 ms; browser client joined, speakers A (60,20) and B (-90,0) found and placed, AudioContext running. NOT TRIED: a real multichannel microphone (needs an array and a browser that gives its channels), several listeners at once, the LAN/tunnel modes, listening quality by ear. LIMITS: segments are independent (the identity of a speaker from one to the next is by direction within 25 degrees; the noise of a segment is measured only where its own blocks are silent; the join of two segments has no crossfade); no avatars (the AVS client's scene is not here).

- 2026-10-20 CAE-USC APP TESTS (CaeUscV25AppTests, Slow, 8: the four Apps run as programs with real files; SRS stand-in, SRS errors, SRS real voice (skipped where the engine is absent), EES both pathways, ARP, ARP refusals, EAE demo room, EAE refusals) all pass. FOUND by running ARP on a realistic tape: the audio analysis's step-in-level detector fired at the EDGES of a dropout and the merge kept the first kind, so the dropout was absorbed as a "step" (and, not classifiable, not selected): AudioAnalysis.Detect now drops the steps within 1 s of a dropout or click. Test CaeUscV25AapTests.ADropoutInATapeWithATonalSignalIsADropoutAndNotAStep. Also: EaeApp now refuses a capture whose channels differ from the array's microphones up front. OPEN (asked of the author): the labelled-acquire hook in Rca/DeviceRegistry exists only because a workflow that streams to devices closes an Exchange Module's exchange too early; the better fix is for the interpreter to hold an Exchange Module until all its required inputs are present, then the hook goes.

- 2026-10-20 EAE REAL-SPEECH TEST (simulated six-microphone array, REAL recordings): Room.Speech (Support) places speech recordings as plane waves (fractional delay by windowed sinc) with noise; EaeApp --demo <folder> --speech a.wav b.wav [--noise N] [--radius R]; server EAE_DEMO=1 EAE_SPEECH=a.wav;b.wav (Run-Eae-RealSpeech.bat). Result with LibriSpeech 1272 (60 deg, +20) and 1462 (-90, 0), noise 150, radius 1.2 cm: both directions found EXACTLY, each speaker in its own channel (in the 2.8 s they speak together: A channel has A 0.70 and B 0.014 = 33 dB, B channel has B 0.855 and A 0.041 = 27 dB; residual 38 % and 11 % of the output = noise and what the order one cannot resolve); radius 4.2 cm is worse (aliasing above 1.3 kHz). FOUND on the way: (1) a sign error of mine in the demo room's delay (everything came out at the opposite direction: found by the real-speech test); (2) a QUIET room made SDS invent sources from noise (a cluster of a few loud points of noise): a source must now be 15 % of the energy of the band; (3) a quiet room was an ERROR of SDS and, through OnDegraded StopModule, stopped the Module: SDS and NCM now give NOTHING when there is no source (not a fault); the workflow then returns no scene; (4) the conference server now runs ONE workflow (EAE-Conference.orch: start once, loop offer/ask/present, QueueDevice) instead of starting the Module for each segment. - 2026-10-20 AIF V3.0 ACCESS, THREE OPEN POINTS FOR THE AIF TEXT (author, no change made to software or schemas): the standard (mpai.community/standards/mpai-aif/v3-0/storage/) lists for Access: AIM reads (MPAI_AIFM_Access_Get, _List, _Version), Provider writes (MPAI_AIFP_Access_Create, _Put, _Delete, versioned), User writes through the User Agent (MPAI_AIFU_Access_Create, _Put, _Delete). A Module is a container of AIMs and cannot read or write: the "Module" API is what an AIM asks of its Controller. (1) AIM to Controller: there must be requests by an AIM to WRITE Access (create, put, delete), not only to read it; the Controller stamps the writer's identity. (2) Controller API for the User Agent: it must READ Access as well (today only MPAI_AIFU_Access_Create/_Put/_Delete). (3) Authorised third parties must be able to write Access from outside the AIF, or necessarily ask the User Agent, or the User Agent asks them: to be settled (the Provider API covers a third party holding rights to the data; whether it covers every outside writer is open). Software today follows the text: AIFM read, AIFP write (AccessProvider.cs), AIFU write (UserAgent.cs).

- 2026-10-20 (night) MPAI STORE: (6) the answer to MPAI_AIFS_VerifyImplementation is SIGNED (PtfSignature, key mpai-store-1) over {AIMName, BinaryName, Fingerprint, Nonce, Result, Time}; HttpAimSource sends a Nonce and treats an answer with a bad or missing signature, or for another question, as NotRecorded; the field is now "Result" (was "result"). (7) the licence service (POST /MPAI/Store/Licence) authenticates its caller: the request carries Caller {Cii, Credential, Chain}, Time and is signed with the key of that identity; the Store checks signature, Time (5 minutes), credential chain to the Trust Anchor asked for, and that the Trust Anchor is one the Store recognises (new route POST /MPAI/Store/TrustAnchor, admin key; trust-anchors.json); a refusal is 401 with the reason. HttpAimSource takes an AimCaller. (8) the price stays a placeholder (author). Tests: StoreLicenceServiceTests (+2). Store tests start the Store with 'dotnet StoreService.dll' (the apphost exe was being blocked from being created). Software only; the AIF text says nothing of either.

- 2026-10-21 MMM WORLD (author: the MMM as a virtual space that can be seen, with buildings; a Persona walks into the meeting room through a door and joins an ABV-like meeting; buildings are glTF; Rocketbox avatars; a conference of 5 people tomorrow 2026-10-22 at 17:00): software only, no schema change. MMM/World/make_world.py writes MMM/Viewer/world/{townhall,library,cafe}.glb (real glTF with walls, door and window openings, floor, ceiling, table, chairs) and world.json; MMM/Client/World.cs makes the Locations (Square MVS = 2D Location; buildings = 3D Locations owned by the AVS Service, not public; rooms = Locations in them; the room is a minimal OSD-BLC Location Object whose LocationQualifier Attributes carry a CAE3 Acoustic Profile, Scene) and the seats (8). A DOOR IS A RIGHT: the AVS Service gives a User it invites, when it asks to join, a seat, the right to speak and the right to MM-Move to the building and the room (a PA with no Nil: any Persona of the User); others get 403. THE RIGHTS OF THE MEETING ARE FOR THE MEETING (author): they carry RightsTime (a Simple Time from the start to the end of the meeting, --until HH:mm or --hours N); the M-Instance holds no Right after its end (MInstance.Live/Expired: a RightsTime with End > Start; a same-instant time does not expire) and the AVS Service withdraws them (May Not) when the meeting ends: at its time, or when the convener sends 'end' (button in the viewer, two clicks); then capture of the room is refused (403), the clients send their Personae to the Square, and a join is answered 'the meeting is over'. MayRead no longer counts a May Not or an expired Right as a right on an Item (it did: the revoked door right still let a User read the room). Server: --world, --invite A,B,C (the first is the convener), --models Name=File.glb, --code (meeting code: cookie; the internal services use a private header), --until/--hours; the avatars are those of Apps/ABV/AVS/Server/avatars (served as /viewer/personae/ without a second copy). Viewer: ?world=1&user=Name&mic=1 (&mic=tone: a test tone): the glTF buildings, walls that stop a walker, doors that open when walked at (MM-Move; 403 shows 'locked'), the Persona sits when it enters the meeting room (Q stands, E sits), reverberation of the room from its RT60 plot (convolution). MMM/Run-Conference.bat/.ps1 and AllApps\42: server + Cloudflare quick tunnel + code. Tests: MmmWorldTests (3): file/Location agreement, doors passable, glTF valid; door right, end of meeting, 403s; rights end with the period; the five Mmm* test classes share a collection because Meeting has static state (Meeting.Reset). NOT TRIED: five people with real microphones over the internet; other people's browsers. FOR THE STANDARD (to tell the author): RightsTime as the validity period is my reading; Acoustic Profile carried in LocationQualifier.Attributes; a building's glTF is a file named by the Location, not a 3D Model Object Item; the Location Object of the room is not validated against OSD-BLC.

- 2026-10-21 REMOVED (software, not the standard): MAD, MAT, MPD - their L2s (MultimodalAnonymousDialogue/Translation, MultimodalPersonalStatusDialogue: to be removed from the schemas site too: MMC-MAD-V2.5, MMC-MAT-V2.5, MMC-MPD-V2.5 and what links to them on the web; their pages, if any), L3 AMDs, providers, apps and tests were temporary developments with no status. MMC-MCA/MTS/AMC are the standard's names (L2s only for now). The example AIM name in AIF/V3.0 AIMInstance.json and AIMMetadata.json descriptions changed from MMC-MAD-V2.5 to MMC-AMQ-V2.5 (to upload).
- 2026-10-21 MMM world (implementation, not the standard): the MPAI building (a public atrium with the counter of the reception, three wings: members' rooms, offices of MPAI, meeting rooms of the groups), world.json/mpai.glb regenerated; Persona catalogue of 113 Rocketbox avatars (MMM/Viewer/personae/catalogue, not in git); identity by Tailscale (no meeting code). NOT IN MMM-TEC V2.2 (to tell the author): in the Personal Profile of Register, a Persona is chosen from the M-Instance's catalogue (`PersonaChoice`) instead of being brought; the rights of a meeting last for its period (RightsTime) and the PARTICIPANTS - not the M-Instance - hold no right after it.
- 2026-10-21 REMOVED from the schemas (a second copy of entities of another standard: an AIM or Data Type is identified once, by its standard and its three letters): schemas/AIP/V1.0/AIMs/{DuringTrainingWatermarkInsertion,PostTrainingWatermarkInsertion,WatermarkExtraction}.json (AIMNames NNW-DWI-V1.2, NNW-PWI-V1.2, NNW-WEX-V1.2: they are NNW1/V1.2/AIMs). They were uploaded in the 2026-10-18 batch (deploy/schemas-publish-2026-10-18/1-new-files/AIP/V1.0/AIMs): REMOVE THEM FROM THE SCHEMAS SITE (schemas.mpai.community/AIP/V1.0/AIMs/) and from the MKG.
- 2026-10-21 MMM (implementation): screen sharing (the convener's only): a Visual Object (OSD-BVO) that is a stream placed in the meeting room by MM-Add, with its own Right (MMAdd at the room), frames as JPEG over a WebSocket; not in MMM-TEC V2.2. The meeting rooms of the building are one for each group of the list of groups and participants (AIF, AIH, AIP, CAE, EVC, ISAC, MMC, MMM, NNW, PAF, XRV): MMM/Registry/groups.json and people.json made from 'list of groups and participants.docx' (ISAC lists one name for two emails: Gérard added from his email).
- 2026-10-21 MMM Register (implementation): the registration in the viewer follows the Register page of MMM-TEC V2.2: Registration options (Account Levels Visitor and Participant, both free: no Service Pricing Model), the human's Personal Data (FirstName, LastName, Email), the Persona chosen among the Items the Account makes available; the Account carries AccountLevel and Items; a Visitor Account has no seat in a meeting. `AccountLevel` in the Personal Profile and the options endpoint (/api/v2.2/register/options) are not in MMM-TEC V2.2.
- 2026-10-21 MMM (implementation): the Register Account Levels are Visitor, Participant and MPAI Officer (the Rights to every place of the building; offered to the host and to those designated); host (whoever runs the meeting on the machine, the President) and chair (who convenes a group's meeting: (C) in the list of groups and participants) are separate; the viewer walks to a clicked point and zooms with the wheel; the building has regular windows and a sign with the MPAI logo. None of it is in MMM-TEC V2.2.

- (2026-10-25, later) THE HASHES OF THE IMPLEMENTER-TO-CONTROLLER WORKFLOW (software, no standard text changed): the Store, when an L3 is submitted, fetches the package its ImplementationURI names (a .zip in its packages folder or at an http(s) address; a folder as before), measures BinaryName.dll in it and records the SHA-256 as approved (Composite AIMs: nothing). The package provider of the Controller (PackageAimProvider) measures the same binary after fetching and asks the Store (MPAI_AIFS_VerifyImplementation, HttpAimSource.Verify); only Same loads the package; Different / not recorded / Store not reachable refuses the AIM, which no other provider then builds. The Service: StoreUrl + L3Source Store + AimSource Packages + VerifyPackages (default true) -> verified; without a StoreUrl it prints "Packages: NOT verified". Only the BinaryName binary is measured, not the other files of the package. Test: PackageFingerprintTests (altered package refused). Item 3c/6 of the email to Ryan reworded to say this.

## 2026-10-26 CAE-ASM as the author's slide of 2026-10-26 (branch asm-nonbasic, worktree D:\AI-asm; the UCM change is in access-environment, fc30d97b)

- CAE-ASM-V1.0 and its editors CAE-AOE, CAE-ASE, CAE-SOE, CAE-SSE: no Basic Data Type: Audio Object OSD-AUO, Audio Scene Descriptors OSD-ASD, Speech Object OSD-SPO, Speech Scene Descriptors OSD-SSD, Multimodal Scene Descriptors OSD-MSD. 14 input and 10 output Ports; AudioObjectFromOutside and MadeAudioObject removed. Port names *UserCommandRQ / *UserCommandRS.
- OSD-BME (Basic Multimodal Scene Editing) becomes OSD-MSE (Multimodal Scene Editing): inputs non-Basic Scenes and Objects; in CAE-ASM it receives Audio Scene and Speech Scene only (no Audio Object, no Speech Object); output OSD-MSD. L3 1OSD-MSE-V1.5-I01.
- OSD: Audio-Visual becomes Multimodal: OSD-MSD Multimodal Scene Descriptors, OSD-BMS Basic Multimodal Scene Descriptors, the AIM Basic Multimodal Scene Description; fields SubAudioVisualScene* -> SubMultimodalScene*, BasicAVSceneDescriptors* -> BasicMultimodalSceneDescriptors*, BAVSDescriptors* -> BMSDescriptors*, AVObjectCount -> MultimodalObjectCount.
- MMC-TST-V2.5 unchanged (it takes and gives OSD-BSO and OSD-SPO).
- Rebuild the MKG for: CAE-ASM (Ports, Topology), OSD-MSE, OSD-MSD, OSD-BMS (names), CAE-UCM.

## 2026-10-26 Names, amounts and List in the User Command (CAE-ASM; branch access-environment)
- CAE-UCM-V1.0 User Command (STANDARD CHANGE, schema to upload: schemas/CAE3/V1.0/data/UserCommand.json): (1) THE OBJECTS AND SCENES ARE IDENTIFIED ONLY BY THE NAME THE USER GAVE THEM ("violin"): ObjectID, BasicAudioObjectID, AudioObjectID, AudioSceneDescriptorsID, BasicSpeechObjectID, SpeechObjectID, SpeechSceneDescriptorsID, MultimodalSceneDescriptorsID hold the name, in data, commands, context and replies (UserCommandRS ObjectID and ResultID are names). The storage keys of the Module (BAO000001 ...) never leave it. A second Object or Scene given a name that is taken is "name 2", "name 3". A thing created with no name is not created: the report says a name is needed. (2) THE AMOUNTS ARE REQUIRED NUMBERS: AddedObjects entries require ObjectID and SpatialAttitude; MovedObjects entries require NewSpatialAttitude (and ObjectID or OldSpatialAttitude); ChangedObjects entries require ObjectID and a SpatialAttitude or a Qualifier; ModifiedObjects requires the LUFS number or, in each entry, an Acoustic Profile or a Spatial Attitude; RemovedObjects entries require ObjectID or SpatialAttitude. LUFS has a description (a number). The AIMs reject a command without the number with a report (Failed, with the reason), they do not guess. (3) NEW FIELD AddedObjects.ObjectID (string, minLength 1): the name of the Audio Object, or of the Scene (Audio, Speech, Multimodal), that the command makes; required when the command makes one. (4) NEW OPERATION ListedObjects: RQ {Kind: Audio Object | Basic Audio Object | Speech Object | Basic Speech Object | Audio Scene | Speech Scene | Multimodal Scene | All} (Kind required); RS ListedObjects = array of {ObjectID (the name), Kind, MemberObjectIDs (names)}; Action enum of the RS gains ListedObjects. Each editor lists what it keeps (AOE: Audio Objects and Basic Audio Objects; ASE: Audio Scenes; SOE: Speech Objects; SSE: Speech Scenes; MSE: Multimodal Scenes) and, for a Kind another editor keeps, reports NotSupported saying which; All lists all that editor keeps. No L2/L3 Port changed.
- Software: AIMs/Core/NameBook.cs (the table name <-> key in the Module's Shared Storage, per kind), CommandReader (a command with an amount that is not a number is reported), Amounts; AoeAim, AseAim, SpeechStore and the five processors keep the private key and tell the name. Dead code removed from AoeAim/AseAim (the Audio Object and ASD editing methods nobody called). MSE no longer replaces a member by a "new version": a member is held by name and is as it last arrived. Tests: AsmNamesTests (duplicate -> "violin 2", amounts, List, no name), the ASM tests follow the names; Expected accepted after checking each difference: asm-editing.json, asm-module.json, mse.json, mse-objects.json.
- Web: Data Type CAE-UCM page (Audio Scene Management Data Types): ObjectID and the other identifiers are names; AddedObjects ObjectID; the required amounts; the ListedObjects operation and its response. To rebuild the MKG: CAE-UCM.
- Not changed, to look at: OSD Position (schemas/OSD/V1.5/data/Position.json) requires only Header and PositionID, so a Spatial Attitude with a Position without coordinates is schema-valid; the AIMs report it ("three numbers"). AIMs/MMC (EDP AsmDialogue) and schemas/MMC still build commands without the placement, the name of what is made, or with BAO000001-style identifiers: the author works there.

## 2026-10-26 (later) CAE-AMI, the Audio Scene Management Interface; features of an Object; List attributes and Scene listing (CAE-ASM; branch access-environment)
- NEW AIM CAE-AMI-V1.0 "Audio scene Management Interface" (author's decision; acronym AMI = Audio scene Management Interface). It is a Sub-AIM of the Composite CAE-ASM and the ONLY AIM that touches the boundary of the Composite. Drawn as a vertical AIM at the far left: its right-hand side serves the Editing AIMs exactly as the boundary served them (figure 33 of the Reference Model), its left-hand side is the boundary. L2 schemas/CAE3/V1.0/AIMs/AudioSceneManagementInterface.json (new, valid against the L1; AccessSources: CommandSchema), L3 AIMs/AMDs/1CAE-AMI-V1.0-I01.json (new). Ports, by Data Type and Port Number. LEFT, inputs: CAE-UCM #1-5 (User Command RQ, one per Editing AIM: Audio Object, Audio Scene, Speech Object, Speech Scene, Multimodal Scene Editing), OSD-OPV #1-5 (User PoV), OSD-AUO #1, OSD-ASD #1, OSD-SPO #1, OSD-SSD #1, and NEW optional MMC-AJI-V2.5 #1 (User AJI Input); outputs: CAE-UCM #1-5 (User Command RS), OSD-AUO #1, OSD-ASD #1, OSD-SPO #1, OSD-SSD #1, OSD-MSD #1, and NEW optional MMC-AJI-V2.5 #1 (User AJI Output). RIGHT (to/from the Editing AIMs): outputs CAE-UCM #6-10 (RQ to the five Editing AIMs), OSD-OPV #1-5, OSD-AUO #2, OSD-ASD #2, OSD-SPO #2, OSD-SSD #2; inputs CAE-UCM #6-10 (RS from the five), OSD-AUO #2, OSD-ASD #2, OSD-SPO #2, OSD-SSD #2, OSD-MSD #1. User Command is ONE Data Type (RQ and RS inside).
- AMI behaviour: forwards data and commands exactly as the boundary did; for the AJI input it reads its Access (AccessSources CommandSchema, Setting CommandSchema: Uri https://schemas.mpai.community/CAE3/V1.0/data/UserCommand.json + SHA-256 of the UserCommand.json of the repository, same pattern as MMC-JSI), checks that the ApplicationSchemaURI of the AJI is that schema and that the Instance validates and is a UserCommandRQ, sends it to the Editing AIMs as any command, and wraps the UserCommandRS in an AJI on the User AJI Output (InReplyTo = AJIID of the AJI, same ApplicationSchemaURI, Instance = the RS). A command that does not validate is answered by a Failed UserCommandRS wrapped the same way, not an exception. ListedObjects with Kind All is sent to every Editing AIM and the answers are merged into one RS. NB: the SHA-256 in the L3 pins UserCommand.json as it is today: it MUST be recomputed every time that schema changes (a test, AsmInterfaceTests.TheAccessOfTheInterfaceNamesTheSchemaOfTheUserCommand, fails when it is not). The entry is IsOptional: the schema published on the site is still the old one (other SHA-256), so the Module must start without it; once the new UserCommand.json is uploaded the entry can be made required.
- CAE-ASM-V1.0 (L2 schemas/CAE3/V1.0/AIMs/AudioSceneManager.json, L3 1CAE-ASM-V1.0-I01): TWO PORTS ADDED to the Composite: input MMC-AJI-V2.5 #1 (User AJI Input) and output MMC-AJI-V2.5 #1 (User AJI Output), both optional; every other boundary Data Type and Port Number unchanged. SubAIMs: CAE-AMI-V1.0 added. Topology rewired: every connection that started or ended at the boundary now goes boundary <-> CAE-AMI and CAE-AMI <-> the Editing AIM with the same Data Types (UCM and OPV numbered as the boundary: AMI #k to/from the editor k; the responses UserCommandRS of the editors go to CAE-AMI, which gives them to the boundary); each input of an Editing AIM has exactly one source. Descriptions of the L2 and the L3 mention CAE-AMI. The L2/L3 descriptions of CAE-AOE tell the features, the list attributes.
- CAE-UCM-V1.0 (STANDARD CHANGE, schemas/CAE3/V1.0/data/UserCommand.json, to upload): (1) ChangedObjects is a DEFINED LIST OF FEATURES, each changed by a value; the schema lists them by name (additionalProperties false): Name, Time, SpaceTime, UserPoV, ParentAudioObjectIDs, DescrMetadata, Children (array of {ObjectID, SpaceTime}: the SpaceTime of each child, by its name, inside the parent), DeviceID, Level (number, LUFS), PerceptStatus (boolean), AcousticProfile, AudioQualifier; SpatialAttitude (place of an Object in a Scene: Scene Editing) and Qualifier (Speech Qualifier: Speech Object Editing) remain; at least one feature is required. Audio Object: Name, Time (AudioObjectTime), SpaceTime (AudioObjectSpaceTime), UserPoV, ParentAudioObjectIDs, DescrMetadata, Children. Basic Audio Object: Name, Time, UserPoV, DeviceID, Level, PerceptStatus, AcousticProfile, AudioQualifier, DescrMetadata. The sound is NOT editable. Name renames (AudioObjectID / BasicAudioObjectID, same Object, the name stays unique: "x 2"); every edit is a new version. (2) ListedObjects (RQ) gains the optional ObjectID = the name of the one Scene (or Object) to list, of the Kind given (not All). (3) ListedObjects (RS, each entry): for an Object, Duration (seconds), ChildCount, Version; for a Scene named in the request, ObjectCount (number of members) and Members [{ObjectID, PoV}], PoV = position and orientation of the member as provided in the Scene (a Spatial Attitude); never a key. PoV in the descriptions means "position and orientation as provided in the Scene". Software: AIMs/Core/UserCommand.cs (ObjectChange features, Unknown, ChildSpaceTime, ObjectListing.ObjectID), NameBook.cs (ListedObject, ListedMember, Link/Depth/Release), SoundLength.cs (new), AoeAim.ChangeFeatures, the five processors' List. Web: Data Type CAE-UCM page (ChangedObjects features, ListedObjects ObjectID and the response); AIM CAE-AOE (ChangedObjects); new AIM page CAE-AMI (Audio Scene Management Interface); the Composite page CAE-ASM (figure with CAE-AMI at the far left, the two AJI ports). To rebuild the MKG: CAE-AMI, CAE-ASM, CAE-UCM, CAE-AOE.
- OSD-OPS-V1.5 Position (STANDARD CHANGE, schemas/OSD/V1.5/data/Position.json, to upload): the Position must state its coordinates - anyOf CartPosition, SpherPosition (each a triple of numbers); before it required only Header and PositionID. The AIM code already rejected fewer than three numbers. NB: the author's wording was "at least two of x, y, z (anyOf of the three pairs)", but Position.json has no x, y, z properties (CartPosition and SpherPosition are arrays of three); the rule is written on the properties that exist.
- Schemas upload list (2026-10-26, late): CAE3/V1.0/data/UserCommand.json (changed), CAE3/V1.0/AIMs/AudioSceneManager.json (changed), CAE3/V1.0/AIMs/AudioObjectEditing.json (changed: description), CAE3/V1.0/AIMs/AudioSceneManagementInterface.json (NEW), OSD/V1.5/data/Position.json (changed). Tests: AsmInterfaceTests, AsmFeaturesTests (new); Expected: conformance, connections, l3-load, schema.
