## 2026-10-26 (evening) - Workflow Description Language: a header clause names the application the Module drives (additive); Controller API: ConnectApplication; JSI state; ASM guide
- Workflow Description Language (docs/MAS-App-Developer.md section 4, "A Module that drives an application"): after `over <Module>` the header may say `application <Module>[, <Module>...]`. Reason: the Voice Command Interface drives the application; the Controller connects them by Data Type and Port Number. The driving Module is the authority (as the Avatar Videoconference Server is for the parties of a meeting): the application does what the person, through it, tells it to do. The steps of the workflow name no Module; a Port that the driving Module has not is the application's, reached by its Data Type and Port Number. A workflow without the clause is unchanged (every workflow of the repository is read in a test).
- Controller API (AIF ControllerApi): one request, ConnectApplication(driving, application): the driving Module's MMC-AJI output goes to the application's MMC-AJI input, the application's MMC-AJI output (#1) to the driving Module's MMC-AJI input #1 (Application Response); the application's MMC-AJI Ports are refused to any other caller (NOT_AUTHORISED). Not offered over MPAI-MAS. Inform the author: this is a Controller API addition.
- MMC-JSI (AIM behaviour, no schema change): (a) an ApplicationContext (AJI #2) that is not a listing is ignored and does not wipe the state; (b) listings that come one after another add up (sounds kept from one, a scene with where its members are from another); (c) an ApplicationResponse to a command this interface did not send is not told, and if it lists it is the state; (d) the guide's Fill may be given by the PATH of a property (UserCommandRQ.AddedObjects.ObjectID = "stage", the name of the one scene the application makes); (e) Shortcuts: phrases that are always the same command (what is in the scene, which sounds) are sent without the language model; (f) with the setting RefreshState true the interface asks the application what it holds (guide StateRequests) before it works out a request, and after each response it tells.
- ASM guide (Apps/CAE/ASM/Access/AsmApplicationGuide.json) changed: Fill by path, Shortcuts, StateRequests, Context.MemberNames, a refusal for "load a sound" (the User Agent loads sounds). The Sha256 of the guide in 1MMC-JSI-V2.5-I02 (and THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md) is updated. Schemas: none changed.

## 2026-10-27 - Access: only the Controller writes (software follows the AIF web text)
- Removed the Provider push dialogue (AccessProviderServer/AccessProvider, MPAI_AIFP_*, UserAgent.OpenAccessToProviders, access-provider.json): the AIF pages (Storage 6.1-6.6, Basic API without section 6, Definitions, Scope) now say only the Controller writes Access.
- AccessStore: Controller is the only Writer (no writer/version arguments; the Version rises by one per write); an item records the SHA-256 of what it held before and data with one of those is refused (a Source is never brought back); Remove(source) and Sources(); the Trace says where an item was obtained (From).
- AccessFetcher: Trust (TrustProtocol): an https URI is fetched over a link the Trust Protocol admitted (ProviderLink: TrustRequest POST to /.well-known/mpai-trust on a TLS link, the Provider answers with a TrustResponse naming the Controller's link certificate; later requests only on connections presenting that same certificate); OpenHosts: hosts whose data is published openly, obtained by SHA-256 alone; without Trust every URI is fetched as before. Refresh(entries) (also at each Module start: Ensure) keeps a new SHA-256 as a new Version; Prune(declared) removes Sources no Module declares (access-removed.log); UserAgent.RefreshAccess(module), PruneAccess(), AccessTrust, OpenAccessHosts. Not Controller API functions.
- Tests: AccessProcessTests (5); AifAccessTests/AccessSourcesTests follow; Expected access-reads.json re-recorded, access-provider.json removed.
- The AIF text might add: the HTTP binding of the Trust Protocol for a Provider (TrustPath), data openly published obtained by its SHA-256 alone, a Source never brought back to earlier data.

## 2026-10-28 - MMC-APC-V2.5 Application Context: Asks by pattern, Memory; JSI behaviour; ASM window (mouse, listener)
- schemas/MMC/V2.5/data/ApplicationContext.json (UPLOAD, changed): (a) an Ask may carry a `Pattern` (a regular expression the request matches) and an optional `Unless` instead of `Words` - for a request that says only part of what is needed (two coordinates and not the third, a time start without its end); `Say` stays required. Reason: such a request cannot be told by words. (b) new `Memory`: the numbers the Keep of the Rules holds, with the value each has before any rule has kept it (the listener at 0, 0 facing 0). Reason: "turn me by 30 degrees" works on the facing the listener has, which the first request has not set.
- MMC-JSI (AIM behaviour, no other schema change): the grammar tolerates a recogniser's writing (full stops in a sentence, "x = 2", "for metres" for "four metres", "4x4", "plays" for "place"); "it", "that" and a bare direction mean the newest sound (the one last spoken of or loaded); a response from the person's user agent marks the state stale (a sound loaded is known to the next request); the guard questions are said as they are (no dialogue model); a caption of a sound ("*snoring*", "[Click]") is noise.
- CAE-ASM (software): the window places a loaded sound at 0, 0, 0; a sound or the listener (position, and orientation by its arrow) is dragged with the mouse and the move goes to the Module as the command a person could say (move NAME to x, y, z / move me to x, y, facing G degrees); UserPoV alone is not reported as a failure by Audio Object Editing; the demonstration runs the grammar alone (the dialogue model took 15 to 20 s a command and sometimes did the wrong thing); Application Context of the ASM: rules for time (start/end), listener, closer/farther, "the room is A by B", statements ("pluto is at x 2, y 0").
- Not to forget in the web specs: MMC-JSI and CAE-ASM I/O tables for the TypedText Port (OSD-BTO-V1.5 #1) on the ASM and the VCI.
- 2026-10-28 (later) MMC-JSI behaviour: only the scenes the guide asks for by name (StateRequests) count; the scenes of earlier sessions, which the application keeps and lists, no longer hide their sounds as "already in a scene"; an object made of others (a scene's own object, its copies) is not a sound; "it"/"that" and bare directions mean the newest sound; the guard "Which object do you mean?" steps aside whenever a rule matches. Application Context of the ASM: rules "put NAME at the centre" (a sound kept, or one in the scene). ASM window: starts in Text mode (microphone off, answers written and never said); Voice/Text buttons; a loaded sound goes to the centre and is placed again until the scene has it.

## 2026-10-28 (afternoon) - CAE-UCM User Command: NewScene, OpenedScene, NamedScene (UPLOAD schemas/CAE3/V1.0/data/UserCommand.json)
- Three new fields of UserCommandRQ (strings) and three new Action values of UserCommandRS: NewScene (a new empty Audio Scene, opened), OpenedScene (an Audio Scene kept, opened), NamedScene (the open Audio Scene is given a name; kept under it and under no other). The Scene that was open stays kept. Audio Scene Editing's. - MMC-JSI (AIM behaviour, no schema change besides APC below): the scene the application is in is a variable ({scene} in the rules, the shortcuts and StateRequests of the guide; the JSI setting Scene gives the first value); it changes only when the application's response says NewScene/OpenedScene/NamedScene was Done; rules may carry Sets (a variable the command sets) and Raw (a name the person gives keeps its case). The grammar's and the shortcuts' commands are completed with the whole schema (a sentence with no trigger verb, "pluto at the centre", was left incomplete).
- Application Context (MMC-APC-V2.5): rules Sets and Raw are properties of a Rule - CHECK schemas/MMC/V2.5/data/ApplicationContext.json (UPLOAD) for Rules.items: Sets (object of strings) and Raw (array of strings) may need declaring; Memory and Asks Pattern/Unless were added earlier today.
- CAE-ASM window: keeps the name of the scene that is open (a file beside the Module's storage) and gives it to the Module at the next start; no fresh scene name per start any more.
- Web to update: the CAE-UCM Data Type page (three fields, three actions, with the Semantics table) and the Audio Scene Editing AIM page (the three actions).

## 2026-10-28 - ASM window: nothing is kept from one session to the next unless saved
- Window behaviour (not a schema): at start, whatever the Module holds that is not in a saved scene is deleted (scenes, then sounds) and the scene saved last is opened. What is saved is recorded in a file beside the Module storage (`<storage>.saved.json`); the first time, the storage is copied to `<storage>.before-saving-<time>` before anything is deleted.
- ASM-VCI.orch: new input stream `SceneCommand (CAE-UCM-V1.0:2)` (the window asks the Module for the list of Audio Scenes and deletes unsaved ones); report ports SceneReport/SpeechReport/SpeechSceneRep/MultimodalRep now go to their own devices. Web: the ASM/VCI figures and port tables must show CAE-UCM:2 as an input.
- JSI (RuleGrammar): a name the application has in another set is no longer matched to a similar name by typo tolerance ("pippo 2" is not "pippo"). Reply to "list my scenes" with none: "No scene is saved yet."
- ApplicationContext (CAE-ASM): new rule after "put NAME at the centre" (Stored): the same with the name as written (Raw), for a sound just loaded that the interface has not yet seen listed; pronouns excluded. Window: after a load it asks "what have i loaded" before placing.

## 2026-10-28 - Acoustic Profile of the room: list, set, hear
- CAE-UCM (schemas/CAE3/V1.0/data/UserCommand.json): `ListedObjects.Kind` gains "Acoustic Profile" (the Profile of the open Scene, or of the Scene named in ObjectID; not part of All); the listed item gains `AcousticProfile` (CAE-ACP Scene). `ModifiedScene.AcousticProfile`: the properties given are set, those not given keep their value (before: the Profile given replaced the old one). Reason: setting one wall property must not clear the others.
- CAE-ASE (Audio Scene Editing): lists the Acoustic Profile; merges ModifiedScene. Kind "Acoustic Profile" is listed by Audio Scene Editing.
- ApplicationContext (CAE-ASM): rules to set Absorption, Diffusion, Reverberation (RT60 as a Plot with one row [1000 Hz, seconds] - to confirm), Reflectivity (early and late together); shortcut to list the profile. Setting the early or the late time alone is not possible while both are required in CAE-ACP Reflectivity.
- Renderer (User Agent, not a standard): a Scene with an Acoustic Profile and a Closed Space is heard in its room: first-order reflections off the six walls (amplitude sqrt(1-Absorption)), late reverberation (Schroeder reverberator, time = RT60 or Sabine), Diffusion = density of the late sound, EarlyReflectionTime = reflections heard one by one, LateReflectionTime = onset of the late sound. Without a Profile the Scene is heard dry as before.
- Window: floating panel "Acoustic Profile of the room" (button "Room acoustics" or "list the properties of the acoustic profile"; closes with "close the acoustic properties").

## 2026-10-28 (evening) - CAE-UCM and CAE-ACP changes for Audio Scene Management (approved by the author; specified in M3282, rationale in M3281)
- schemas/CAE3/V1.0/data/UserCommand.json (UPLOAD, changed): UCM-1 MovedObjects.NewSpatialAttitude, ModifiedObjects.SpatialAttitude and ChangedObjects.SpatialAttitude accept a Spatial Attitude in full or only the parts that change (Position, Orientation, SpatialAttitudeTime; new $def SpatialAttitudeOrChange) - reason: setting a time or a position alone no longer needs the rest read and resent. UCM-2 ListedObjects: Kinds "Listener" and "Closed Space" (not part of All); response Members gain Time and Level, a Scene gains Duration, new response fields UserPoV and ClosedSpace, AcousticProfile may be the Object or Scene part - reason: the Scene can be read back. UCM-3 new request field ModifiedClosedSpace (Shape, Walls, DefaultMaterial, at least one) and new Action value - reason: the room can be edited without being replaced. UCM-4 AcousticProfile is a profile in full or an AcousticProfileID (new $defs ObjectAcousticProfileOrID, SceneAcousticProfileOrID) in ChangedObjects, ModifiedObjects, MovedObjects and ModifiedScene; a profile in full is kept under its ID; ListedObjects Kind "Acoustic Profile" with ObjectID = a Scene gives its profile, with no ObjectID lists the kept profiles (it was the open Scene's); DeletedObjects accepts an AcousticProfileID. UCM-5 top-level LUFS is the loudness of the open Scene (programme); ModifiedObjects entries gain LUFS (the loudness of the Object), and ModifiedObjects no longer takes the top-level LUFS as the amount for its entries (each entry needs an Acoustic Profile, a Spatial Attitude or a LUFS).
- schemas/CAE3/V1.0/data/AcousticProfile.json (UPLOAD, changed): ACP-1 Absorption 0 to 1, mean absorption coefficient, with its relation to the Materials of the Closed Space (a surface with a Material takes it; one without takes the Absorption, as the DefaultMaterial). ACP-2 RT60, RT30, RT20 are 2D Plots of (frequency in Hz, time in seconds), a Plot of one pair applies at all frequencies; EDT in seconds. ACP-3 EarlyReflectionTime = seconds from the direct sound to the first reflection, LateReflectionTime = seconds from the first reflection to the onset of the late reverberation. ACP-4 Diffusion 0 to 1. ACP-5 Doppler factors defined. ACP-6 the level of the late reverberation is derived (critical distance 0.057 sqrt(V/T60); T60 = RT60 or Sabine 0.161 V/A). ACP-7 Object part requires only Header and AcousticProfileID. ACP-8 Spectrogram (time s, frequency Hz, level dB), Loudness in LUFS.
- Checked: both schemas are valid JSON Schema; 21 instances (partial move, profile by ID, closed-space edit, listing, deletion, responses, and refusals: empty attitude, Absorption 1.5, Diffusion 2, empty entry) behave as intended.
- NOT yet aligned (software): C# UserCommand model (AIMs/Core/UserCommand.cs), Audio Scene Editing / Audio Object Editing AIMs, the interpreter (ApplicationContext of CAE-ASM) and the window still follow the old fields: partial attitude, ModifiedClosedSpace, Listener / Closed Space listing, profile by ID, LUFS per entry, and the "Acoustic Profile" listing by Scene name. The renderer should take Absorption from the Materials of the Closed Space per surface and derive the late level from V and T60.
- Web to update: CAE-UCM Data Type page (the new fields, Kinds, Semantics), CAE-ACP Data Type page (units, ranges, meanings, relation with Material), Audio Scene Editing and Audio Object Editing AIM pages (new actions and Kinds).
- Web updated 2026-10-28 (evening), via WordPress REST (WP revisions allow rollback): CAE-UCM Data Type page (43319: Functional Requirements, Semantics - also the NewScene/OpenedScene/NamedScene rows that were missing), CAE-ACP Data Type page (45881: units, ranges, meanings, relation with Material, late field level), Audio Scene Editing AIM page (44329: partial attitude, kept Acoustic Profiles, Listener / Closed Space listing, New/Opened/Named Scene, Closed Space, Modified Closed Space, Modified Scene, LUFS of the Scene), Audio Object Editing AIM page (43282: profile by identifier, LUFS in the entry). Not yet done: the schemas UPLOAD (UserCommand.json, AcousticProfile.json) to schemas.mpai.community, the figures of ASM/VCI, and the software alignment above.

- Schemas: UserCommand.json UCM-1 is now ONE object form (SpatialAttitudeOrChange: all the properties of a Spatial Attitude, none required, at least one of Position, Orientation, SpatialAttitudeTime), not an anyOf of two objects - the JSON Interface's schema view reads one object; AudioSceneManager.json and VoiceCommandInterface.json: TypedText (OSD-BTO #1) is routed (VCI: to JSI and EDP, as the recognised text is; ASM: to the VCI) - the author: "Typed Text is text the user types and the text produced by VCI".
- Core (AIMs/Core): SpatialAttitude says which parts a command states (PositionStated, OrientationStated) with a converter; AcousticProfile may be a reference (Reference); KeptProfiles (kept under their AcousticProfileID); ModifiedClosedSpace; ObjectChange.LUFS; ObjectPlacement.AcousticProfileID; ListedObject UserPoV / ClosedSpace, ListedMember Time / Level; ListKinds Listener and Closed Space.
- Audio Scene Editing: partial move/change (merged with what the member has), ModifiedClosedSpace, list of Listener / Closed Space / Acoustic Profile (of the Scene named; with no name, the kept profiles), Members with Time and Level, Duration of a Scene, profile by identifier and kept (the Scene's profile as it now is), deletion of a kept profile, top-level LUFS = NotSupported (the Audio Scene Descriptors carry no loudness of a Scene - a GAP: OSD-ASD / BAS has no field for it). Audio Object Editing: the LUFS of a ModifiedObjects entry, profile by identifier; the top-level LUFS is no longer an Object's.

STILL RED, NOT FROM THIS CHANGE (same set with the old schemas): 40 of the 83 JsiTests / JsiGrammarTests, RevocationAimTests (the AOE L3 has no OSD-AUO input), the tests that start the whole Module (the L3 pins the SHA-256 of UserCommand.json and it is not that of the published file: pins of 1MMC-JSI-V2.5-I02 (CommandSchema, ResponseSchema) must be updated when UserCommand.json and AcousticProfile.json are uploaded), AsmStudioSelfTests, CaeUscV25DataTypeTests.

- 2026-10-29 MMM speech path follows the standard (D:\AI\MMM): a User's speech is UM-Captured at the human's U-Location With a Speech Qualifier (TFA-SPQ: time, stream format), Identified (Identify) as a Speech Object and MM-Added at the seat; the data is fed only after Identify. Used the OpenAPI (MMM/Api/MMM-API.json: Nil/At/With complements), no new field. Observations on MMM-TEC V2.2 (author to decide): (1) Table 2 gives UM-Capture as MMM-1PP, but its page and its schema (UMCapturePA.json) say MMM-1CP, the code of UM-Actuate; (2) the UM-Capture page returns "Nil Item" while the Verification Use Cases show UM-Capture (Nil DataID) followed by Identify - I followed the use cases; (3) the Identify page says header MMM-IDF, its schema MMM-IDP, and its schema link points to /data/ instead of /actions/.
- 2026-10-29 MMM conventions of mine that are NOT in MMM-TEC (informal contents of MM-Send Messages, listed in M3283 section 8): hand;up/down/down;user=X (to AVS), Hands-for-<User> (AVS to each participant), spoke;user;ms (Secretary to AVS, only when a hand is up), summary;lang;mode (to Secretary) with answer Summary-for-<id> (JSON with MMC-SUM-V2.5). The author to decide whether they stay.
- 2026-10-29 MMM Speech Objects are streams with no duration fixed beforehand (D:\AI\MMM): the speech is one Speech Object from start to stop; its Qualifier (TFA-SPQ) names only the stream format (no time); the Speech Object has no BasicSpeechObjectTime while the stream goes on (BasicSpeechObjectTime is optional in OSD-BSO) and the M-Instance puts it in when the stream closes. No schema changed. The Secretary listens to a streamed Speech Object and has it recognised at pauses. To decide: SimpleTime requires EndTime, so a stream has no way to state an open-ended Time inside itself; for now the Time is simply absent until the end is known.
- 2026-10-29 MMM: the mouth of a Persona follows the speech of its human, in real time (D:\AI\MMM). The User's client derives mouth shapes from 16 ms of speech (loudness, an LPC envelope with the first two formants, noise) in the Oculus viseme set the Rocketbox Personae carry (AA_VI_00..14) and sends them as an Animation Stream: UM-Capture With an Animation Qualifier (TFA-AMQ-V1.5, SubTypes Animation, ContentFormat Viseme, TransportFormat AudioVisemeStream, both values from the TFA lists), Identify, MM-Animate the Persona With the stream; frames go out over a WebSocket (/ws/v2.2/animation/{id}), watched by whoever may see the Persona. The frame payload ({"f":[[viseme,weight],...]}) is mine: the standard names the stream format but does not define its content - the author to decide. No AIM here derives visemes from live audio (GFD needs the words; EFD is identity). Coarse, no words, no delay.

2026-10-30 MMM: a catalogue Persona is offered to one human only. Once chosen it leaves the list (GET /api/v2.2/register/personae returns only the free ones) and a second Register choosing it is refused (409 Clash, "was chosen by another human"); the viewer refreshes the list every 2 s and asks for a new choice. The standard does not define this - the M-Instance's own decision, so that two people do not meet as the same avatar. The author to decide whether it is to be said in MMM-TEC (Register). Test still to add.

4. Access of the L3 I03: CommandSchema and ResponseSchema (MMM4/V2.2/data/UserCommand.json) and ApplicationContext (MMM/Access/SecretaryApplicationContext.json); its SHA-256 are those of the files now, to be recomputed when the files change; the Uris point to the repository (not published).
5. Dialogue (my convention, not in the standard): a participant sends its Speech Object, a stream, to the Secretary with MM-Send (the M-Instance gives the recipient the Item, not a copy) instead of MM-Adding it in the room; the Secretary listens (a pause of 1.2 s ends a request), has it recognised, interpreted (the real JSON Interface, llama3.1 via Ollama) and answers with the Message Secretary-<User>-<n> {Say, Speech (16 kHz PCM, base64), Written, Do{Summary,Display,Microphone,Screen,Mode}}; only that participant can read it. A yes/no after "close the meeting" is understood by the Secretary itself. Sharing a screen cannot be started by voice (the browser wants the human's gesture).
6. Viewer: the three summary cells (form, language, Summary) are gone; one Secretary button (a toggle), the display shows the summary (and written answers if asked). Board height halved; second row labels.
7. Not yet done: the Response Hint of the JSI is not used to word the answers (the Secretary words them itself, in English); no web page text; no schemas upload.

2026-10-30 MMM viewer (not the standard): the shared screen is no longer shown full-window with the room as a thumbnail. It is shown in the room, on all four walls of the meeting room at once (the east wall, which has the door, a smaller screen beside the door; the north one clear of the MMM sign); each participant turns to the wall it likes and sizes it with Ctrl + wheel (zoom). What a client does with the Visual Object, where and how big, is its own decision. Still to do: the sharer's clickable floating window (Document Picture-in-Picture) to come back to the meeting, the Secretary commands for sharing (the convener enables sharing for all; B asks A to hand over, the Secretary writes on A's display), the board cut to Secretary, Mic and Share screen.
2026-10-30 MMM viewer: board button Edit slides (next to Share screen): the same sharing, but the browser brings the chosen window (PowerPoint) to the front (CaptureController.setFocusBehavior focus-captured-surface), so the sharer edits and the room watches on the walls in real time. A sharer that shares a window sees it on the walls too (not a whole screen or the meeting tab: it would show the room in itself). Not tried with a real share. The deck presenter idea is abandoned.
2026-10-30 MMM viewer: the participant's own board is no longer a box on the table: the buttons are ONE line at the foot of the page (full width, 24 px), so the room has almost the whole window. The boards of the others (name, raised hand) stay on the table.
2026-10-30 MMM viewer/server (not the standard): (1) no Floating room: neither the button nor the window; (2) Share screen becomes Close screen while sharing (Edit slides is hidden meanwhile); Chrome's own "Stop sharing" bar is the browser's and cannot be removed (it can be hidden); (3) each wall of the meeting room is wholly the screen (black, the picture in it as large as it goes), the MMM sign gives way; the east wall beside the door; (4) /viewer/whoami on loopback takes the name from ?user=, so reloading the page returns the participant to its seat (before: registration again); after a resume in a seat the hand, Secretary and microphone are started again.
2026-10-30 MMM (not the standard): the seats of the MMM meeting room are given with the north side last (corner seats, then the middle one), so that nobody sits between the participants and the screen on the north wall; Edit slides stays on the bar, dimmed, while sharing. Chrome's blue "Stop sharing" bar is the browser's: a page cannot remove it.
2026-10-30 MMM viewer (corrects the entry above): the seating order is NOT changed (put back). While a screen is shown, the Personae that sit straight (looking as their seat says) in front of a wall (north, south, east, west of the table) step aside round the table by 25 degrees, for everybody; a Persona that has turned stays; they come back when the screen is closed. Rendering only: the positions in the M-Instance do not change.
2026-10-30 MMM viewer (corrects the entries above): while a screen is shown the three seats of the north side (between the table and the north wall) are moved and compacted to the two ends of the wall - two at the west end, one at the east end - with their chairs (the chairs are cut out of the model of the building, one piece for each seat of the meeting room) and the Personae that sit straight in them; the Secretary (east seat) and the others stay. The view of a User in a moved seat goes with it. Rendering only, the same for everybody. The microphone starts when the participant sits (it did only with &mic in the address; &mic=off keeps it off).
2026-10-30 MMM: SCREEN HELPER (D:\AI\MMM\ScreenHelper, copy in Downloads\MMM-ScreenHelper): a tray program on the sharer's machine that captures a window (PrintWindow) or a screen and sends JPEG frames to the M-Instance over the same WebSocket the page uses (/ws/v2.2/screen/{id}), so that Chrome's own "Stop sharing" bar does not appear. The page talks to it on http://127.0.0.1:7391 (/status, /pick, /share, /stop; the sharer chooses in the helper's own window; only the page named in /pick may start the sharing); without it the buttons use the browser's capture. No sound yet. Tried on one machine, in auto mode, with a second participant seeing the frames on the walls.
2026-10-30 MMM viewer (corrects the entry on the seats): the seats of the north side (chairs and Personae) are drawn aside ONLY for the participant who is looking at the screen (a screen is shown and the participant faces the north wall, within 55 degrees); turning away, it sees the room as it is. Nobody's position changes in the M-Instance, nobody else's view changes, and the participant's own view is never moved.

## 2026-10-31 - Between Controllers: Trust Authority of a service, payload channel (AIF V3.0 Communication 4, 7, 9; Basic API 3.9; PTF Security)
- Web (done 2026-10-31, WordPress, AIF V3.0): Communication (Table 1 row 'Payload channel', Table 2 Payloads of Remote/External, section 4 closing paragraph, section 7, section 10, COM-7 reworded, COM-11 new); Basic API 3.9 introduction (the Controller establishes trust only at its User Agent's request; payloads travel apart); Zero Trust and Profiles, row 'Admission between Controllers'. Draft: Downloads\AIF-V3-Communication-payload-channel-draft.docx. PTF pages left alone (the Verifier's TrustAnchors already identify the authority).
- Software (D:\AI\AIF, 2026-10-31): MPAI_AIFU_External_Authority / _Join / _Leave in ControllerApi (+ IControllerApi defaults = FAILED over MPAI-MAS); ExternalAuthorityService (enrolment: Challenge signed by the Authority proves it holds the anchor; proof of the joiner's key; policy Roles/DefaultRole/AuthorityRole/Exchange/Attest/LifetimeMinutes; the endpoint is also the rendezvous), ExternalMember, ExternalTrust, RoleCheckedLink, RendezvousDiscovery; payload channel in ExternalHub (second TLS connection, raw body frames in RemoteLink, PayloadStore.PutRemote = lazy fetch, length+hash checked, refused and counted); tests Test/Mpai.Aif.Tests/ExternalAuthorityTests.cs (8, pass).
- PROPOSED PTF CHANGE (not made): PTF-SEC Security.json Integrity is closed (additionalProperties false). The signed Message states the payloads that travel apart in DataXMData.Security.Integrity.Payloads = [{Reference, Length, Hash}]; the schema needs that member (or another place) - the author decides. Until then a Message with payloads apart does not validate against Security.json.
- Deviations from Basic API 3.9 to decide: External_Join takes an optional role (the C signature has none; the Authority's DefaultRole otherwise); rendezvous NULL (announcement in range) is not implemented (FAILED); 'authority' is the Trust Anchor object (JSON); ExternalAnchor/ExternalRendezvous are the Controller's way to let the User Agent publish them.
- AIH-HSP: not changed. HFE and HBE Modules are Continuous (so External Ports are possible) but all their Ports have IsRemote false and the Back End answers many clients: the ExternalHub sends an output to every linked Controller, so a reply to one Front End would reach the others. Needs the author's decision (addressing of replies) before Trust.cs is replaced.
- AIH-HSP, 2026-10-31 (first use of External_Authority/Join): the Federated Learn Ports of the Health Front End (FederatedLearnRequestUN in, FederatedLearnResponseNB out) and of the Health Back End (FederatedLearnResponseEN in, FederatedLearnRequestNE out) are External Ports: IsRemote true in the L2 (schemas/AIH1/V1.1/AIMs/HealthFrontEnd.json, HealthBackEnd.json: schemas to upload) and in the L3 (AIMs/AMDs/1AIH-HFE-V1.1-I01.json, 1AIH-HBE-V1.1-I01.json; SHA pins of the L2 in the L3s to update when uploaded). STANDARD CHANGE: L2 Ports of AIH-HFE/AIH-HBE become External; the web pages of Health Front End and Health Back End (Ports tables) and the Reference Models slides should say so. HFE.orch/HBE.orch: no device for the Learn Ports. HbeUserAgent.StartService / HfeUserAgent.Join; IFrontEnd, TrustedFrontEnd, TrustedFrontEndClient removed. Test AihFirstSliceTests passes (2).
- AIH-HSP, still through the User Agents (not changed): registration, AHP requests, DIA, ADT. Reason: the Front End Module has no Port to give the End User's User Agent the Back End's answer (the Tokens); making them External needs a change of the Reference Model of the Front End (author's decision). Also Port numbers: the Back End's REG/AHP responses leave on Output 1 while the Front End receives them on Input 2 - External Ports match by Data Type and Port Number, so the numbering would need to change too.
- Line ends: files I edited by script keep their CRLF.
- WDL (AIF draft), 2026-10-31: new command 'ask Controller to join "<service>" [as "<name>"] [role <Role>]' (MPAI_AIFU_External_Join). It is a step of the trust protocol, not a datum: the workflow names the service; the User Agent knows the Trust Authorities its User decided to trust (anchor and rendezvous, DeviceRegistry.RegisterService). No Qualifier is used (an earlier version stretched the Qualifier of 'type'; withdrawn). Code: AIF/UserAgent (Wdl/Workflow.cs, WorkflowReader.cs, Ua/WorkflowInterpreter.cs, Ua/DeviceRegistry.cs), ControllerApi/IAsyncControllerApi.cs; tests JoinServiceTests. The web page of the Workflow Description Language (AIF V3.0) is not updated yet.
- AIH-HSP registration through the workflow (decided with the author 2026-10-31: join first, the workflow drives registration, the Front End Module answers on an output Port): not built yet. Port changes it needs: HFE gets an Output REG#2 (RegisterResponseNU) to the User Agent (the HDP already has its Output REG#2); HFE Output REG#1 and Input REG#2 become External; HBE Input REG#1 and its response Output (REG, number 1 today) become External with the response renumbered to 2.
- AIH-HSP registration through the workflow (built 2026-10-31, test AihFirstSliceTests passes): HFE.orch joins the service 'AIH-HBE' (role FrontEnd) before anything is streamed; the End User's request and the Back End's answer travel between the Controllers. Reference Model changes (STANDARD CHANGE, L2 and L3, schemas to upload: HealthFrontEnd.json, HealthBackEnd.json): HFE gets Output REG#2 'RegisterResponseNU' to the End User's User Agent (the Back End's answer, with the Tokens); HFE Output REG is now explicitly REG#1; HFE Output REG#1 and Input REG#2, HBE Input REG#1 and Output REG are External (IsRemote true); HBE's registration response leaves on Output REG#2 (was REG, number 1), so that it meets the Front End's Input REG#2. The web pages of HFE/HBE (Ports tables, Topology) and the Reference Models slides need the same.
- Interim for Third-Party Users (option 1 chosen by the author: they will join as Controllers): they have no Module yet, so the Back End's hub gives the User Agent what it cannot route to a Controller (ExternalHub.Unrouted; ControllerApi.OutputRead on an External Output Port returns only those). Open: the Third-Party User's Module (new AIM/Reference Model element: a decision for the author). Also: the Module is Paused while a round of federated learning is requested, so that it has every request before the first answer (a round is complete when answers = requests).
- AIH-HSP profiles, 2026-10-31 (author's decision: ONE Health Front End, no new L2/L3, no formal MPAI-PRF profile; what the Front End and the Back End do depends on the User Profile ingested): End User = an individual, works on its own AIH Data only, no control of licence; Third-Party User = a Third-Party User Profile, or a User Profile of an individual who is a member of an organisation, makes/keeps no AIH Data, trains nothing, asks only for AIH Data Processing as each licence prescribes (no upload, no de-identification, no audit, no Federated Learning, no licence setting or change). Done: UserProfile.json (AIH-UPR-V1.1) Human gets optional Membership [{CompanyName, RegistrationCode, Country, Type[] per DataUser}] (schema to upload); the Descriptions of the L2 (HealthFrontEnd.json, HealthBackEnd.json: to upload) and L3 (1AIH-HFE/HBE-V1.1-I01) say it; FE HDP and BE HDP behave by profile (AIMs/AIH/V1.1/HDP/Profiles.cs); tests added (AihSchemaTests, AihBackEndModuleTests, AihFrontEndModuleTests). The web pages (User Profile Data Type: Membership; HFE/HBE: the two profiles) need the same. No Ports added: registration and AHP Ports serve both profiles.
- NOT DONE (needs the Module to take over from the Back End's User Agent): the AIH Data Process Ports (AHP) as External Ports for Front Ends and Third-Party Users. Blockers: HbeUserAgent keeps the owners and copies of AIH Data (needed by de-identification and audit) and answers the Blockchain (Smart Contract, Licence Confirm) synchronously inside each request; with External AHP the requests no longer pass through it. These must move into the Module (the review's step 3), and AHP responses must echo AIHDataProcessID for the hub's correlation. Until then Third-Party Users stay on the User Agent's path (ExternalHub.Unrouted).
- Routing of answers in the hub is by the Message's own identifier named in the service policy ('Correlate': RegisterID, AIHDataProcessID, FederatedLearnID, DeIDAndAnonymID, AuditID), not by any identifier (an earlier version could send the Back End's own question about user-1 to user-1's Front End).
- 2026-10-31 (evening) AIH-HSP: the AIH Data Process Ports are External too (HFE Output AHP#1 and Input AHP#2; HBE Input AHP#1 and Output - renumbered from 1 to 2 so that it meets the Front End's Input 2); the Third-Party User runs a Health Front End (no Unrouted stopgap for it); the Back End's User Agent answers the Blockchain all the time and keeps no owners/copies (AIH Data Processing says which AIH Data are a user's when it authorises an audit, copies found by ParentAIHDataID). Hub: answers matched by the policy's Correlate, Controllers named by Address (HFEID), clients hold their outputs until linked, join waits for the link, handlers are set before the Hello (a race that lost the first Message). Schemas to upload (package in Downloads\schemas-publish-2026-10-31, FileZilla queue schemas-upload-queue-2026-10-31.xml): AIH1 HealthBackEnd.json, HealthFrontEnd.json, data/UserProfile.json (+ the 20 others in MANIFEST.txt). Web done (WordPress, AIH-HSP V1.1 and AIF V3.0): Health Front End and Health Back End (Register Response output, Profiles), User Profile (Membership), Third-Party User Profile and Register (notes), Architecture (the Controllers carry the exchanges), AIF Workflow Description (join row replaced: 'join "<service>" [as ...] [role ...]'). Not on the web yet: the AHP/DIA/ADT Ports tables of HBE (response is Output 2), the L2 Ports diagrams (figures), PTF Security Integrity.Payloads (proposed, not made).

## 2026-10-23 - ACCESS BY DATA TYPE, ENFORCED (AIF V3.0 STANDARD CHANGE, author's requirement: enforceable, no new L3 for a new set)
- AIM Metadata AccessSources entries are now by KIND of data, not by Source name: DataType (of the items, required), Writer (Provider or User, required), KeyPrefixes, IsOptional, Name and Description (labels). An AIM reads every Source of that Data Type written by that kind of Writer, whatever its name: another set of the same kind needs no new L2/L3. L1 AIMMetadata.json; L2s/L3s of MMC-ACR and MMC-MAC declare OSD-BVO-V1.5 (faces) and OSD-BSO-V1.5 (libri-speech), Provider, optional.
- AIF API: a Source states the Data Type of its items when created (MPAI_AIFU_Access_Create(Source, DataType), MPAI_AIFP_Access_Create(Source, DataType)); new MPAI_AIFM_Access_Sources(DataType) for an AIM; MPAI_AIFM_Access_Get returns NOT_AUTHORISED outside the AIM's entries. Web: Storage 6.1/6.2/6.3/6.4, Basic API 3.9.1, 4.11.3.1, 4.11.3.4 (new), 6.1; Metadata AccessSources row, MET-7, MET-8.
- Code (EI): AccessStore (DataType per Source, DataTypeOf, Sources, SourcesOf), IAccessReader (InfoOf, SourcesOf; RemoteAccess over the link), BoundAccess (enforcement per AIM: Data Type, Writer role, Key prefixes), AccessEntry, DescriptorNode.AccessSources, Controller/AimHost (entries bound per AIM; AIM hosts bind them from the L3), AimContext.MPAI_AIFM_Access_Sources, UserAgent (a Module is refused when a required entry has no Source), AccessProvider (Create with a Data Type), L2Conformance (an L3 declares every kind of Access of its L2). Tests: AccessSourcesTests (new, 6), AifAccessTests (adapted), MetadataTests; Expected access-user.json and access-host.json re-recorded. 22 + 22 tests pass.
- Limits: on an AIM host the Controller trusts the host to apply the AIM's entries (the host binds them from the L3 it holds); the descriptors ACR writes and MAC reads in Shared Storage (rule: ACR writes, Readers MAC) are the next step; Models, voices as Access Sources with their URLs are next.

## 2026-10-23 - ACCESS IDENTIFIED BY URI (supersedes the by-Data-Type entry above; AIF V3.0 STANDARD CHANGE; author's decision: no classification of what Access holds)
- AIM Metadata AccessSources entry: Name (the handle by which the AIM asks), Uri (a file, or a set of files under a URI ending in a slash with a manifest.json {"Items": {key: sha256}}), Sha256 (of the file or of the manifest), KeyPrefixes, IsOptional, Description. The Writer of what the Controller obtains is the Controller; the Provider push API and the User API stay. Decided by me on the author's delegation: the Provider push stays (a spec'd, tested capability); pull by URI is added.
- Code: AccessFetcher (obtain by http/https/file, check SHA-256, keep under the URI; items of a set on first read, each against the manifest), BoundAccess (an AIM reads only the Uris of its entries and the Keys they allow; a file's Key is its name), AccessEntry, DescriptorNode.AccessSources, AimHost.BindAccess/Fetcher, UserAgent (a Module is refused when a required entry cannot be obtained; message names the reason), AimContext.MPAI_AIFM_Access_Sources(Name) -> Uris, L2Conformance (an L3 declares every Name of its L2), AccessStore/Provider/User API unchanged (the by-Data-Type changes were reverted). Tests: AccessSourcesTests (6), AifAccessTests, MetadataTests; Expected access-user/host re-recorded; 138 tests pass in the groups run.
- L1 AIMMetadata.json; L2s/L3s of MMC-ACR and MMC-MAC declare faces and libri-speech (Uri and Sha256 are placeholders until the data is hosted: the author to say where). Web: Storage 6.1-6.5 (new 6.5), Basic API 3.9.1, 4.11.3.1, 4.11.3.4, 6.1; Metadata AccessSources row, MET-7, MET-8.
- Limits: on an AIM host the items of a set are not fetched lazily (the Controller answers from what it holds); models and voices still come from aim-settings.json (Source:/SHA256:), to be moved into AccessSources next; the ML Model proposal in Downloads is no longer a prerequisite.

## 2026-10-27 - Application Context is a Data Type (MMC-APC-V2.5)
- Decision (author): the VCI/JSON Interface figure (MPAI-MMC.pptx, slide 55) keeps an Application Context input. A Port needs a Data Type (M3194), so the Application Context is a Data Type of MMC V2.5: acronym MMC-APC-V2.5, schemas/MMC/V2.5/data/ApplicationContext.json (the structure; Header const MMC-APC-V2.5; optional CommandSchema and ResponseSchema addresses). The path schemas/MMC/V2.5/access/ was never published.
- L2s: MMC-JSI and MMC-VCI have an optional Input Port ApplicationContext (MMC-APC-V2.5); VCI Topology: boundary APC -> JSI APC. CAE-ASM does not use it (the context comes from Access: Setting ApplicationContext); the port stays unconnected there.
- Behaviour (JSON Interface): an Application Context received on the Port replaces the one read from Access for the following requests, if it follows the schema (otherwise ignored); no change when none arrives.
- Schemas to upload: MMC/V2.5/data/ApplicationContext.json (new), MMC/V2.5/AIMs/VoiceCommandInterface.json, MMC/V2.5/AIMs/JSONInterface.json.
- Web: MMC Data Types page: row 'Application Context | MMC-APC | The standing description of an application served by JSON Interface: the addresses of the schemas of its commands and of its responses and what they do not say (conventions, words, examples).'
- M3283 drafted (MMM Conference recap, features MC-1..MC-6) in input2000 on 2026-10-29.
- MMC-TTS-V2.5 speaks Greek (2026-10-29): Piper voice el_GR-rapunzelina-medium (CC0 dataset, MIT repo; fetched from huggingface main - the v1.0.0 tag has only low) declared as Voice:el / VoiceConfig:el in the L3 1MMC-TTS-V2.5-I01 and I03 and in schemas/MMC/V2.5/AIMs/TextToSpeech.json (schema to upload); MMC-CTS LanguageDetector knows 'el' by script; Greek added to the language lists of the clients and the MTS app; test GreekVoiceTests.

## 2026-11-01 - CAE-USC V2.5 reference software against the AIF V3.0 conformance criteria
- Check: Test/Mpai.Aif.Tests/CaeUscAifConformanceTests.cs (8 tests): every 1CAE-*-V2.5 L3 validates against the AIM Metadata Schema and is an instance of its L2 (MET-1, MET-2); Ports and Topology ends by Data Type and Port Number (MET-4, MET-5); AccessSources Terms, Names once, one Sha256 per Uri (MET-8..10); the CAE-USC Workflows (ARP, EAE x2, EES, SRS) are read and offer to/ask of Ports their Module declares (WDL-1,2,4,5); a Topology with a loop is Continuous (EXE-3); the Apps name no AIM and ModuleHost reads no Metadata of the Module (UA-2,3,4); no AIM opens a file (AIM-5); the provider reports for each AIM the binary its L3 names (CTR-2/3, AIM-7).
- Fixed (gaps 1-3): (1) UA names AIMs: SRS App passed settings keyed by AIM names and ModuleHost read the Module's L3 and rewrote Relation to External. Now the Controller has UserAgent.AllAimsApart (a deployment places every AIM of the Module on the host named for the Module; the Metadata stays as written), ModuleHost.Start(module, profile, ...) reads no Metadata, and the settings of the voice engine are a profile of the deployment (AIMs/aim-settings.openvoice.json) the SRS App selects by name. (2) No Store verification: ModuleHost now gives the Controller a Trust domain and Trust Anchors for the Controller and the AIM host (Trust Protocol; --anchor/--trust, no shared key), and a reference Store (AIF.Store.ReferenceStore.ApproveBuild: approves the fingerprint of each BinaryName an L3 names that the build holds); UscProvider.ImplementationOf now reports the binary of each AIM (Mpai.Cae.Usc.Smc ... as the L3s name them), so the Controller measures what the Store approved. LIMIT: the reference Store approves the build when the host starts - it proves the mechanism, not the guarantee of the real MPAI Store. (3) AIMs read files: AudioObject/SpeechObject no longer read DataURI files; the data reach the AIM in the Object (Data, base64).
- AIM-4 settled by the author 2026-11-01 (lenient): in continuous execution the run of the Module is one execution; the buffers ARP AAP/PAP/TIC/VAP and SRA keep during the run are not state between executions; no change. The AIF text does not say it (AI Modules, Execution): a sentence could.
- Open: CAE-ASM Apps (Apps/CAE/ASM) name AIMs (1MMC-ASR, VCI, JSI) and ASM.orch offers OSD-AUO-V1.5:1, which 1CAE-ASM-V1.0-I01 does not declare.

- 2026-11-01 S-3 TUNED (author: stop the check blocking the CAV): TrajectoryCheck: (1) CommitHorizon 2.5 s (ConstraintParameters): a violation of C1, C2, C4, C5 beyond it is reported, not hard; (2) C2 charges only going further from the lane's middle than the CAV already is (and the edge, 1.75 m); C4 charges only a speed above max(cap+0.5, v0 - NormalBrake*t); (3) FallBack(strategyBrake): never less braking than the strategy asked for (first second), never more than PhysicalBrake 8.4 m/s2 x grip. Result (Test/Reports/constraints-monitor.json, 5 MAS scenarios): no collision with Constraints on for IDM and SpaceTime; IDM+Constraints on ice no longer collides; SpaceTime+Constraints = SpaceTime (the check no longer blocks it); IDM+Constraints cut-in arrives (stopped 0.6 s). Open: IDM+Constraints cut-in leaves the lane by 3.78 m (IDM alone 1.26) - not looked into; IDM cut-in still undercuts the RSS distance (physically unavoidable at that gap). M3287 in input2000 rebuilt with S-7, C7.4 and the parameter rows (backup of the previous: scratchpad m87\M3287-backup-before-S3-tuning.docx); Downloads copy too. Constraints still default OFF (Constraints=1 to enable).
- 2026-11-01 OVERTAKING WITH THE SPACE-TIME PLANNER (author: go on): causes found and fixed. (1) The planner's following distance was seconds of worst-case reach (80-120 m, with the grip unknown 130 m) so the Path Selection Planning (LaneChangePlanner, DecisionDistance 60 m) never saw it held up; now the same-direction vehicle ahead is kept at the RSS safe distance of the Motion Constraints. (2) Traffic that keeps to its lane (no sideways speed) is taken in the frame of the lane (straight road ahead: the CAV's heading frame): lateral margin ConfinedMargin 0.5 m, lane slack OncomingSlack 0.3 m, speed along the lane; the hypothesis lines drawn from a noisy heading put a truck in the passing lane at 100 m. (3) A Remote CAV's report of an object the CAV perceives is dropped (own perception kept; the remote one is old). (4) Comfort speeding-up 2.0 m/s2 (ISO 15622 normal limit, was 1.5). (5) LaneChangePlanner setting DecisionSeconds (default 0 = as before): considers a pass within max(DecisionDistance, DecisionSeconds x speed). (6) The grip must be known: unknown = 0.4 makes the safe distance 130 m; the highway test loop tells the AMS the road state (CavInTheLoop.RoadState(friction) as an AMS-MAS Message). Test HighwayOncomingTests.TheSpaceTimePlannerPassesTheTruckToo (Duration Long; Drive got roadFriction, decisionSeconds, planner parameters): no collision, pulls out, 30 m/s, passes, back in lane (final 2811 m against the truck's 2231). The IDM oncoming tests and 34 related tests pass. Not yet re-run: the other recorded scenario tests with Planner=SpaceTime (Step5/6, MAS closed loop), nor the earlier animations (they show the OLD planner).
- 2026-11-01 FALL-BACK AT A CRAWL (found with the overtaking work): the fall-back's trajectory ended its braking where the CAV stops; the MAS's AMI looks ahead (preview) and, with no braking left there and a speed above 0.5 m/s, commanded the motor: the CAV crept at 0.8 m/s into a stopped vehicle (SpaceTime+Constraints 'a vehicle ahead brakes hard' collided). Now the braking is asked for the whole trajectory. The fall-back also never brakes less than the strategy's hardest braking in its first 3 s (was 1 s). All variants of the monitor test are collision-free; 34 related tests pass. The space-time planner is now closer to a vehicle ahead (RSS distance), so it undercuts the safe distance by up to 7 m in the cut-in scenario (it was 45 m clear before).

## 2026-11-02 - WorkflowInterpreter: a Status that was never answered tells nothing
AIF/UserAgent/Ua/WorkflowInterpreter.cs (software only, no schema/spec): the interlock poll (every 500 ms) brought every device to its safe state when StatusAsync was not Ok. Over a remote Controller (RemoteControllerApi) Status is always refused (Failed), so 0.5 s after start all devices of the ASM (Scene included) went to safe state and the scene was never shown. Now: if the link has never answered a Status, the poll ends; "no longer there" applies only to a Module that was there. Found with the ASM window (asm-ua.log: "is no longer there: Failed").

## 2026-11-02 - CUI-CPP V2.1 reference software: conformance with AIF V3.0 (author: read ALL the chapters)
Read all 22 chapters of AIF V3.0 and the 5 AIF Data Type pages (not a selection). The standard's own conformance of an AIM Implementation (Conformance 3.1/3.2): (1) AIM Instance Metadata validates against the AIM Metadata Schema and is an instance of its AIM Metadata (MET-1..10); (2) the Implementation meets AIM-1..7 (Ports by Data Type and Port Number, Controller API only, Objects with Qualifier, no state between executions except Storage, no real-world acquisition, supports what the Metadata declares, runs only after verification); (3) from the Data Types at its Input Ports it produces those at its Output Ports as its Standard specifies. The repository tests are our own aids, not AIF conformance tests.
- Checked: topology and I/O of the 7 AIMs and 2 composites against the published V2.1 pages: they agree (CAP = OMA + CDP + PDP; CPP = CAP + RMG + PRP; PRP takes PDD, SRM and the horizon).
- Fixed: the AIMs read the machine's clock (ReferenceModels.AsOf = UtcNow) for the age of a company; now they take the time of the Controller's time base (ports.Now = MPAI_AIFM_Time_Get; CTR-5, COM-6). ReferenceModels.AsOf is a test override. Fixed: ImplementationURI of the 7 Basic AIM AMDs pointed at a repository that does not exist (mpai-cui), now mpai-cav like the other AMDs.
- TO DECIDE (author): all 9 CUI AIMs (L2 schemas, inherited from V2.0, and L3 AMDs) declare APIProfile "Secure". The Secure Profile needs hardware roots of trust, attestation of the Controller, Secure Storage and the Security API; the reference Controller does not implement it (AIF Reference Software: "hardware root of trust: not implemented"). The AIMs of the other V3.0 standards declare Basic. Keep Secure (company financial data) or Basic?
- Qualifiers (author, 2026-11-02): a Qualifier is a facility, not an obligation; not all MPAI Data Types are Objects. The CUI Data Types having none is not a gap (my earlier reading of AIM-3 was too strong). Observed, not changed: the V2.1 web pages have acronym slips (CAP/CPA, CDP/PFP, PDP/PCP; PRP inputs differ between Table 3 and the function table; "Secondary Business Discontinuity Probability" vs "Secondary Discontinuity Probability").
- Not yet done: recorded Test Module + expected results per Data Type and Port Number (AIF Conformance 5.1), AccessSources for the trained model (MET-7..10, Terms with the CC BY attribution), Model_Update/Model_Drift for the ML AIMs.

## 2026-11-02 - Controller API: conformance of what is implemented with Basic API (author: not every call must exist, what exists must conform)
Read the whole Basic API text against AIF/ControllerApi + AIF/Controller (IControllerApi, IAimPorts, ContinuousExecutor.Ports, PayloadStore). The API is "a definition for any programming language", its types are opaque (2.1), and 4.1 says an Implementation in which the AIM reads/writes its own Ports through handles conforms, and that the functions of 4.3 and 4.5 (Register, Channel) need not be exposed where the Controller builds the Module from its Metadata.
- Conforms: the outcomes (AifError has all of Table 2 but BUFFER_TOO_SMALL, which a string API does not need); UA Input_Write/Output_Read (NOT_STARTED, NO_SUCH_PORT, TYPE_NOT_ACCEPTED, NOT_PRODUCED, TIMEOUT; 0 = do not block, negative = wait without limit; the first read after writes runs the Module); Pause/Resume/Stop/AIM_Stop/GetStatus; the AIM's Ports by Data Type and Port Number; the Controller stamps, the writer supplies none; Dropped counts dropped + discarded; Time; Report; Payload_Put on the Channel path.
- DEVIATIONS FOUND AND FIXED: (1) MPAI_AIFM_Payload_Get / _Release did not check that the caller is at the far end of the Channel (4.8: NOT_AUTHORISED otherwise; Communication COM-4): now an AIM that is not a reader of the Channel of the reference gets UnauthorizedAccessException (PayloadStore.IsAtFarEnd). (2) A payload was freed after as many Release calls as readers, so one reader releasing twice could free it for the others (4.8: when every reader has released it): a named reader now counts once (PayloadStore.Release(reference, reader)). Test/Mpai.Aif.Tests/PayloadAccessTests.cs (2 tests); ContinuousTests + RemoteTests 12/12.
- Not exposed, allowed by 4.1: AIM_Register_*, AIM_Deregister, Channel_Create/_Destroy. Not implemented as API calls (a gap against "exposes the Controller API", Conformance 1.1): Resource_Get/Set (UA and AIM faces), Port_Reset, Port_Probe, Message_* (our datum is a string: no buffer/copy), Model_Update/Model_Drift, AIM_Start/_Pause/_Resume requests to other AIMs, External_List/_Probe/_Output_Read/_Input_Write.
- Form (allowed): on the AIM face an outcome is an exception where the C API returns a code (NO_SUCH_PORT = InvalidOperationException, NOT_FOUND = KeyNotFoundException, NOT_AUTHORISED = UnauthorizedAccessException); TIMEOUT is null (read) or false (write).
- NOT yet checked against the spec text: SharedStorage_* and RuledStorage_* semantics, Record_Start/_Stop, Session_*, SharedStorage_Init/_Keep, External_Join/_Leave/_Authority.

## 2026-11-02 - Controller API: Storage, Record, Sessions checked against the spec (author: what is implemented must conform)
Reviewed (an independent read of the code against the Storage chapter and Basic API 3.5-3.7, 4.11.1, 4.11.2; the main findings re-read by me in the code) and fixed:
- STO-6: the record of the boundary (category Boundary) can be Put/Deleted by the Controller alone (RuledStore.Boundary); before, the User Agent or any AIM could add new keys to it, and the central control could overwrite/delete the Controller's entries. With a central control and no rule for Boundary the User Agent still reads the record (Storage 5). The synthetic-drive test stand-in now writes as the Controller (Test/Expected/synthetic-drive.json: the record's writer is .../Controller).
- Private Storage of an AIM is per Module and AIM: private/<Module>/<AIM> (an AIM name alone identifies nothing, 4.11); two Modules at one location no longer share an AIM's Private Storage (ContinuousTests.PrivateStorage lists private/<Module>/<AIM>).
- GetKeyInfo.RequestedBy is the client of the User Agent the Put was made for (the session Session_Serve names), stored by the Controller with the datum (StorageTrace.RequestedBy), not the reader's and not always "local" (Controller.ClientOf).
- The Module's Private Storage handle of an AIM follows Session_Serve (the served client's session), as the Shared Storage handle did.
- What lapsed with a session or a Module is deleted at once in every store, Private Storages too (RuledStore.SweepAll over all stores; called at Session_End and at MODULE_Stop): before, only Shared Storages, the rest on next access.
- A narrowing of the longest time by the central control applies at once to what was written (Expired reads the category's rule).
- A Put at an offset is atomic with respect to its key (read and write under the store lock).
- The boundary record: a Message counts once as not recorded however many items (payloads) it has; Take never throws into the Module (STO-7).
Tests: Test/Mpai.Aif.Tests/StorageConformanceTests.cs (5); the Controller/storage/access/drive tests re-run, all pass.
NOT CHANGED, TO DECIDE (author): (a) Keep: Basic API 3.5.2 says the deployment's time applies "where their writer does not say" (a default); the code and StorageUntilTests keep the earlier reading (a writer may keep a datum for less, not for more: a cap). (b) Put by another writer: a datum another wrote is not overwritten (except Shared Storage written for every holder, without central control); the spec says concurrent Puts to one key resolve last-writer-wins. (c) Init: control_MODULE naming ANOTHER Module is a bool for this Module; Init of an unknown Module is NotFound, not NOT_STARTED; List returns an empty list where the spec has NOT_STARTED (no scope); the User Agent's root counts as a scope. (d) AIMs placed on a host (AimHostServer) get a plain FileSharedStorage in a host-local folder, not the Module's Shared Storage under the rules (Storage 2, STO-1/5): a gap. (e) Record Always: the totals are not returned at MODULE_Stop (they are in the stored header); the record starts before any Init at the root fallback, and a later Init splits it. (f) Fallback handles (no instance/session set) stamp with DateTime.UtcNow, and a Reader entry is an exact string (naming an AIM Instance where the rule names its Module is refused). (g) Two clients served by one UA on one Module overwrite each other's Session_Serve (the spec has one Controller Instance per client over MPAI-MAS). (h) Session_Serve/_End return nothing (no outcome).

## 2026-11-02 - Controller API: Access, External joins and Store API checked against the spec (author: what is implemented must conform)
Three independent reads of the code against the spec; main findings re-read in the code; fixed what is clear and local:
- EXTERNAL (Communication 9/10, Zero Trust, Basic API 3.9): (1) a Message from another Controller is signed BOUND to the Port it is for and the stamp of its writer (DataXMData.Security.Transmission.Binding = DataType#Port@stamp, in what is signed; ExternalSigning.Sign/Verify binding, Outcome.NotBound): before, the stamp, the Data Type and the Port Number travelled in the frame outside the signature. Delegates Sign/Verify/SignPayloads of ExternalHub gained the binding (CityTrust, ExternalMember, ControllerApi, tests). (2) Freshness and replay: a signed Message whose stamp is outside Options.Freshness (5 min) of the clock, or that was received before (same signature), is refused and counted. (3) A payload on the payload channel is fetched only by the Controllers the Message that offered it concerns (offered[reference].For): before, any admitted Controller could fetch any offered reference. (4) The Authority's refusals are signed and echo the nonce/challenge, and the member judges the proof of the anchor BEFORE the refusal: an impostor's unsigned refusal is NOT_TRUSTED, the Authority's own is NOT_AUTHORISED (Basic API 3.9.1). Tests: ExternalBindingTests (2); External tests 13/13.
- ACCESS (Storage 6): (1) data already in Access under another Uri replaces what the pinned Uri holds when that is not the entry's SHA-256 (readers kept; a Source only moves forward); before, only a reader was added and the AIM read bytes that did not match its declared SHA-256. (2) An optional entry whose ValidWith partner is missing is not read by the AIM (AccessStore.Withdraw): before it was removed from the AIM's list but the data stayed readable. AccessConformanceTests (2); all 37 Access tests pass.
- STORE/CONTROLLER (Store API, Basic API 2.3): a refused AIM Licence (NOT_LICENSED), or Metadata/files that cannot be obtained, is now FAILED with its reason (ControllerApi.PrepareError); NOT_TRUSTED only where an Implementation or a model is not what the Store approved. 85 licence/store/trust tests pass.
NOT CHANGED, TO DECIDE / DO (author): EXTERNAL: credential and anchor validity are checked when a link opens, not at each Message or at expiry (Zero Trust 'no caching of trust'; no renewal); a joiner chooses its controllerID and a left id can be taken again (Basic API 5: not reused in a run); Hello claims an id the link does not prove; External trust decisions are not written to the signed hash-chained Trace; External_List/_Probe/_Output_Read/_Input_Write are not API calls (the hub moves the boundary Ports marked IsRemote); External_Join with rendezvous NULL (announcement in range) is refused FAILED; Join returns OK after a 20 s wait even if no Controller linked; incoming frames are delivered each in its own task (order not guaranteed), the Port's Timeout on a full Port is ignored at the hub and not counted. ACCESS: set items already held are not re-checked against a current manifest; Remove/Delete drops an item's history and the Version restarts; a Provider needs no credential for data supply and admission (AccessTrust) is off by default; Access_Version and List do not return NOT_AUTHORISED; the checks for an AIM apart from the Controller are split and the AIM identity is the host's word; Access_Open for a remote AIM reads into memory; Access_Path file is not read-only; MET-8 is checked by the Store tool, not at Module start; Model_Update/Model_Drift do not exist. STORE: the in-process MpaiStore/LocalStoreService (legacy path) overwrites with replace, does not refuse a missing Sub-AIM, authenticates nobody (the REST Service conforms); fingerprints are kept per AIM name, not per version; no quote of the marked-up price before issuing the licence (STR-9); Grant limits (MaxControllers, MaxConcurrentModules, ExcludedPurposes, ProvideService, Obligations) and Updates are not enforced; a Controller without Trust/approvals configured runs unverified code (fails open); only <BinaryName>.dll is measured, not the other assemblies of a package; the repository is not re-verified with the Store at use; the Store's key is fetched from the Store itself; HTTPS is not enforced; the mark-up covers four Payment shapes; an Implementer ID is assigned before MPAI approves.
Two long tests fail and are not caused by today's changes (disabling each of freshness/replay, binding and the payload restriction in turn did not change them): RemoteEndToEndTests.Step5TwoCavs (collision) and CaoTests.Step4OneModule; probably the CAV changes of the last days (recorded expectations not updated).

## 2026-11-02 - Trust over time (author: fix these first)
- A link lasts until the credential of the Controller it admitted expires (Communication 9; Zero Trust 2.7 no caching of trust): TrustProtocol.StillTrusted(keyId) re-checks the admitted anchor's validity and, where it was admitted by a credential, the credential's, now; ExternalTrust/CityTrust.Verify refuse a Message from a Controller no longer trusted; the hub asks StillTrusted of every link about once a second (ExternalHub.Options.StillTrusted) and CLOSES a link whose Controller is no longer trusted (ExternalHub.ClosedOnExpiry; the payload channel with it). Wired for a Controller that joined (ExternalJoin) and for the Authority's own hub. No renewal: the spec has none; a Controller whose credential lapsed joins again (External_Join).
- Every trust decision of the External path is a signed, hash-chained Trace record (Zero Trust 2.9): new Trust/TrustOperations.cs (PTF Trust Operations + TrustTrace for a party without a TrustDomain); ExternalTrust.Trace (link checks through the Trust Protocol, Messages and payloads refused, links closed: VerifyMessage, ServePayload, VerifyPayload, CloseLink, AcceptLink), ExternalAuthorityService.Trace (AdmitController / RefuseController with why). TrustTrace.Verify now reads the sequence of records made in memory (it threw on them).
- A controllerID is not reused for another Controller within a run (Basic API 5): the Authority remembers the key an id was admitted with; the Controller that left comes back with its own key, another is refused ('was admitted before to another Controller'). The id a Hello gives is the one the link was admitted for (a Controller cannot take another's link).
Tests: ExternalAuthorityTests +2 (credential expiry closes the link and the Trace; id reuse), External 15/15, Trust/Trace tests pass.
Still open on External (see the previous entry): External_List/_Probe/_Output_Read/_Input_Write, Join by announcement in range, Join returning OK after 20 s with no Controller linked, delivery order and Port timeout at the hub, the Authority's anchor validity at Join, attestation of Controllers to each other at link opening.

## 2026-11-02 - Access items, then the External items (author: go on)
- ACCESS (Storage 6, STO-8..11): (1) what left Access is not forgotten: new ledger retired.json (AccessStore) keeps per Source the last Version and per item the SHA-256 of everything it held; a Source made again goes on from its Version, and data with a retired hash is refused (a step back past a deletion); (2) the file given by Access_Path is read-only (made writable only to be replaced or deleted); (3) PutFile always has the file's SHA-256; PutFrom keeps where the original was obtained; (4) an item of a set is read against the manifest as it is now: if its SHA-256 is not the one the manifest names it is obtained again, and if that fails it is not given (AccessFetcher.CheckItem, used by BoundAccess.Get/Open/LocalPath).
  Tests: AccessLedgerTests +3; Access tests 55/55.
- EXTERNAL (Basic API 3.9.1, 5; Communication 9): (1) Join with rendezvous NULL = by announcement in range: the Authority announces where it admits (UDP multicast, port ControllerApi.ExternalAnnouncementPort, default 41041; 0 = no announcing); a Controller that joins listens for the Authority of the anchor (ExternalAnnouncementWait, 10 s) and is admitted as at a rendezvous - the Authority must still prove it holds the anchor (NOT_TRUSTED otherwise); none heard = FAILED with the reason. (2) MPAI_AIFM_External_List/_Probe/_Output_Read/_Input_Write: IAimPorts.ExternalList/ExternalProbeAsync/ExternalOutputReadAsync/ExternalInputWriteAsync (default interface methods; an AIM without an External Port gets NOT_AUTHORISED); ExternalHub implements IExternalPorts: controllerID = int per link, from the count of the run (never reused), valid until the link closes; the Controllers exchange the Metadata of their Modules after the link opens (returned by List); Read/Write/Probe are requests on the link, the Controller of the Module serving them answers only for an AIM of the Module and an External Port of it (NO_SUCH_PORT otherwise); what is read is signed by the Controller that wrote it with its stamp (verified, fresh, taken once); what is written is verified at the other end and inlined (COM-7). Mode: ControllerApi.ExternalPull=true makes the Module's External Output Ports wait to be read (no push to the Controllers linked); in push mode (default) a remote Read is REFUSED. (3) Messages of another Controller that no External Input Port takes are counted (ControllerApi.ExternalDropped, COM-8).
  Limits to decide: Port identified by AIM+Data Type+Port Number only, so for an Input and an Output Port with the same Data Type and number Probe answers for the Input; External_List returns Metadata but not role; no first-read/link close handling beyond FAILED.
  Tests: ExternalAuthorityTests +2 (announcement join; AIM list/probe/write/read), External/Continuous/Trust/Channel/Payload 59/59.
- NOT DONE: Provider credential role check (the Trust Protocol defines no Provider role); Join waits up to 20 s for a first link (kept: only a wait); hub delivery order across ports.

## 2026-11-02 - Store items (author: go on with the Store items)
- STR-9: the price with the mark-up is made known before the AIM Licence is issued. New route GET /MPAI/Store/L3/{id}/quote (Payment with the mark-up, signed by the Store); AimLicences.Quote/PricesOf; IAimSource.Quote (HttpAimSource, StoreAimSource); AimRepository.AcceptPrice (the Entity decides before the licence is asked for) and the AIM Licence must state the price quoted (else NOT_LICENSED).
- STR-3/Verification: fingerprints are kept per version of the Metadata (ImplementationFingerprints Versions; the Service records them under the version it publishes; POST .../verify takes an optional Version and signs it in the answer; HttpAimSource asks for the version it obtained). A new version no longer takes away the approval of the one a Controller holds.
- Store API 5: HttpAimSource refuses http: unless the Store is on this machine (loopback). It can be given the Store keys the Controller knows (storeKeys), and then never fetches a key from the Store itself.
- WKF-5: GET /MPAI/Store/Implementer/{id} says assigned=true only when MPAI approved the Implementer.
- Grant.MaxControllers: the Store refuses a licence for one Controller more than the offer allows under one Trust Anchor (403).
- Legacy in-process MpaiStore: a replaced version is kept under versions/ (STR-2); a Sub-AIM that is in neither the submission nor the Store refuses (submittedWith).
- FIX of mine (trust over time): a Controller that joins a service again (Leave, or credential lapsed) had a new key and the Authority refused it as 'admitted before to another Controller'; ControllerApi now keeps the key of each identity it joins under.
Tests: AimLicenceRepositoryTests +5, StoreLicenceServiceTests +1; Store/AIH/licence/repository tests pass except AihFirstSliceTests.TheFirstSlice (flaky: links leave and re-link, passes about one run in two, with or without the announcement/Metadata/trust-expiry additions).
NOT DONE (to decide): fail-open without Trust/approvals configured (a Controller then runs unverified code; making it fail closed breaks every local use); Grant.MaxConcurrentModules, ExcludedPurposes, ProvideService, Obligations, Updates; the repository is not re-verified with the Store at each build; only <BinaryName>.dll is measured (the spec measures the file the Metadata names); mark-up covers four Payment shapes (the rule is the Store's, outside AIF).
