## 2026-11-02 (night) - CUI-CPP V2.1 software: the trained financial model as Access data (step 4)
- AIMs/CUI/V2.1/PdModel.cs (new): the financial model of the Prediction AIMs as a class (logistic, ratios standardised and clamped to 3 scales); PdModel.Reference = the built-in model, a trained one is a file (Format MPAI-CUI-LOGISTIC-1: Intercept, Features Name/Mean/Scale/Weight, Training) read from the setting FinancialModel; a file that is not a model is refused with the reason. Primary Default, Organisation Model and Primary Discontinuity Prediction use it (CuiProvider passes it); without the file they use the reference model.
- AIMs/CUI/Tools/Mpai.Cui.Train (new, a tool of the Implementer, not an AIM): trains the model from Test/Data/CUI/Bankruptcy/Polish/5year.arff (ratios 5 years in, bankrupt a year later = an annual probability): the ten ratios of the AIM computed from the dataset's X1..X64 (X4, X46, X49, X7, X1/X10, X42, X2/X9, X2-X51, X27, X42/X27); winsorised 1-99%, standardised, L2 logistic (Newton); a ratio whose weight would go the wrong way (more profit, more risk) is left out and the rest refitted. Result: QuickRatio, ROA, ROE, ROS, DebtOnSales kept; held-out (30%) AUC 0.7635 against 0.7389 of the reference model, Brier 0.058 against 0.073. First fit without the direction rule had AUC 0.817 but weights the wrong way (EBITDAMargin +1.4, ROS -1.8): not explainable, not used.
- AIMs/CUI/V2.1/Models/FinancialDefault-Polish5y.json (new, 2.3 KB, SHA-256 A93DABFA76192E488F985B0F6D0A0FB79A567048A08DF3F26BDEC9ED7907BBEE): carries the CC BY 4.0 attribution (Tomczak 2016, doi 10.24432/C5F600), the method, the held-out figures and the caution (Poland, calibrated to the sample, not for real companies).
- L3 1CUI-CDP-, 1CUI-OMA-, 1CUI-PDP-V2.1-I01: AccessSources entry financial-default-model (Uri raw.githubusercontent.com/.../main/AIMs/CUI/V2.1/Models/FinancialDefault-Polish5y.json - exists only after this branch is merged to main; Sha256 as above; Setting FinancialModel; IsOptional true; Terms CC-BY-4.0). The L2 schemas are unchanged (AccessSources is an L3 field), so no SHA pin changes.
- Tests (Mpai.Cui.Tests 15/15, 4 new): the file and its training record; files that are not models refused; trained model gives another probability and explains it with the ratios kept; the Module run by the Controller reads the model it obtained as Access data from a file: URI, and a model that is not the bytes the Metadata pins is refused and the AIM goes on with the reference model. The other CUI tests no longer touch the network (AMD copy with a local Uri; MPAI_ACCESS in a temp folder).
- Still open on CUI-CPP: the console User Agent (step 2); synthetic test companies with recorded expected results (step 3); Model_Update for a new version of the model; the CUI web pages do not mention the model; the draft pages say 'no trained model' (check).
- CUI-CPP V2.1, step 2 (the console User Agent), 2026-11-02 night: Apps/CUI/CPP = CUI-CPP.orch (the workflow: stream Horizon (number), Financial CUI-FND, Governance CUI-GVS, Climate CUI-LRD, Cyber CUI-CRD, SecondaryRisks CUI-SRS from devices; deliver Organisation CUI-ORD, PrimaryDefault CUI-PDD, PrimaryDiscontinuity CUI-PSD, SecondaryProbability number to device Report; all by Data Type, none by Port name), Controller/CuiController (a program of its own, loopback, --model), Ua/CuiUa (console: company file in, report out, --json), Samples/healthy.json and distressed.json (the test companies), Cui.bat, README. Mpai.Aif.PortData: JsonObjectCodec for the nine CUI Data Types, new NumberCodec ('number', a Basic type), schema files of the nine in PortDataSchema (the Governance Descriptors carry the Header of the Governance Statements). Tests: Mpai.Cui.Tests CompanyAppTests (4: samples = the tests' companies; the App as two processes gives the distressed company a probability equal to the AIM's with the model obtained as Access data and the healthy one a lower one; an incomplete company file is refused by the UA). Mpai.Cui.Tests 19/19. Example (model on): distressed company default 16.7 %, healthy 2.9 %.
- CUI-CPP V2.1, step 3 (synthetic test companies, conformance), 2026-11-02 night: Test/Mpai.Cui.Tests/SyntheticCompanies.cs (16 invented companies, each with a purpose: average, blue-chip, healthy-small, leveraged, loss-making, distressed, negative-equity, startup, family-owned, zero-revenue, partial-financials, absurd-figures, climate-exposed, cyber-attacked, all-risks, distressed-all-risks), corpus Test/Data/CUI/Companies/*.json (the files the generator makes; a test says so), recorded results Test/Expected/cui-companies.json (reference and trained model: default, organisation impact, discontinuity, secondary, the descriptors kept), ConformanceTests (10): data conform to their schemas; recorded results; probabilities in [0,1], discontinuity >= default, secondary >= default, importances add up to 100; a longer horizon never lowers default or discontinuity (6/12/24/60 months); a worse company never less likely (blue-chip <= average <= leveraged <= distressed); what is not given is left out, risks not given are not in the discontinuity; an AIM that cannot compute says so and gives nothing; the Module (by the Controller, both models) gives what the AIMs give and its outputs validate against their schemas; an AIM computes when its required inputs have come and not before. Mpai.Cui.Tests 29/29 (about 1.5 min).
- FINDINGS of the conformance run (CUI AIMs): (1) a return on equity with equity <= 0 had no meaning (a loss over a negative equity looked like a profit): ROE is now left out when the equity is not above zero (Prediction.cs). (2) An AIM that gets an optional Input after its first computation computes again, so the Module gives more than one PSD/ORD in turn: the User Agent (CuiUa) now waits until nothing has come for 1.5 s and keeps the last (before, it could print the first, without the Cyber/Climate entries). (3) The trained model barely separates the middle (average 6.3 %, blue-chip 2.2 %, distressed 16.7 %): five ratios, a sample of Polish companies; the reference model spreads more (0.02 % to 73 %). (4) The Cyber Risk Descriptors schema patterns for IP addresses match nothing (told before). (5) Financial Descriptors in the corpus are shaped as the schema says (AQ is an array of questionnaire records).

## 2026-11-03 (night) - the flakiness of AihFirstSliceTests.TheFirstSlice: cause and fix; Access ledger corrected; Model_Update / Model_Drift
- CAUSE (found with temporary logging of the hubs): THREAD POOL STARVATION, not the protocol. Every call that waits (OutputRead / InputWrite with a timeout, the workflows of the User Agents of the process) holds a pool thread; the pool adds threads slowly; the announcements the Authority relays every 250 ms reached the Controllers 10-22 s late (20-34 work items waiting), so the hubs found the others 'not heard for more than 3 s', closed their links ('left') and the Back End had nobody to answer (no answer to the registration; no new NN Model; no AIH Data for the audit). FIX: ControllerApi's static constructor raises the minimum of the pool's worker threads to 128 (ControllerApi.cs). No change to signing, freshness, binding or trust checks, nor to ExternalHub (its 'left' message now says how long the Controller was not heard). Result: TheFirstSlice 18 s a run (was 1-2 min) and 7 runs in a row (14 cases) passed; no 'left' events.
- Wide regression (External, Continuous, Trust, Channel, Payload, Store, AIH, Access, Licence, Repository): 313/314; the one failure was MY Access ledger (2026-11-02): a Source that the Controller removed because no Module declared it, declared again with the same bytes, was REFUSED (the ledger retired the latest content too), so a Module could not start again with a model it had run with. FIX: the ledger retires what an item held BEFORE its latest content (Earlier); the state a Source was left in is not an earlier one and is obtained again. The Version still only rises (a Source made again goes on from the Version it had): AccessProcessTests now expects 2 where it expected 1. Access tests 62/62.
- MPAI_AIFM_Model_Update / _Model_Drift (Basic API 4.12) - the CUI model update (2026-11-03): IAimPorts.ModelUpdateAsync(modelName) and ModelDrift(aimName) (default interface methods), ContinuousExecutor.Models/Drift, UserAgent (reads the Metadata again, AimHost.ModelUpdate) and ControllerApi.ModelUpdate/ModelDrift for the Controller's own callers; BoundAccess.Rebind; IModelDrift. Update: the URI names a model of the AIM's AccessSources AS THE METADATA THE CONTROLLER HOLDS NOW states it; the Controller obtains what it pins (a new SHA-256 for the same URI is a new Version of the Source), checked against that SHA-256 before it is kept; NOT_AUTHORISED if the Metadata names no model at that URI, NOT_TRUSTED if the bytes are not the pinned ones or the Source held them before, FAILED if not obtainable, NOT_STARTED / NOT_FOUND. A NEW URI is a new version of the Metadata and takes effect when the Module is started again (Store, WKF-11). CUI: ModelSource (the AIM asks at most once a minute of the Controller's time, takes the new model from Access between two computations, keeps the old one if the new is refused) and drift (share of the last 200 ratios beyond 3 scales of the model's means). Tests: Mpai.Cui.Tests ModelUpdateTests (3). NOT in the spec text: how the AIM learns of a new model; the interpretation above is mine - to confirm.

## 2026-11-05 (night) - PGM-AUA first slice
- NEW SOFTWARE (not a standard change): AIMs\PGM\V1.0 (assembly Mpai.Pgm): AUC, CXT, PRC, BKN, DAC, USR, PAL, AUF (composite) and its Sub-AIMs PSD, MOG, EFD, EBD, each against the Ports of its L3 (tests PgmSliceTests: ports = L3 ports; every Data valid against its schema). CXT, PRC, DAC, USR, PAL and AUF are composites in the Metadata and are implemented as one AIM each (an AIM is an AIM). Plan: MKG\first-slice-plan.md.
- CODE CHANGE in an existing AIM: PAF-PDX (AIMs\PAF\V1.6\PDX\PdxAimProcessor.cs) now gives the Face and Body Descriptors (PAF-FDO, PAF-BDO) of the Portable Avatar on the two Ports its L3 declares; it declared them and gave neither. Reason: the A-User Module needs them for UNT-3OD. (PDX still drops the Speech Object when the Portable Avatar also carries a Text Object: right for the Videoconference receiver, wrong for an A-User whose Portable Avatar carries both - the slice's Portable Avatar carries speech only. To decide.)
- SCHEMA FIX (clear, local, NOT uploaded): schemas\MMM4\V2.2\data\ProcessAction.json (MMM-PAC-V2.2): the three alternatives of the items of PAStatus (Ack, Request, Error) had no "required", so every object matched all three and no non-empty PAStatus could ever validate (oneOf). Each alternative now requires its property. Upload with the next MMM4 set. Still open there: the array-level "required": [Ack, Request, Error] on PAStatus is meaningless (required applies to objects).
- NOT CHANGED, TO DECIDE (found by the slice, details in the report): (1) the L3 of the A-User Module has no "Execution": "Continuous" (the schema AutonomousUser.json has it; the generated AMD lost it) and the Module has loops; (2) no Port of any PGM AIM is optional, so under the Continuous executor A-User Control (12 Inputs) can never fire; (3) the AMDs list Sub-AIMs for composites, so the Controller wants a provider for every Sub-AIM (e.g. 1PGM-PDA inside DAC); (4) Context Descriptors (PGM-CXD) has no property for the recognised text; (5) Instruction codes of the AUC-XXX Requests are not defined in the schema; (6) no source of MMC-EST for USR in the Topology of the Module; AUF's AvatarModel and SpeechModel Inputs are not connected to the boundary; (7) SimpleTime schema requires AccuracyPlusMinus when AccuracyMode is absent (so {FlagsByte, StartTime, EndTime} is not valid).

## 2026-11-06 Objects at the Ports (author's rule: between AIMs flow OBJECTS - Data + Qualifier in the form of the schema; inside an AIM only the data) - software only, no schema/web change
- Found by the TMA end-to-end run: AUS, FIR, SIR, ASR (and 9 more AIMs) read the Object at the Port in the repository's old flat form (Data at the top level) and not in the form of the schema (BasicSpeechObjectData / BasicVisualObjectData, the Qualifier by the schema's names); with the schema's form they found "no data".
- New AIF\PortData\PortObjects.cs: ReadSpeech / ReadVisual / ReadSpeechObject (tolerant: the schema's form, and the old flat form still understood) and WriteSpeech / WriteVisual / WriteSpeechObject (always the schema's form). The classes (BasicSpeechObject, ...) are what the AIM works on after the Port.
- Codecs (BasicSpeechObjectCodec, BasicVisualObjectCodec): ToWire/ToInternal pass an Object already in the target form (no data lost).
- Readers changed to PortObjects.Read*: AUS, ASR, SIR/SPE (SpeechInputs moved from Core\OSD to PortData), FIR, ESD, OCR, TIQ, BVS, VII, EFD, FPE, GBD, GFD. Writers changed: AUS (Speech Object with its Basic Speech Objects as Objects, SpeechObjectSpaceTime added), BVS (Scene with its Basic Visual Objects as Objects; MInstanceID and BVSDescriptorsSpaceTime added: the schema requires them).
- NOT yet changed (still write the old flat form; their consumers read both): TTS, SOE, ASI, SpeechStore, and the clients that read outputs (RcaShell, Desktop). QualifierCheck (Controller observer) looks at the flat form only: with the schema's form it checks nothing.
- My own faults found on the way: FBO read the Speech Object's entries as Basic Speech Objects (they hold them as BSObjectIDOrBSObject); the Text key BasicTextObjectData does not exist (BasicTextData): AuxiliaryText.Words and ASR's context read the wrong key, so the Auxiliary Text never reached them.
- Test material: Test\Data\Images\lfw pictures are 94x125 and SCRFD finds no face in them; the TMA test puts each on a 640x480 frame with a backdrop of its own per person (a change of speaker = a change of shot).
- RESULT: TmaEndToEndTests.TheThreeSpeakers PASSES (11 scenes, 5 of speech, 6 of pictures; MEV valid; the 6 faces named right in order; 3 voices right of 5 turns + 1 unrecognised; texts real). OPEN: Identity Reconciliation names only the first person (it is built for one face + one voice together, not for a programme in which they arrive apart); one voice (overlapped turn) unrecognised.

## 2026-11-07 (night): XRV-LTP show control (M3289 row 5)

- Schemas XRV1/V1.0/data: REOperatorStatus.json and VEOperatorStatus.json get an optional ShowControl (Go, Hold, Skip, Back) inside REOperatorStatus / VEOperatorStatus. Copies in ALL-to-upload/XRV1/V1.0/data.
- Web (published): RE Operator Status 25208 and VE Operator Status 25210 (a paragraph in Functional Requirements and a ShowControl row in the semantics table); Action Generation 18451 (a paragraph in Functions). The show is in one of four states: not started, running, held, ended; a Cue whose Conditions are never met waits for a Go or a Skip; a Hold of the show is not a Pause of the Module; a show with no operator runs from the first to the last Cue. The wording of Go, Hold, Skip and Back is mine, for the author to read.

## 2026-11-08 night - XRV-LTP V1.0 Cue engine (S1 Common, S2 ACG/PFI/PTI/OSI): PROPOSALS ONLY (no schema or web page changed)
- Software added (nothing committed): D:\AI\AIMs\XRV1\V1.0 (Common, ACG, PFI, PTI, OSI, Provider); L3s 1XRV-ACG-V1.0-I01, 1XRV-PFI-V1.0-I01, 1XRV-PTI-V1.0-I01, 1XRV-OSI-V1.0-I01 in AIMs\AMDs (new files); mini show in Test\Data\XRV\MiniShow; tests XrvCommonTests, XrvCueEngineTests; Test\Expected conformance.json and l3-load.json: four new "conforms"/"loads" lines.
- PROPOSAL 1 (gap in the schemas): the ShowControl (Go, Hold, Skip, Back) is in the RE/VE Operator Status but the RE/VE Operator Descriptors have no element that carries it, so the Operator Status Interpretation has nothing to read it from. Tonight's reading: a Control Object (XRV-CTO-V1.0) of the Show Console whose ControlData is Go, Hold, Skip or Back. Proposal: either add an optional ShowControl to the Operator Descriptors (same enum as the Status) or state on the OSI web page that the Show Console Control Data carries it.
- PROPOSAL 2 (text to write on the pages): the rule by which an Interpretation AIM "matches" is not written. Tonight's reading: every element that the target Status of the Cue names is in the current Status and equal (nested objects: the same rule inside; arrays: each element of the target has an equal element; the identity of a nested Object - Header, its own ID, its Time - is not compared). Proposal: write it on the Action Generation and the three Interpretation pages (it is the G1 sentence).
- PROPOSAL 3 (text to write): Cue Conditions that name Descriptors (RECueConditions / VECueConditions allow REPerformanceDescriptors ... in the schema of the Cue List) have no stated meaning; they are not evaluated tonight (a group with such a Condition is never met, and Action Generation reports it). Proposal: state that Conditions are on Statuses only, or define the comparison.
- PROPOSAL 4 (text to write): who has an operator. Tonight: the show has an operator when the Script lists at least one Operators/Consoles entry; with none the show starts by itself at the first Cue. Proposal: write it on the Action Generation page.
- NOTE (L2): in the L2 of PFI, PTI and OSI the Participant Data input is not optional (no IsOptional), while the decision of 2026-11-07 says the AIMs only receive it and do nothing with it; the L3s repeat the L2 (required). Consider IsOptional: true on it in the three L2s if the AIMs are to run before any Participant Data exists (the continuous executor fires an AIM that is only an IAimProcessor when every required input has a Message; the XRV AIMs are IAimRunners and do not wait for it).

## 2026-11-08 night - AIH-HSP V1.1: who starts the functions; Ports of de-identification, anonymisation and audit (PROPOSAL, nothing applied)
- Finding (L2 of AIH1 V1.1, not edited): (a) HealthFrontEnd.json has no Port for the De-ID and Anonym or the Audit Request/Response, so a Health Front End cannot start them Controller to Controller; (b) HealthBackEnd.json has IsRemote "" on AuditRequestEN, AuditResponseNE, DeIDAndAnonymRequestEN, DeIDAndAnonymResponseNE, AIHDataDeIDEN/NE (AIH-AHD:1/2), AIHDataAuditEN, AIHDataProcessingEN: L2Conformance reads "" as "not remote", so an L3 cannot make them External.
- Proposal, Health Front End: add Input AIH-DIA-V1.1 (response, IsRemote true), Output AIH-DIA-V1.1 (request, IsRemote true), Input AIH-ADT-V1.1 (response, IsRemote true), Output AIH-ADT-V1.1 (request, IsRemote true), each Optional; topology: boundary to boundary (the Front End's HDP is not involved), or through HDP like AIH-AHP.
- Proposal, Health Back End: IsRemote true on AuditRequestEN, AuditResponseNE, DeIDAndAnonymRequestEN, DeIDAndAnonymResponseNE. The Back End's own questions to AIH Data Processing, and the AIH Data handed to Auditing and De-Identification, still need a route that does not go through a User Agent: a Topology line from AIH-HDP-V1.1 to AIH-ADT-V1.1 and AIH-DIA-V1.1 (AIH-AHD-V1.1) inside the Back End would remove AIHDataAuditEN, AIHDataDeIDEN, AIHDataProcessingEN and AIHDataDeIDNE from the boundary.
- Then: the L3 1AIH-HBE and 1AIH-HFE, HBE.orch, HFE.orch, HbeUserAgent (DeIdentify, Audit relay removed), the service policy (Correlate already names DeIDAndAnonymID and AuditID).
- Web, AIH-HSP V1.1: the AI Modules table (Table 1) lists no De-Identification and Anonymisation AIM (AIH-DIA) although the Data Types and the Tokens (service DIA) name it; the Architecture page does not say who starts de-identification, audit and processing (the text of 2026-11-07 does); the Register page says a Third-Party User registers "directly" with the Back End, the architecture text and the Controllers say through a Health Front End.
- Done in software (no schema change): Back End starts an audit and a processing itself (HbeUserAgent.AuditOf/ProcessData; HDP answers the Back End's own Auditing and AIHDataProcessing requests); TPU.orch and TpuUserAgent (Test/Expected/workflows.json has the new line).

## 2026-11-08 - CAE-ARP / Tape Audio Restoration (CAE-TAR): round trip on music
New test file Test\Mpai.Aif.Tests\CaeUscV25TapeRoundTripTests.cs (10 tests, pass; with CaeUscV25TarTests 18). Music of Test\Data\Music, an equalisation of another standard (IEC2 written, IEC1 read, 7.5 ips) and a speed of twice (written 7.5 ips, read 15 ips) are simulated and restored through the Tape Audio Restoration AIM. The classifier looks at four seconds of the Audio File of the irregularity centred on its middle and answers with one of: da, di, pps, ssv, esv, sb, wf, or none (the irregularity is not selected). Where the classifier is not sure, or has not been given, the AIM classifies by the definitions of the types." Also: the types the audio can show are da, di, pps, ssv, esv, sb and wf; sp, b, sot, eot, m and s only the film shows.
- OPEN POINTS FOR THE AUTHOR: (1) the model is trained on SIMULATED impairments of music; it has never heard a real tape. (2) The standard has no code for noise: bursts of noise are given di (dirt) - a choice. (3) The L3 names a file: URI of this machine; the model has to be published (https, same Sha256) to be obtained elsewhere. (4) The equalisation constants of Tape.cs (used by the simulator) are from memory of IEC 60094 and only the speeds 7.5 and 15 ips have two different curves.

ASE: AddedObjects naming a Full Audio Object places it part by part, each part where the Object's placement and its own put it (AseAim.Leaves; Mpai.Core.OSD.Placements.Within, moved from AoeAim); a Full Audio Scene (several sub-scenes, sub-scenes that are Full) arriving on the Scene Port is the FRAME the open Basic Scene is edited in: the Module gives it back whole, each Basic Scene it keeps as it now stands; the first Basic Scene it keeps (at any depth) is the one opened, another by OpenedScene. SOE: a Speech Object of several (named by AcquiredObject) is open; UserPoV and the Speech Qualifier of ChangedObjects act on each Basic Speech Object in it; a Spatial Attitude on it, and its translation, are refused with the reason (part by part). SSE: AddedObjects naming a Speech Object of several places it part by part (SpeechStore.Leaves); a Full Speech Scene arriving keeps every Basic Speech Scene given in full that the Module did not hold, opens the first, and is the frame given back whole. MSE: it already took Scenes and Objects of any medium, Basic or not, the same way (Full Audio Scene tested).
SCHEMA/L3 (PROPOSED, not changed): schemas\CAE3\V1.0\AIMs\AudioObjectEditing.json and AIMs\AMDs\1CAE-AOE-V1.0-I01.json, Description. Now: "...of an Audio Object its Name, Time, SpaceTime, UserPoV, ParentAudioObjectIDs, DescrMetadata and the SpaceTime of each child, named; of a Basic Audio Object its Name, Time, UserPoV, DeviceID, Level, PerceptStatus, AcousticProfile, AudioQualifier and DescrMetadata." Proposed: "...One Audio Object, Basic or Full, is open at a time... It changes the features of the Objects it keeps, each by a value (ChangedObjects): of an Audio Object (one or more Basic Audio Objects and Audio Objects; a Basic Audio Object is the Audio Object of one part) its Name, Time, SpaceTime, UserPoV, ParentAudioObjectIDs, DescrMetadata and the SpaceTime of each child, named; of a Basic Audio Object its Name, Time, UserPoV, DeviceID, Level, PerceptStatus, AcousticProfile, AudioQualifier and DescrMetadata. What the User Command modifies (ModifiedObjects: the Acoustic Profile, LUFS) is that of the Basic Audio Object, or of each Basic Audio Object an Audio Object holds." The "APIProfile: Basic" of the AIM schemas is the AIF profile and stays.
OPEN, FOUND (ASM): a Full Speech Object cannot be acquired by a User Command: ManagedObject (UserCommand.cs, the AcquiredObject) has AudioObject, BasicAudioObject, BasicSpeechObject and BasicVisualObject, no Speech Object or Visual Object of several, and SOE has no Speech Object Input Port in its L3 (only SSE has): a Speech Object of several reaches SOE by name, after SSE kept it. ## 2026-11-09 (night) - AIH-HFL federated learning demo (N-1 to N-4): number of cases per model, checks of a round
- PROPOSAL (schema, not changed): the Federated Learn (AIH-FDL-V1.1) Response and the ML Model Object (AIP-MLO-V1.0) have no field for the NUMBER OF CASES a post-learning model learned from, which AIH-HFL needs to average weighted by it. Proposed: an integer property "TrainingCases" (minimum 1) in the Response of FederatedLearn.json, next to AIHDataID (AIHDataID is one string, so it cannot stand for the several AIH Data a model learned from). Carried today (no schema change): the SECOND Data item of MLModelData holds the number as decimal text (HflAimProcessor.Cases); the first holds the weights (32-bit floats, little endian: the weights of the final layer, then the bias). A model without a second item counts as one case.
- SOFTWARE ONLY, no schema/L2/L3/Data Type/Port change: AIMs\AIH\V1.1\HFL\FrontEndTrainer.cs (a Front End trains the final layer of the End User's NN Model on the End User's own cases, in the End User's storage; frozen ONNX network below it), HflAimProcessor.cs (RoundPolicy: size, finite, distance from the start model; evaluation on a fixed set and a limit on the fall of accuracy / rise of loss; refusals recorded; average weighted by the number of cases; no use of any sender field).
- OPEN (standard): the standard names no size limit, distance limit or evaluation limit for a round: they are settings of the Back End in the software. If the standard should say that the Back End checks a model before averaging and validates the result, the AIH-HFL page should say so; nothing was written on the web.
- OPEN (standard): the HFL input Port takes a Federated Learn Response with the sender in EndUserID/MInstance/UEnvironment; the Back End's code does not read them (N-4). Whether the schema should carry them at all in a response to HFL is the author's.
- 2026-11-09 (night) N-6: the Model Licence (AIH-MDL-V1.1) and the Blockchain's contract are for AIH Data. For the NN Models of federated rounds the software uses them for a model VERSION (EndUserID = the Back End as owner, AIHDataID = the version id, AIHDataType 'NNModel', which is not a value of the schema). PROPOSAL: either a Model Licence for NN Models of its own (who may use a version, which round, which AIH Data fed it) or AIHDataType accepting a model. Code: AIMs\AIH\V1.1\Chain\ModelVersionRegister.cs.

## 2026-11-09 - CVE software: VDI and VOE built; ASM Face label (author: "develop what is missing")
- SOFTWARE (new, nothing committed): AIMs\CVE\V1.0\VDI (CveVdiAimProcessor, VdiPlugin) and AIMs\OSD\V1.5\VOE (OsdVoeAimProcessor, VoePlugin); tests Test\Mpai.Aif.Tests\CveVdiVoeTests.cs (9, pass, no model needed); the two projects added to the test project. The L3s 1CVE-VDI-V1.0-I01 and 1OSD-VOE-V1.5-I01 now list their implementation (Mpai.Cve.Vdi, Mpai.Osd.Voe). VDI: the Point of View is the line from the wrist through the tip of the index finger of the hand that reaches farther from its shoulder (BlazePose keypoints of PAF-EBD); VOE: the object of the Visual Scene Geometry in the cone of the Point of View, closest to its axis, in front of it.
- ASSUMED, NOT IN THE STANDARD (to confirm): (1) the Body Descriptors Object has no reference frame: its keypoints are taken to be in the frame of the Visual Scene Geometry (x right, y down, z away); (2) the Orientation of the Point of View (three Euler angles) is read as (azimuth, elevation, 0) in degrees; (3) the Aperture of the Point of View is the cone in which an object is crossed (10 degrees, twice the half-angle); (4) an object of the Visual Scene Geometry is a point (the Position), the schema gives no extent; (5) VDI takes the Visual Scene Geometry as a required input and does not use it; (6) a Port carries one Visual Object, so VOE keeps the Visual Objects it receives by identifier and says if the crossed one has not arrived.
- STILL MISSING: code for the composites VOI and VOB (their L3s list no implementation), DVI, and OSD-BMS (Basic Multimodal Scene Description: L2 exists, no L3, no code); no L3 for DVI and BMS until software exists.
- ASM Face (UserAgent page AIF\UserAgent\Assets\cav-webview.html): the page said "Loading the CAV's face" in every App; it now says "Loading the <name> Face" (?name=, AvatarUaHost.InitAsync(faceName:); ASM passes "ASM", the CAV HciApp "CAV", the others get "Loading the Face"). The page itself loads and shows the Face in a plain browser (tested 2026-11-09); in the ASM window it did not: AvatarUaHost.Diagnose (set by ASM to App.Log) now logs the page's navigation, failed requests, the Face file and page errors as "[avatar page]" lines in asm-ua.log. Cause not yet known: needs one run of ASM.

## 2026-11-09 - ASM: the message at the start (the refusal of loading a sound)
- Cause: the workflow's first request (offer Welcome in Apps\CAE\ASM\ASM-VCI.orch) was the whole welcome sentence, which contains "press Load a sound"; the grammar reads those words as a request to load a sound by voice and refuses it, so the first thing a person saw was "To load a sound, press the Load a sound button, and tell me what to call it."
- Change: the request is now "Hello."; the Application Context has an Ask for "hello" whose answer is the welcome (Grammar\asm-context.base.json -> Access\AsmApplicationContext.json, regenerated). The pin of the context changed again (now 2D92EC86...): AIMs\AMDs\1MMC-JSI-V2.5-I02.json, MODELS.md, THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md. Tests: AsmWelcomeTests; AsmGrammarTests pass. The Controller must be restarted to read the new context.

## 2026-11-09 - ASM: which materials are available (author: it would be useful to be able to ask)
- Asking "which materials are available", "what materials do you know", "list the materials", "show materials" (and a few like them) is answered with the list the rules are made from: compile.py generates the Ask from Materials of asm-grammar.json (no hand-written list). The Room acoustics panel shows the same list, with the absorption at 1 kHz, and the surfaces that can be set.
- Context pin changed again (now 8BC6EBF8...): AIMs\AMDs\1MMC-JSI-V2.5-I02.json, MODELS.md, THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md. Tests: AsmWelcomeTests (4 new cases), AsmGrammarTests pass. Controller to be restarted.

## 2026-11-09 - ASM: the walls by the compass (author: west-north-east-south is simpler)
- The walls can be named by the compass as well as left/right/front/back: west = Left (x smaller), east = Right (x bigger), north = Front (y bigger, ahead of the user), south = Back. Grammar\asm-grammar.json Faces got four names; the rules are generated for each (416 rules, was 348); the Conventions of the context say it. "set the west wall to carpet", "cover the north wall with foam", "the east wall is glass", "set the south wall absorption to 0.5". The Room acoustics panel names a wall "Left (west)" etc.
- Context pin changed again (now 9EFF9CF4...): the JSI L3, MODELS.md, THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md. Test\Expected\asm-grammar.json: 10 new lines (5 sentences, with and without a sound waiting). AsmGrammarTests and AsmWelcomeTests pass. Controller to be restarted.

## 2026-11-09 - HOW THE SCENE IS RENDERED (headphones, loudspeakers) and the sound output (author: do both; the layout is a property of the scene)
- STANDARD CHANGES (told at once; the author said to add what is needed): (1) OSD V1.5 data: BasicAudioSceneDescriptors.json and AudioSceneDescriptors.json get two properties, RenderingMode (Mono | Stereo | Multichannel | Binaural: how the Scene is rendered for the listener; absent, binaural) and ChannelCount (4, 6 or 8, for Multichannel); the values are those of RenderingMode of the RenderConfiguration of the Audio Qualifier (TFA-AUQ), less Ambisonics. (2) CAE3 V1.0 data: UserCommand.json, ModifiedScene gets the same two properties (a command changes how the open Scene is rendered). Reason: a Scene has no Qualifier, so the layout is a property of the Scene itself; it is the Module's (ASE) business because it changes the scene; the User Agent's renderer reads it from the scene.
- UPLOAD LIST (schemas site): OSD/V1.5/data/BasicAudioSceneDescriptors.json, OSD/V1.5/data/AudioSceneDescriptors.json, CAE3/V1.0/data/UserCommand.json. WEB, TO DO: the BAS and ASD data type pages (OSD V1.5) and the CAE-ASM User Command page; the description of ASE (it now also sets how the scene is rendered).
- PINS: UserCommand.json now B22BA231... (was EBDD0FB4...) and the Application Context E918FBC8...: the JSI L3 (1MMC-JSI-V2.5-I02: CommandSchema, ResponseSchema, ApplicationContext), MODELS.md, THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md. UserCommand.json was edited in place, only the ModifiedScene properties added.
- SOFTWARE: Core Basic/Audio Scene Descriptors and SceneChange get the properties; ASE (AseAim.SetBasicSceneRendering, Store and the copies keep it; the processor refuses a mode that is none of the four, and a Multichannel without 4, 6 or 8) and gives it on the ASD; AudioRendering.LayoutOf reads it (root, else the first sub-scene that states one) and the renderer uses it; the Loudspeaker Unit (AOD of the UA) has OutputDevices, DeviceNumberOf and PlayAsync(wav, device). Grammar: Renderings in asm-grammar.json, rendering() in compile.py: "play on headphones", "hear it on stereo speakers", "set the output to 5.1", "render for quad" ... (422 rules). ASM window: Sound output button and panel (layout: given to the Module as the command; output: the User Agent's, kept in asm-output.json beside the program, not told to the Module); typed: "play on <output>", "which sound outputs are there".
- TESTS: AsmRenderingModeTests (15), AsmGrammarTests (30 new lines in Test\Expected\asm-grammar.json: rename 6, compass 10, rendering 14), AsmWelcomeTests; VciTests.WhatIsSaidBecomesACommandAndTheResponseIsSaid fails with or without these changes. NOT TRIED: playing on a real second output, or on 5.1; the voice (Heard text goes through the Module: "play on headphones" by voice is the Module's, "play on <output>" by voice is not intercepted).

## 2026-11-09 - HMC V2.2 implementation: first slice (MDX and SID built; BMS, DVI, ECU, CEC, provider left)
- GAP ANALYSIS (HMC-CEC-V2.2 = SID, BMS, ECU, TST, EDP, TTT, RSR; HMC-ECU-V2.2 = MDX, ASR, DVI, AII, NLU, PSE, TTT). WITH L3 AND CODE: MMC-ASR, NLU, EDP, TTT (LeafAimsProvider, HciProvider), MMC-TST (composite of ASR, TTT, TTS), MMC-PSE (composite of SPE, FPE, PMX), PAF-RSR (Module), CAE-AII, CVE-VII, CVE-VDI, OSD-VOE (L3 of VDI and VOE list their implementation). WITHOUT CODE: OSD-BMS, CVE-DVI (needs BMS), and the composites HMC-ECU and HMC-CEC (no L3).
- DONE (nothing committed): AIMs\OSD\V1.5\MDX (Mpai.Osd.Mdx) and AIMs\HMC\V2.2\SID (Mpai.Hmc.Sid): they work on the JSON of the data by the Header of each element. MDX takes a Basic Multimodal Scene Descriptors apart (Space-Time, Speech, Text, Audio, Visual Object, Face Object = a Visual Object whose type is Face, Face and Body Descriptors, the first Basic Audio Scene, and the Audio and Visual Scene Geometry made from the scenes); what the scene does not hold is not produced. SID adds the Avatar of the Portable Avatar to the scene it conveys (or a generic one), at the Spatial Attitude the Portable Avatar states, else at the origin facing the listener (180), with the Avatar's Face and Body Descriptors. L3s 1OSD-MDX-V1.5-I01 and 1HMC-SID-V2.2-I01 (conform to their L2s, load; Test\Expected conformance.json and l3-load.json got the 4 lines). Tests: HmcMdxSidTests (8 pass).
- ASSUMED (to confirm): (1) MDX for a Multimodal Scene Descriptors (OSD-MSD) is not done: only OSD-BMS; (2) the L3 of MDX chooses the Basic types where the L2 allows two (BSO, BTO, BAO, BVO, BAS, BAG, BVG); (3) the avatar's descriptors are carried into the scene as Basic Objects (BasicObjectsData) so that MDX finds them; (4) the Speech Scene Geometry output of MDX is never produced (no speech scenes in OSD-BMS).
- LEFT: OSD-BMS (the L2 says it describes the scene from the Multimodal, Audio and Visual Objects and EXTRACTS the speech of the input, the speech of the participant, the Face Object, the Face and Body Descriptors: it would use the face detector of VSI, the pose model of EBD, and the face descriptors of EFD; open: what 'Input Speech' and 'Participant Speech' are); CVE-DVI (composite of BMS, VDI, VOE, VII); composite L3s of HMC-ECU and HMC-CEC; an HmcProvider (HciProvider is the model: it already creates AII, VII, ASR, NLU, EDP, ...) and an HMC Controller; an end-to-end test (fakes for the heavy AIMs, then the real ones at night).

- HMC, 2026-11-09 (later): the author corrected the Reference Model: a scene holds a PLURALITY of Speech, Audio and Visual Objects. OSD-MDX now gives every object of a kind, in the order it stands in the scene, one datum after the other on the Port of its kind (it is an IAimRunner as well as an IAimProcessor; as a processor it gives the first of each kind); the Visual Objects that are faces leave on Port 2, the others on Port 1; every Basic Audio Scene gives its Audio Scene Descriptors and its geometry. Tests: HmcMdxSidTests (10 pass). The L2 and L3 ports are unchanged (a Port carries a sequence of data, as in the rest of the software); the L2 descriptions of MDX and BMS and their web pages say Speech, Audio and Visual Object in the singular: to align if the Reference Model is the master (the deck on OneDrive, last saved 2026-11-06 13:31, already shows Visual Objects and Audio Objects in ECU). OSD-BMS will take many objects in and keep them all.

- CAV PLANNER, 2026-11-09 (author: 'look at the CAV planner collisions next'): three real faults found and fixed in AIMs/CAV/V2.0/Ams/Planning, planner tests 27 of 27 pass (junction 7 + random junction encounters 6 + highway 6 + the others; CAV tests 33 of 33): (1) SpaceTimePlanner.Check: the lane shortcut for traffic that keeps its lane took EVERY hypothesis of the object in the lane's frame, so the junction branches Road:Left and Road:Right of an oncoming vehicle were never checked against the CAV's path: the CAV accelerated to 33 m/s into a junction where a car timed to turn across it; the shortcut now leaves the branches (Road:* other than Road:Lane) to the check along their own line and reach (hit at 45.8 s before). (2) Prediction.TrackedObject.ObservedBraking (new) and the planner's reach of an object: an object may brake at least as hard as it has been SEEN to brake over the last 1.2 s (never less than ObjectBrake 3, never more than MaxBrake 8): the oncoming car slowed at ~5 m/s2 to time its turn, the planner took drivers to brake at 3. (3) the same lane shortcut skipped the FOLLOWER rule for a vehicle behind in the neighbouring lane (safe distance 0): the CAV changed lane in front of a car 32 m behind closing at 7 m/s and made it brake at 9 m/s2 (RandomTrafficOnTheHighway seeds 1 and 5); the follower deficit (the gap the driver wants for FollowerBrake 3) is now taken there too. Seed 1 of the highway test is the narrowest: others braked at most 5.2 m/s2 (limit 6). Not run: the Slow CAV tests (CaoWorkflow, Hci). The CAV is now more careful at junctions (it slows for a vehicle that may turn across its way until it has passed the branch).

## 2026-11-11 night - N-16 and N-17 (nothing published changed; proposals only)
- N-16 CONTROLLER, 2026-11-11 (night list N-16): the reference Controller now runs each Sub-AIM in each composite as its own instance and resolves each Topology connection inside the composite that declares it (M3194: each composite its own pool). Instance name = path of composites + AIMName (e.g. 1MMC-TST-V2.5-I01/1MMC-ASR-V2.5-I01) when an AIMName appears more than once in a Module; unchanged otherwise. Not a schema change. QUESTION for the standard (M3194, AIF V3.0): is the number on a Sub-AIM end of a Topology connection the AIM's own Port Number (as the Controller reads it) or its number in the composite's pool (Numbering 2)? MMC-ASR has OSD-BTO #1 and #2: a pool number would clash with its own numbers. The TST/VCI/ASM pools are NOT numbered until this is answered.
- N-17 XRV-LTP, 2026-11-11: new L3s (software, not standard): AIMs/AMDs/1XRV-END-V1.0-I01.json, 1XRV-REG-V1.0-I01.json, 1XRV-VEG-V1.0-I01.json (make_l3.py), and the Composite 1XRV-LTP-V1.0-I01.json (make_ltp_l3.py, from the L2; the L2 Topology by Port name turned into Data Type + Port Number). MKG: the three AIMs and LTP now have reference software (draft). New App: Apps/XRV/LTP (XRV-LTP.orch). Web: the AIM pages say "Reference Software: under development" - still true (first slice).
- XRV DEFECT (proposal), TFA-CXQ-V1.5 ControlQualifier.json: Formats lists "ANSI E1.20-2010" (RDM), "ANSI E1.37-1-2012", "MIDI", "OSC" - no DMX512 (ANSI E1.11). XRV-REG gives a DMX command as a Control Object; it uses "ANSI E1.20-2010" and writes Protocol DMX in Attributes. Proposal: add "ANSI E1.11-2008" (DMX512-A) to the enum.
- XRV GAP (proposal), XRV-END: the page says the Script-Venue Associations identify the data, but not how an element of RE/VE Data In names its source. The software reads: an Audio/3D Model/Visual/MoCap/LiDAR/Metaverse Object by its own ID, a Control Object by its ControllerID, matched to a VenueElementID of the Associations; the Designation (Characters, PerformerAvatars, TheatricalSetList, Participants, ParticipantAvatars, Consoles) gives the Descriptors. A seat: a Control Object of a source with DataSourceQualifier "seat", ControlData = SeatID. To be written in the END page, or replaced by the author's rule.
- XRV GAP (proposal), XRV-REG/VEG: "incorporates any required Show Descriptors" - which Show Descriptors are required, and for what, is not said. The first slice receives them and does not use them.
- XRV GAP (proposal), XRV-VEG: how a VE Action command becomes VE Data Out is not said; the software puts each command (ActionCommand JSON) as the Data of a Metaverse Object (XRV-MVO), its Qualifier Attributes naming Object and Action. The Metaverse Qualifier (TFA-MAQ-V1.5) Formats has only OtherMetaverseAPIFormat.
- XRV NOTE: the VEG page says Show Descriptors are "required by the RE Experience Generation AIM" - copy of the REG text; should say VE.

**2026-11-11 XRV virtual venue on MMM-TEC (no schema change):** the XRV-LTP Virtual Environment is an M-Instance of MMM-TEC V2.2 (D:\AI\MMM). New device Apps\XRV\LTP\VirtualVenue (MmmVirtualVenue): the show is a collection of Processes (performers' and participants' Users, whatever animates Items); the device is the Process that animates the virtual venue; Virtual Objects placed with MM-Add; a VE command {Object, Property=Intensity, Value} = Property Change with a Light Source (CVE-LTS-V1.0, RQ7 of MMM-PCP-V2.2); VE Data In = the Things that come onto the stage, from MM-Capture. MMM refsoft (MInstance.cs) extended: MM-Add makes an OSD-3DO-V1.5 the Request carries; Property Change applies a carried Light Source (Item.LightSource; Snapshot gives Intensity). Gap: an XRV VE command names a Property freely ("Intensity"); MMM-TEC Property Change changes only Perceptibility, Resize, 3D Model/Visual Object, Acoustic Profile, Light Source, Audio Source, Personal Status, Rights - the mapping of Action Table VE commands to MMM-TEC Process Actions is not written in XRV.

**2026-11-11 CAV orientation follows ISO 8855 (author: "yes, follow ISO 8855"; no schema change):** the CAV software (ESS SAG/BED/BVS, MAS SAG, AMS PSP/MSP/types, simulators and tests) wrote the Orientation as (roll, pitch, yaw) - the yaw third - with no Spatial Qualifier. Now (yaw, pitch, roll): intrinsic Z, Y', X'', right-handed ENU (origin of the Offline Map), vehicle X forward, Y left, Z up; positive yaw = left (anticlockwise from East, as the heading already was), positive pitch = nose down. Every Spatial Attitude of EssJson.Attitude carries a Spatial Qualifier (TFA-SAQ-V1.5, ID ISO8855-ENU: Local ENU, Right, +Z up, +X forward, m, degree, Anticlockwise, Euler ZYX Intrinsic). Points of View (AMS Path, BVS UserPoV) are reordered but carry no Qualifier. GAPS: (1) CAV2 SpatialData.json Inclinometer LongitudinalInclination/LateralInclination give no sign (ISO 8855: pitch + nose down, roll + right side down); (2) the OSD V1.5 web pages of Position/Orientation/Spatial Attitude say nothing of the Spatial Qualifier the schemas have (web changes of 2026-10-31 not published). Not changed: ASM/JSI/MMM software (not CAV) - ASM writes [0,0,180], MMM writes yaw first. Context: Schultens rev2 (OSD Spatial Attitude proposal) proposes the same convention as default.

**2026-11-11 OSD Position/Orientation/Spatial Attitude: ISO 8855 default, angle ranges, ObjectID (author; from the normative text of Schultens rev2). SCHEMA CHANGES, to upload from D:\AI\schemas:** (1) OSD/V1.5/data/Orientation.json: Orientation = oneOf three Euler angles with prefixItems ranges (alpha, gamma in (-180, 180] = exclusiveMinimum -180, maximum 180; beta in [-90, 90]) or four quaternion numbers in [-1, 1]; description: ISO 8855 default (yaw, pitch, roll, intrinsic Z, Y', X''; quaternion (w, x, y, z), w >= 0). (2) OSD/V1.5/data/SpatialAttitude.json: new optional ObjectID (the same in every Spatial Attitude of the Object). (3) OSD/V1.5/data/Position.json: descriptions of CartPosition, SpherPosition (azimuth from +X towards +Y, elevation), CartVelocity, CartAccel - in the reference frame, ISO 8855 default. (4) TFA/V1.5/data/SpatialQualifier.json: description - an absent Qualifier = the default conventions of the Data Type (OSD Position/Orientation/Spatial Attitude: ISO 8855); a Data Type with no stated default does not state its conventions. No SHA-256 pins on these files. WEB DRAFTS: Downloads\OSD-web-spatial-attitude\{position,orientation,spatial-attitude}.html (from the live page HTML; replace the OSD rows of Downloads\Spatial-Qualifier-web-changes.docx); TFA draft page updated for the default. Decided: a contained Qualifier overrides everything, the frame included. OPEN: (a) the beta range binds also a sender whose Qualifier declares a proper-Euler order (ZXZ...); (b) the origin as geodetic point or Object (Qualifier ReferenceFrame.Origin is free text); (c) PointOfView.json Orientation has no ranges (not asked). Software: CAV EssJson.Angle normalises yaw/roll to (-180, 180]; simulators likewise.

Basic API: new 3.1.3 MPAI_AIFU_Credential_Obtain, 3.10.4 MPAI_AIFU_Bout_Give and 3.10.5 MPAI_AIFU_Bout_End; 3.1.2 Destroy revokes the credential. Zero Trust, Conformance, Definitions and the Bout page aligned.
- PTF V1.0 (live): Verification Pipeline 11 and 11.1 check 3 (signer holds an Instance Credential the receiving Controller issued), 11.3 CloseBout by the party that gave the Bout; Definitions and the Trust Operation taxonomy aligned.
- MAS V1.1 (live, internal draft): API 2.1 Create Controller takes a Proof and returns the credential; new 3.3.4 Give Bout and 3.3.5 End Bout; Refusals become 3.3.6 (401, 422 added); 4.2 Delete revokes. Architecture 2.2.3 and actions 5, 6, 21, 23, 28; AIM Description, Scope and Introduction aligned.
- Schemas: AIF/V3.0/data/Bout.json (BoutID description), MAS/V1.1/data/AIMDescription.json (Bout description).
- Test vectors: AIF/V3.0/test-vectors/Bout/ (README.md, Bout-test-vectors.zip, A-each-instance, B-chain; JSON visually aligned), linked from AIF Conformance 5, the AIF Bout page and PTF Conformance Testing 3.8. In the upload set.

- AIF V3.0 MPAI as a Service (live): "The body of an input or an output is the JSON form of the Data Type." replaced by the sentence of the user: Port data travels as native JSON objects or as JSON objects compressed as specified by Communication 9.1 (one Zstandard frame of the canonical form).
- MAS V1.1 API (live): chapter 3 intro, 3.1.1 and 3.2.2: body = native JSON object or compressed JSON object as specified by MPAI-AIF of the Port of the Module; CONTENT-TYPE application/json or application/zstd (MPAI/port-data removed). AIF MPAI as a Service: section reference removed ("as specified by MPAI-AIF").
- {pid}: AIF MPAI as a Service now uses the MPAI-MAS form <DataType>#<PortNumber> (was OSD-BTO-V1.5:2); AIF and MAS V1.1 state that # is written %23 in the request URL (RFC 3986). AIF sentence: "as specified by Section 9.1 of the Communication chapter".
- {pid}: the Data Type is expressed in canonical form (its Header <Standard>-<Acronym>-V<Major>.<Minor>, with its regular expression); example OSD-BTO-V1.5 removed (AIF MPAI as a Service, MAS V1.1 API).
- MAS V1.1 API (live): a boundary Port in a request URL is two path segments {DataType}/{PortNumber} (canonical Header; Port Number ^[1-9][0-9]*$, always present, 1 where the AIM Description gives none); 404 for a bad form or a Port not in that direction; # form not used in URLs. Output Bout paths moved under /Output/{DataType}/{PortNumber}/Bout, input under /Input/{DataType}/{PortNumber}/Bout(/Close). AIF MPAI as a Service table and text aligned; MAS V1.1 Introduction states the change. Software (RemoteControllerApi.cs, MasServer.cs) still on the V1.0 form.
- Decided: AIF AIM Metadata Port gains optional Bout (schema AIF/V3.0/data/AIMMetadata.json, AIM Metadata page row, Communication 9.1 item 8: stated in the AIM Metadata, read with External_List; existing AIM Metadata unchanged: omitted = false = full instances). The proof for the Instance Credential is the CII (PTF-CII) with a fresh CryptographicInstanceTime (Basic API 3.1.3, MAS V1.1 API 2.1).

## 2026-11-12 (night) - CAV driving core (request N-19): proposals, nothing changed
- CAV2 V2.0 FullEnvironmentDescriptors.json, FullEnvironmentObject.PredictedPath: one path only (T, East, North), with no probability and no uncertainty. The new driving core (AIMs\CAV\V2.0\Driving, MotionInference.cs) infers SEVERAL hypotheses per road user (goes on, turns left/right, changes lane, gives way at the line, brakes hard, steps into the road), each with a probability and a radius of uncertainty growing with time. PROPOSAL: PredictedPaths, an array of { Probability (0..1), Label (text), Points [ { T, East, North, Sigma (m) } ] }, PredictedPath kept for one path. Not changed (published schema); the core is not yet in the FED AIM, so nothing is written to the schema today.
- CAV2 V2.0 FullEnvironmentDescriptors: no place for what the CAV cannot see (how far it sees along each lane; areas hidden by a stopped vehicle). The core uses both (scene Visibility and HiddenAreas) to assume a road user stopped beyond the view, a car coming on the oncoming lane from beyond the view, a pedestrian stepping out from behind a parked van. PROPOSAL: optional Visibility [ { SegmentID, Lane, From, To (m) } ] and HiddenAreas [ { ByObjectID, Edge (East, North), Toward (unit vector) } ]. Not changed.
- CAV-TEC V2.0 web (AMS page): says nothing on predicting other road users or on collisions (the author, 2026-11-01: prediction is our implementation; an informative chapter later if the results are interesting). Results of the night: C:\Users\Thor\Downloads\cav-night-2026-11-12.md.

## 2026-11-12 (evening) - a Port of an AIF type in a MAS request URL
- Software (Apps/MAS/Server/IModuleRunner.cs, PortSegment): the {DataType} segment of a boundary Port is the canonical Header OR a type of the AIF V3.0 type system (Metadata 1.1: string, int32, float64, boolean, ..., a struct or variant in braces, optionally []), the AIFType pattern of AIMMetadata.json. Cause: the MachineID input (string) of the MMC Modules was refused with 404 since 2026-11-11 and the Apps of the MAS App stopped after the first utterance.
- TO DECIDE (web text): MAS V1.1 API and AIF V3.0 MPAI as a Service say {DataType} is the canonical Header only (with its regular expression). They leave out the AIF types a Port may carry. Proposed: "{DataType} is the Data Type in canonical form or a type of the AIF type system (Metadata 1.1); a struct or variant type is percent-encoded." Not changed on the site.
- DONE (author: "Yes, correct them", 2026-11-12 18:48): web text corrected. MAS V1.1 API (page 47491), Introduction: {DataType} "... or a type of the type system of MPAI-AIF (Metadata 1.1), such as string"; "A struct or variant type of the type system of MPAI-AIF is percent-encoded (RFC 3986); no other segment contains a character that needs percent-encoding." AIF V3.0 MPAI as a Service (page 44179): "... or a type of the type system of the Metadata (1.1), such as string, percent-encoded where it is a struct or variant type". No schema changed.

## 2026-11-12 (night) - AIH-HSP watermark: the NoConstraint method (IMT, M3304)
- Software: AIMs/NNW/V2.0/NoConstraint (Mpai.Nnw.NoConstraint): the NoConstraint method (weights at secret positions REPLACED by the watermark; Pearson detection; fingerprint variant) and two AIMs: Post-Training Watermark Insertion and Watermark Extraction. NEW L3s AIMs/AMDs/1NNW-PWI-V2.0-I01.json and 1NNW-WEX-V2.0-I01.json (conform to the NNW1 V2.0 L2s). No schema changed.
- Choices of the implementation (the documents leave them open, M3304 section 8): the Key (NNW-KEY reals) is hashed into the seed of the positions and values; the Payload is carried at 64 values a bit (W_X = bit sign x key values); magnitudes 0.5 to 2 standard deviations of the marked weights (the patent example [-0.2, 0.2] cost 10 points of accuracy on the demo network); presence tested on the strength of the bits at 95%; the method name in the Locator carries the number of bits (the WEX needs it); the first and last layers are avoided.
- HSP: the federated learning demo (Apps/AIH/HSP/FlDemo) marks the frozen network with the PWI AIM and recognises the mark with the WEX AIM; the Key stays with the Back End. The demo method of 2026-11-09 (HFL/ModelWatermark.cs, additive spread spectrum) is removed. Not done: During-Training Watermark Insertion (NNW-DWI), the check by the HFE (it would need the key), the HFE mark.
- Use of the method depends on the licensing declaration of IMT (the author deals with it).

## 2026-11-12 (later) - AIH-HSP federated learning: the network is trained (no longer frozen); the watermark's one use
- Purpose of the Back End's watermark (author, 2026-11-12): the Back End is sure that a trained AIM it receives was made from ITS model. Same mark for every End User (all download the same AIM from the MPAI Store); one key kept by the Back End.
- Software: the End Users now train the NETWORK and the final layer together (AIMs/AIH/V1.1/HFL/Network.cs runs and trains the ONNX network in C# - Conv, Relu, AveragePool, Flatten, Gemm, Mul by a constant - and writes the weights back in place; checked against ONNX Runtime). FrontEndTrainer: the NN Model (AIP-MLO-V1.0) carries the network as its third Data item; images are standardised before the network. HFL averages the final layer AND the network (HflAimProcessor.Vector); RoundPolicy.Evaluate takes the model.
- OnnxFloats moved from Mpai.Nnw.NoConstraint to Mpai.Onnx (AIMs/Onnx) and reads the graph's nodes too; Mpai.Nnw.NoConstraint references Mpai.Onnx.
- NoConstraint: the number of values a bit is now carried in the method name of the Locator ("16 bits, 16 values a bit"); default 64.
- Demo (Apps/AIH/HSP/FlDemo): the Back End trains its first model (network + final layer), marks each version with PWI (16 bits, 16 values a bit: 256 of 5,760 weights), uploads one AIM; End Users train 5 passes, gain 50 cases a round; the Back End runs WEX on every trained AIM and averages only those that carry its mark; 4 rounds. Three runs: the fixed-set accuracy went from 62% to 66-73%; the mark found in every trained AIM and in every average (strength about 4 against a threshold of 1.05).
- Not changed: no schema, no L2, no L3. M3304 (input2000) updated 2026-11-12 on the author's edited copy: sections 5, 6, the row Threshold and small models, and a new section 10 (implementation, choices, results, limits).
- Open: ModelVersionRegister makes a "Model Licence" per model version; per the author the Model Licence is a JSON schema against which the licence that governs the processing the HBE or a TPU may execute must validate - the register is to be reviewed.

## 2026-11-12 (MKG rebuilt) - links of the AIMs, L3s and Modules to the software components
- New MKG/mkg_software.py, called by build_mkg.py. Each L3 (AIMs/AMDs) is linked to its software component: the project whose assembly is the L3's BinaryName; else the project of the Provider that registers the L3 by name; a composite L3 is marked composite (the Controller executes it from its Topology; each AIM it contains has its own software) with the AIMs it contains; else "no software yet". The project then implements the L3 and the AIM it is an instance of. Result: 152 L3s linked to a project, 29 composite, 42 without software (40 PGM AIMs beyond the first slice; OSD-BOS, -BRS, -BUS, handled as data inside OSD-MSE).
- New kind App: each workflow Apps/**/*.orch, with the Module (L3) it runs and the projects whose code names the workflow (25 Apps).
- GitHub links only for paths in git, with git's case (one path was in the wrong case, five were not in git); the public page publishes a project when it is in git (it used the old label D:\DI, which would now have hidden all software).
- Software is read from D:\AI only (D:\AI-old is no longer read: it added only the old HCI schemas and its old projects).
- Viewer and Finder: an AIM or L3 shows "Implemented by", BinaryName, how its software was found, and the Apps that run it as their Module.
- Built: MKG/mkg.ttl, mkg.jsonld, mkg-viewer.html, mkg-finder.html; public: schemas/mkg/index.html and finder.html (to upload).

## 2026-11-13 - AIH-HSP: the Back End's service and the federated learning round in the workflow, not in code (review finding A3)
- NO STANDARD CHANGE: AIF V3.0 Workflow Description already has 'ask Controller to admit by "<policy>"' and 'ask Controller to leave'; the reference software did not read them. Now: StepKind AdmitService and LeaveService (AIF/UserAgent/Wdl), the interpreter calls MPAI_AIFU_External_Authority / _Leave (IAsyncControllerApi.ExternalAuthorityAsync, ExternalLeaveAsync); JoinServiceTests read both.
- HBE.orch: 'ask Controller to admit by' with the service policy (was HbeUserAgent.ServicePolicy and StartService, removed); HBE.orch and HFE.orch 'ask Controller to leave' on Stop. The User Agent reads the anchor and the rendezvous after the workflow started (a query of the Controller).
- Round of federated learning (author's choice "One round request"): the User Agent offers ONE Federated Learn whose FederatedLearnData holds a Request for each Front End (the schema allows the array); Health Federated Learning counts the Requests and sends each Front End a Federated Learn with its own Request only (the round's FederatedLearnID kept; each addressed by its HFEID). The Module is no longer paused (api.Pause/Resume removed from HbeUserAgent).
- AIF REFERENCE SOFTWARE (not the standard): an AIM may give several Messages at one Output Port from one input - Message.More (the first in Ports, the others in order); dispatched by ContinuousExecutor and ExchangeOnChannels (continuous runs), carried by AimHostServer/AimHostClient ("More"); a Module run per exchange still gives one Message per Port. Web: nothing to change for this (author to say whether AIF Communication should state that an AIM may produce several Messages at a Port).
- Tests: AihFederatedRoundTests.ARoundIsOneRequestAndEachFrontEndIsSentItsOwn; JoinServiceTests admit/leave; Test/Expected/workflows.json re-recorded (HBE 8 offers 9 asks, HFE 6 offers 5 asks - the A1/A2 Ports, missed on A1). AihFirstSliceTests tonight (N-21).

## 2026-11-13 - AIH-HSP: AIH Data in the Private Storage of the Module, under rules (review finding A4, AIF V3.0 Storage 1-3, STO-3)
- NO STANDARD CHANGE: the AIH-HSP pages already say AIH Data Processing "keeps AIH Data in the Private Storage of the AIF, through the storage API of the AIF"; the software kept it in the Shared Storage (MPAI_AIFM_SharedStorage_Put, no rules), which every Module given the location reads.
- Now (AIMs/AIH/V1.1/HDP/AihStore.cs, Provider/AihProvider.cs): AIH Data Processing is given the Private Storage of the Module (the handle the Controller binds, at <the location of 'keep storage in'>/private/<Module>) and writes each AIH Data with MPAI_AIFM_RuledStorage_Put: Category "AIH Data", Readers none named (in the Private Storage of the Module: its writer alone), Time none (kept until deleted); the Controller writes the Trace. No StorageControl: without a central control the writer sets the rules (Storage 3) - none is needed.
- Tests: AihHdpTests.TheBackEndKeeps... checks the Category, the Trace's writer, that HDP reads it and that DIA and the User Agent get NOT_AUTHORISED; AihHdpTests and AihAimsTests give HDP a Module Private Storage.
- FOUND, NOT CHANGED (AIF reference software): on an AIM host apart from the Controller (AimHostServer), the storages of an AIM are under the host's own storage root, not at the location the User Agent gave ('keep storage in'): with HDP on another host, the Vault is not where the End User said. To decide with the author.

## 2026-11-13 - AIH-HSP: training in NNM, the model returned by the Front End, NoConstraint in DWI mode, no licence for NN Models (review finding A8, author's decisions)
- Author: "the front end checks no watermark, but the NoConstraint watermarking method should be used in DWI mode, not PWI mode"; "the selected NNM is sent (code + Model) to HBE. If you want to send only the Model, NNM has a Model output"; "go ahead with A8". On Access: "We have systematically placed Models in Access"; "Currently Terms are optional" - BUT the published AIF V3.0 Metadata page says "Terms, required" (AccessSources row) and MET-10 "Every entry of AccessSources that names data shall state its Terms"; the schema (if/then) and the Controller (BoundAccess.cs:77) follow the page. So the NNM model is NOT yet in Access (A7 waits on this point).
- STANDARD CHANGE: NeuralNetworkModel.json - Output ML Model Object (NNModelOut) restored: the model the AIM holds, trained on the AIH Data it processed; Description. AIHDataProcessing.json - new Input AIP-MLO-V1.0 (NNModelIn). HealthFrontEnd.json - Topology NNM ML Model Object -> HDP; InternalTypes NNModel. L3 1AIH-HDP-V1.1-I01 likewise. Web: NNM page 45101 (Functions text and the three tables with ML Model Object out; Figure 1 = media 47569), AIH Data Processing page 43799 (an ML Model Object input row in its three tables; Figure 1 = media 47568, which also shows the AIH Data input 2 of A6), Health Front End page 43806 (Table 3: NNM out ML Model Object, HDP in ML Model Object; Figure 2 = media 47570). AIH deck slides 5, 16, 18 redrawn. Upload set Downloads\Upload-2026-11-13 refreshed.
- Software: NnmAimProcessor learns as it processes (stand-in: the mean risk score of the AIH Data it processed and their number) and gives the model it holds after each AIH Data; FrontEndHdpAimProcessor keeps it and answers a Federated Learn request with it (FrontEndLearning, its own stand-in training, removed); a Front End with no such model does not answer. HbeUserAgent.PublishModel publishes the first NNM before the first round. ModelAimPublisher: NNM L3 with NNModelOut, the Front End version wires NNM ML Model Object -> HDP. FrontEndTrainer.Train(mark:) = During-Training Watermark Insertion: the mark inserted in the network before training, the marked weights kept as set after every update. ## 2026-11-13 - AIH-HSP: registrations kept in the Private Storage of the Module (review finding A9)
- NO STANDARD CHANGE. AIH Data Processing kept who registered and what was granted (Back End) and the profile it registered with (Front End: Third-Party User or not) in memory only: lawful in a continuous run (AIF V3.0 AIM-4), lost when the Module stops. Now kept like AIH Data: Private Storage of the Module, Category "Registration", no reader named (AIH Data Processing alone reads it), no time; read back the first time the AIM runs after a start (AihStore.PutRegistration / Registrations; Back/FrontEndHdpAimProcessor.Restore).
- Not kept, by design: what is pending in a run (uploads waiting for the Smart Contract, processings waiting for the Licence Confirm, De-ID answers waiting for the copy, AIH Data waiting for ARA, the latest model of NNM) - of that run (AIM-4).
- Tests: AihHdpTests.ABackEndStartedAgainKnowsWhoRegistered (an audit the Tokens grant is served after a restart; Auditing cannot read the record), AFrontEndStartedAgainKeepsTheProfileItRegisteredWith (a Third-Party User's Front End still refuses to store).

## 2026-11-13 - AIF V3.0: Security API - G1, G2, G3 of M3305 applied to the web page (author: "Apply G1-G3 to the Security API page")
- STANDARD CHANGE, web page https://mpai.community/standards/mpai-aif/v3-0/security-api/ (page 44178). No schema change.
- G3: section 1 now lists where the functions are executed (new Table 1): secure area = 4 Secure Storage, 5 Attestation, 6.2-6.9; non-secure area = 3.1 Initialize_Secure, 6.1 Hashing. A non-secure caller reaches the secure area only through the Secure Controller; the Non-Secure Controller passes only the User Agent's Basic API requests (start, pause, resume, stop, status) and Initialize_Secure. Tables renumbered: data_t is Table 2, key_attributes_t Table 3.
- G1: 5.1 MPAI_AIFAT_Get_Token(challenge, challenge_size, token_buf, token_buf_size, token_size) - the token includes the Verifier's challenge (32, 48 or 64 bytes) as its nonce claim; the caller provides the buffer (the sentence "The token buffer is managed by the Implementation" removed, also G10); new 5.2 MPAI_AIFAT_Get_Token_Size.
- G2: new 6.9 Random generation, 6.9.1 MPAI_AIFRNG_Generate_Random(output, output_size); 6.5 now says the IV is generated with it.
- MKG: Security API functions Get_Token (changed signature), Get_Token_Size and Generate_Random (new).
- G10 resolved by the G1 text (caller provides the buffer). Open (M3305): G4-G9 not applied.

## 2026-11-13 - AIF V3.0: G4 of M3305 (Owner of keys and Secure Storage areas) and Zero Trust and Profiles 6.2 (author: "Zero Trust 6.2 + G4")
- STANDARD CHANGE, Security API (page 44178): Table 3 key_attributes_t gains Owner (the AIM Instance, Controller or User Agent that generated or imported the key, set by the Secure Controller; only the Owner uses the key; Copy_Key gives another Owner only with COPY in the Policy and the Controller's authorisation); 6.2.3 Copy_Key says so; section 4: an area belongs to whoever initialised it (its Owner), the same name used by another caller names another area; section 1: the Secure Controller verifies that the caller is the Owner of the key or area the call uses.
- STANDARD CHANGE, Zero Trust and Profiles (page 44177) 6.2, first bullet: "The functions of the Security API that each part executes, and what the Non-Secure Controller passes to the Secure Controller, are specified in section 1 of the Security API (Table 1)."
- No schema change. Open (M3305): G5-G9.

## 2026-11-13 - AIF V3.0: G9 of M3305 - the Secure Profile always attests (author: "Remove useAttestation, the Secure Profile always attests")
- STANDARD CHANGE, Security API (page 44178) 3.1: MPAI_AIFU_Controller_Initialize_Secure() has no parameter (was bool useAttestation). Text: the Secure Profile always uses Attestation; the Controller gives its Attestation Evidence to the MPAI Store, the Trust Authorities and the other Controllers that verify it, and verifies that of the AIMs that execute apart from it.
- MKG: the function's signature changes. No schema change. Open (M3305): G5-G8.

## 2026-11-13 - AIF V3.0: G5-G8 of M3305 applied to the Security API (author: "Apply G5-G8 to the Security API page")
- STANDARD CHANGE, Security API (page 44178):
  - G5: new Table 3, the types (error_t = Basic API 2.3 values incl. MPAI_AIF_NOT_AUTHORISED for a non-Owner, plus new MPAI_AIF_INVALID_SIGNATURE and MPAI_AIF_INSUFFICIENT_ENTROPY; key_id_t; algorithm_t; flags_t WRITE_ONCE / NO_CONFIDENTIALITY / NO_REPLAY_PROTECTION; storage_info_t; key_derivation_step_t SALT/SECRET/INFO; the state types); data_t only where memory locations are exposed, otherwise a byte array. Key attributes became Table 4.
  - G6: new 6.3.3 MPAI_AIFKD_Setup (HKDF-SHA-256 at least), 6.3.4 Input_Bytes, 6.3.5 Output_Key (caller is the Owner), 6.3.6 Abort; 6.3.2 gives the SECRET.
  - G7: 6.1 now Hash_Compute (one-shot), Hash_Setup, Hash_Update, Hash_Finish, Hash_Verify, Hash_Abort (MPAI_AIFCR_Hash removed); 6.7.1/6.7.2 Sign_Message/Verify_Message without state (sign_state_t removed); 6.5.1/6.5.2 renamed MPAI_AIFCIP_Encrypt_Setup/Decrypt_Setup.
  - G8: AIF_ALG_CTR added; ECB on one block only; data between AIMs and in storage encrypted with 6.6 (AEAD); RSA_PKCS1V15_CRYPT only for interoperation, RSA_OAEP for new uses.
- STANDARD CHANGE, AIF V3.0 References: RFC 5869 (HKDF) added.
- MKG: Security API functions renamed/added/removed as above; two new error values. No schema change. M3305 gaps all closed.

## 2026-11-13 - AIF reference software: the Security API implemented (author: "Implement the Security API in the reference software")
- New project AIF\Security (AIF.Security): SecurityApi (the Trusted Services on a root of trust; For(owner) gives a SecurityCaller bound to its Owner), SecurityCaller (every function of the Security API page with its name: Storage_*, Get_Token, Get_Token_Size, Hash_*, KM_*, KX_*, KD_*, MAC_*, CIP_*, AEAD_*, SIGN_*, ASYM_*, Generate_Random), AttestationToken (EAT in COSE_Sign1 ES256 by the identity key: eat_nonce, ueid, iat, mpai-party, mpai-ptf-evidence = the PTF Attestation Evidence over the same challenge; Verify), SecurityTypes (Table 3/4 types, ISecureRoot).
- Secrets: a master key drawn from and sealed by the root of trust to its registers; persistent keys and Secure Storage areas kept AES-256-GCM under it, bound to their place; versions of the areas in a sealed index (replay refused).
- AifError (error_t) moved from AIF.Controller\UserAgent.cs to AIF.Trust\AifError.cs (namespace unchanged), new values BufferTooSmall, InvalidSignature, InsufficientEntropy.
- SimulatedTpm implements ISecureRoot (Random via TPM2_GetRandom); AIF.RootOfTrust references AIF.Security.
- Packages: System.Formats.Cbor 10.0.15, System.Security.Cryptography.Cose 10.0.14 (10.0.0 had advisories).
- Tests: SecurityApiTests (11, software stand-in root), SecurityApiTpmTests (1, simulated TPM: token proven, wrong challenge/key/code/altered refused, master key reopens after reboot, not with other code). Expected trust-root.json: 11 files measured (AIF.Security.dll added).
- Not offered (Failed): DES, DH/FFDH, PURE_EDDSA, RSA_OAEP label (salt), GCM nonce other than 12 bytes, XTS on partial blocks.
- Spec gaps found while implementing, added to M3305 as G11-G15 (proposals, NOT applied): (a) Table 4 has no key size (Bits) - needed to generate a key; (b) 6.4 lists no MAC algorithm (HMAC used); (c) Table 4 has no key type for ChaCha20 (RAW_DATA 256 bits used); (d) 6.5 "the Implementation generates the IV" but no parameter returns it (state.Iv used); (e) no NOT_SUPPORTED error for an algorithm an Implementation does not offer (Failed used).
- Not yet: MPAI_AIFU_Controller_Initialize_Secure and the Controller split (Secure/Non-Secure Controller); HSP not moved to Secure.

## 2026-11-13 - AIF V3.0: G11-G15 of M3305 applied to the Security API (author: "Apply them")
- STANDARD CHANGE, Security API (page 44178): Table 4 key Type gains CHACHA20 (G13) and a new attribute Bits (G11); 6.4 lists ALG_HMAC_SHA_256/384/512 with a HMAC key (G12); 6.5 the caller gets the generated IV in its iv buffer, *iv_length 0 on entry (G14); Table 3 error_t gains MPAI_AIF_NOT_SUPPORTED (G15). Not applied: the G15 sentence on stating unsupported algorithms in AIF Metadata (needs an AIF Metadata field; author to decide).
- STANDARD CHANGE, AIF V3.0 References (page 44165): RFC 2104 (HMAC).
- Software aligned: KeyType.ChaCha20, AifError.NotSupported (DES, DH/FFDH, PURE_EDDSA, OAEP label, GCM nonce != 12, XTS partial blocks, SHA-3/ChaCha20 absent from the platform), Encrypt_Setup/Decrypt_Setup(state, key, alg, Span iv, ref ivLength); CipherState.Iv removed. 12 Security API tests pass.

## 2026-11-13 - AIF: the Controller of the Secure Profile split (author: "Now do the Controller split"; chose "Relay, end-to-end")
- STANDARD CHANGE, Security API (page 44178) section 1: my G3 text said the Non-Secure Controller passes only start/pause/resume/stop/status - too narrow (Port data, Shared Storage, Join/Authority had no path). Now: it relays every Basic API request of the User Agent and its reply; what they carry is encrypted and authenticated end to end between the User Agent and the Secure Controller with a key agreed at MPAI_AIFU_Controller_Initialize_Secure; the Secure Controller serves only the User Agents its User decided, each proving its identity; the Non-Secure Controller never has that key. 3.1: the User Agent verifies the Attestation of the Secure Controller (a token over a challenge that binds the agreed key) before agreeing the key.
- Software, new project AIF\SecureProfile: SecureController (holds the Controller and the Security API; Hello: ephemeral ECDH key, EAT over SHA-256(Nonce||UaKey||ScKey), HKDF session key, all through the Security API; Sealed: AES-GCM, replay refused; executes every IControllerApi request), NonSecureController (relays frames, reads none), SecureControllerClient (the User Agent's IControllerApi; MPAI_AIFU_Controller_Initialize_Secure verifies the token, signs its Hello with its identity key), Frames + TcpFrameLink/FrameServer (loopback link to the secure area).
- Test SecureProfileSplitTests (simulated TPM): Module answers as directly; the relay sees no Module data; replay, alteration, a session of the relay's own, another party, unapproved code refused. trust-root.json: 12 files measured.
- Not yet: the Secure Controller as its own process with the App's AIM providers (the link is loopback TCP within the test process); the AIMs executing apart attest to the Secure Controller as before (AIM host), not changed; HSP not moved to Secure; InputWrite/OutputRead through the split not exercised by a test (Advance and Status are).

## 2026-11-13 - AIF V3.0: S1, S4, S5, S6, S7 from the security implementation applied to the web spec (author: "Apply them")
- STANDARD CHANGE, Zero Trust and Profiles (44177): new 6.3 The Profile of an AIM - APIProfile of AIM Instance Metadata = the Profile of its Implementation; of AIM Metadata = the least Profile of its Implementations (a Secure Implementation may implement Basic AIM Metadata); a Secure AIM is built only where its party has a root of trust and the Security API, else the Controller refuses the Module with MPAI_AIF_FAILED and the reason; the Sub-AIMs of a Composite declare its Profile (S1). Section 5: the Controller measures the package of the Implementation - each file it loads - and the models (S7).
- STANDARD CHANGE, Metadata (44173) APIProfile row: in AIM Metadata the least Profile, in AIM Instance Metadata the Profile of the Implementation (S1).
- STANDARD CHANGE, Security API (44178): 4 - replay protection of an area (without NO_REPLAY_PROTECTION) by a monotonic counter of the root of trust, also when the whole storage is put back (S6); 5.1 - the token is a COSE_Sign1 by the identity key, claims eat_nonce (10), ueid (256: 0x01 + SHA-256 of the identity key's SPKI), iat (6), mpai-party, mpai-ptf-evidence; what a Verifier checks (S4).
- STANDARD CHANGE, Storage (44171): in the Secure Profile what an AIM keeps in the Private Storage of its Module or the Shared Storage is encrypted and authenticated with a key of the AIM held by the Security API (S5).
- STANDARD CHANGE, MPAI Store (44175): the fingerprint of an Implementation is that of its package - SHA-256 of the list (in path order) of path and SHA-256 of each file the Implementation loads; a model's is the SHA-256 of its file (S7).
- Reference Software (44181) section 6: not yet implemented - the package fingerprint (one file measured) and the MPAI_AIF_FAILED refusal of a Secure AIM without Security API (the provider throws).

## 2026-11-14 (night, N-23) - AIH-HSP: a Secure mode for FlDemo (Apps\AIH\HSP\FlDemo --secure); no standard change
- Software only, uncommitted: Apps\AIH\HSP\FlSecure.cs (FlCode, FlParty, SealedSharedStorage, FlWatermarkKey, FlFrontEndSecurity, FlUpdateGate), FlDemo --secure, test Test\Mpai.Aif.Tests\AihFlSecureTests.cs (2 Facts, simulated TPMs). Each party (Back End, three Front Ends) has a simulated TPM 2.0 (ports 2501-2531) and a Security API of AIF V3.0.
- What the Secure Profile is used for, as a CHOICE of the demonstration (no AIF or AIH text says how a federated-learning round uses the Trusted Services): the End User's vault sealed (AES-256-GCM under a persistent key of the vault's Owner, MPAI_AIFAEAD_*); the Back End's watermark key a persistent RawData key of its Security API, exported only into memory to make the Key (NNW-KEY-V2.0) given to WEX at its Input Port; each Front End signs the AIP-MLO it returns (MPAI_AIFSIGN_Sign_Message, ECDSA P-256) and answers a 32-byte challenge of the Back End with MPAI_AIFAT_Get_Token; the Back End checks the token (AttestationToken.Verify, approved code = PartyCode + Mpai.Aih.Hfl.dll) and the signature (MPAI_AIFSIGN_Verify_Message on the imported public key).
- Possible standard points, NOT proposed as changes (for the author to judge): (1) AIH-HSP V1.1 does not say that the Back End checks the attestation of a Front End before taking its PostLearnNNModel - in the platform the Controllers do it (Zero Trust); FlDemo, having no Controllers, does it itself. (2) The Federated Learn response carries no signature of the Front End: in the platform the Controller signs its answers. Nothing to change while the Controllers are in the path.
- Also fixed in FlDemo (Basic mode): it read the NNM model as a Store-held file (repository.FileOf(... .model.json)), which fails since A7 (the model is data of Access); it now takes the model from the AccessSources of the L3 (Uri + SHA-256 checked).

## 2026-11-14 (day) - XRV-LTP vs AIF V3.0 review: X6 applied (author: "Tell operator, go on"); no standard change
- AIF\UserAgent\Ua\WorkflowInterpreter.cs (reference software of the User Agent): 'on Degraded:' now runs in continuous execution too, as Workflow Description (44176) and Communication (44170) say - (1) a write to the boundary that fails (a device's stream, a record played) runs it once, until a write succeeds again; the outcome of every continuous write was dropped before; (2) the status watcher runs it each time an AIM leaves ALIVE; (3) an 'on Degraded:' that stops the Module ends the workflow. The guard against running it twice at once is thread-safe. No App workflow other than XRV-LTP has an 'on Degraded:' block, so no other App changes behaviour.
- Apps\XRV\LTP\XRV-LTP.orch: on Degraded: prompt "Part of the show is not running as designed: {Status}. The show goes on." (the Module's policy is Continue; the operator decides).
