## 2026-11-14 (day) - AIF V3.0 MPAI Store page (44175): who calculates the fingerprint, and how (author: "Apply both drafts to the MPAI Store page") - STANDARD CHANGE (web)
- Table 1 "Fingerprints": "Calculates and records the fingerprint of each Implementation the AIM Instance Metadata names, from its package where the Implementer keeps it (4), ..."
- 3 Submission, new paragraph after "Otherwise the Store publishes it...": the Store obtains each package from the location the Metadata states, calculates its fingerprint (4), records it as approved, keeps no copy; an Implementer's own fingerprint that differs is a finding (the Store keeps its own); a package it cannot obtain or inspect has no fingerprint (finding; a Controller is answered NotRecorded).
- 4 Verification: the method - a model: SHA-256 (FIPS 180-4) of its file, 64 lower-case hex; an Implementation: its package = every file at the location (archive files, or the one file), whether loaded or not; per file "path SP sha256hex LF", path relative, "/", no leading "/" or "./", UTF-8 as in the package; lines in ascending byte order of paths; fingerprint = SHA-256 of the joined lines, lower-case hex. Worked example (unnumbered): Mpai.Example.deps.json "{}", Mpai.Example.dll "MPAI", lib/Mpai.Example.Common.dll "AIF" -> 2581d32593c2c97cc29c63ee52bd2a9d5d0654a601a80749c0a2b735d93f48d7.
- NOW INCONSISTENT, author to decide: Zero Trust and Profiles (44177) "the package of the Implementation - each file of it that the Implementation loads -"; Reference Software (44181) step 8 describes the software (binary + libraries it loads), which is not yet aligned with the new method (all files, lower-case hex, paths with folders).
- Software to change: Store\ImplementationFingerprints (all files of the package, lower-case, relative paths with '/'), re-measure the submitted AMQ/XRV packages.
- Author's point (GME): MPAI-GME specifies what happens outside AIF and MAS; in principle the MPAI Store page belongs to GME, which is not ready to host it yet.
- ALIGNED (author: "Yes, align them"): Zero Trust and Profiles 44177 - "the package of the Implementation - every file of it (MPAI Store 4) -"; Reference Software 44181 step 8 - "(...libraries of the package it loads; MPAI Store 4 now defines the fingerprint over every file of the package, see 6)"; 6 Not implemented - the fingerprint as MPAI Store 4 defines it (the software: binary + libraries it loads, not AIF.*, file names only, upper-case hex; no Implementer fingerprint yet). Checked live.
- SOFTWARE ALIGNED with MPAI Store 4 (author: "Yes, do it"), uncommitted: Store\ImplementationFingerprints - OfDirectory (every file, any depth), OfSingleFile, OfPackage (every file of the zip; null if the binary is not in it), OfFiles (paths with "/", no leading "/" or "./", lower-case hex, ascending UTF-8 byte order, lower-case result), OfImplementation = the folder of the binary (an obtained package's folder; for an AIM built into a program, the program's folder); PackageFiles kept only for building packages. Store\AimRepository obtains and keeps the whole package (folder, zip or one file) by its paths and measures it; FileOf measures the kept package. Store\MpaiStore.Approve measures the location the L3 states (folder / zip / one file; else the binary's folder). Store\Service\PackageCheck: a location that is one file is a package of one file. Tests: PackageFingerprintTests - the worked example of the web page gives 2581d325...48d7 as folder, zip and list; every file of a package (loaded or not) is in its fingerprint; TrustEvidence masks lower-case; StoreHttpSimulation reads Mpai.Mmc.Asr.dll. Passing: 195 trust/Store/package/XRV/AMQ tests (AMQ from its published packages, real run); AIH demo Basic and Secure. No stored approvals to re-measure (none tracked; MAS service uses no Store).
- Reference Software 44181 updated: step 8 "(every file of the package at the location the AIM Instance Metadata states, by the method of MPAI Store 4)"; 6 - only "An Implementer cannot yet submit a fingerprint of its own with its AIM Instance Metadata (MPAI Store 3)". Checked live.

- MAS Service: UscProvider added, AES and SME offered (mas-server-AI.json); codec MMC-EEM-V2.5 registered; SelectorCodec keeps every element of the schema instance (SelectorID, Language...) both ways, adds the flat languages inside only; CAE Speech Object reader (Mpai.Osd.Speech.SpeechObject) also reads the Mpai.Core form the Service gives; Ua.SpeechWav (Speech Object -> one WAV; a chosen WAV -> 16 kHz mono).
- Clients: browser (WAV chooser beside the microphone, emotion list + slider, speech presenter + Play again, rca.playWav) and desktop (small window "Choose a WAV file...", emotion dialog, speech presenter, Play again button). Interpreter: a value written in a workflow is also the datum of its label (fixes "{Welcome}" shown literally by "display Welcome").
- Stand-alone AesApp/SmeApp answer the same workflow from their command-line files (Support/SpeechFiles.cs); AesApp --language removed (the workflow says en).
- Tried in the browser (in-app pane, microphone blocked there, so synthetic WAV files): AES (Anger 80 %) and SME both play the result. Desktop client built, NOT tried by hand. Tests: MasCaeAppsTests (4), WdlInstanceValueTests (9), CaeUscV25SmeAesTests pass; MAS/MMC/codec/workflow run 80 pass, 1 fail (MasEndToEndTests.Step7EndToEnd, failed the same way the night before).
- OPEN: the ready-made model utterances for SME (CREMA-D, 4 emotions, one actor) - the author has not answered; persuasive is not an emotion.

## 2026-11-15 (night, N-26) - XRV-LTP V1.0: the Status Interpretation AIMs read the body, the applause and the facing; two readings of Cue Conditions; a Cue with no group ends the show - software, uncommitted; PROPOSALS for the standard, nothing of the web or the schemas changed
- Software: AIMs/XRV1/V1.0/Common/Behaviour.cs (new), Interpretation.cs, JsonMatch.cs; END/End.cs; ACG/CueEngine.cs, Acg.cs; REG/Reg.cs; VEG/Veg.cs; MMM/Instance/MInstance.cs (MM-Add of an Audio Object OSD-AUO-V1.5, Kind "Audio" in MM-Capture); Apps/XRV/LTP/VirtualVenue/MmmVenue.cs, ProcessActionPlayer.cs (new); Apps/XRV/LTP/TwoMetaverses (new demo); Test/Data/XRV/LongerEvening (new show).
- PROPOSAL 1 (Status Interpretation, web pages of PFI/PTI and the Status Data Types): say how the Status elements are obtained from the Descriptors, or that it is the Implementer's. Done here as the simplest reading: Visual Behaviour from a Body Descriptors Object (Gesture "Raising arms" when both upper arms are above 135 degrees; StandSit from hip and knee flexion); Audio Behaviour ClappingIntensity from an Audio Object whose ID is the participant's (loud, noise-like frames); Spatial Attitude to the nearest metre and 45 degrees.
- PROPOSAL 2 (StandSit, VisualBehaviour.json): the schema says only "boolean". Taken: true = standing, false = sitting. Please state it in the schema description and the web page.
- PROPOSAL 3 (Cue Conditions, Script/Cue List web page): how a target is met. Two readings were needed: (a) an element named ...Intensity (0-100) in a target is a MINIMUM (met by a value at least as high); (b) a list of numbers (CartPosition, Orientation) is a TUPLE, met element by element (before, [0,0,0] was met by [180,0,0]). Please say so in the text or give a way to write thresholds.
- PROPOSAL 4 (Cue List): nothing says how a group that is not in the last Cue ends the show (NextCue is a string). Taken: a Cue with no group (here "END") can never fire, so reaching it the show has ended. Alternatives: a reserved NextCue value, or an EndOfShow flag in the group.
- PROPOSAL 5 (where a body and a facing travel in a Data In): taken as the text of a Data item: a Body Descriptors Object (PAF-BDO-V1.6) and a Spatial Attitude (OSD-OSA-V1.5) in the MoCap Data of a MoCap Object (RE Data In) or in the Metaverse Object Data (VE Data In); END puts them in the Descriptors (RE: BodyDescriptorsObject + SpatialAttitude; VE: AvatarOrAvatarID[Portable Avatar with BodyDescriptorsObject] + SpatialAttitude) and the Audio Objects of performers/participants in the MultimodalSceneDescriptors of their Descriptors. The 3D Model Object has no Spatial Attitude (its SpaceTime is a Simple Time): a place for it would help.
- PROPOSAL 6 (why the Module acted): the Action IDs (OSD-BTO) carry in DescrMetadata "Cue Cx (Kind): <Status kind> (<Data Type>): <what it says>"; REG and VEG copy it into the DescrMetadata of the RE/VE Data Out, so the User Agent can show it. If wanted, the web pages of ACG/REG/VEG can say so.
- MMM-TEC (MM-Add): an Audio Object carried by the Request is made where it is added (the applause of the audience), public where the M-Location is public. Not in MMM-TEC V2.2 text.
- Rebuild the MKG for XRV-LTP (Behaviour readings as notes on PFI/PTI).

- 2026-11-16 (later) Door use case: a Persona walks with MM-Animate (author: "you could walk a Persona with a veeery long list of MM-Move's"). BKN personaMoves -> MM-Animate PA (RQ1 Persona, RQ3 At M-Location, RQ4 Stream = the Portable Avatar, filled by AUC); every Portable Avatar is the Stream of an MM-Animate of the Persona; M-Instance MM-Animate takes a PAF-PAV in With, walks the Item At an M-Location over time (1 m/s), Snapshot Animating/Walking; the User Agent answers the Module when the animation is over (so "Reached" comes from the M-Instance). After a Condition held, BKN gives two Final Responses: walk + Process Actions without Text, then (continuation "Process Actions done") the Text "Welcome". AUC takes a Process Action Response only by its ProcessActionID and each Datum once (the Controller showed a repeated input). Visitor walks by MM-Animate too (Porch, Hall M-Locations). Tests: PgmConditionTests, PgmConditionOllamaTests; 38 PGM tests pass. Open: author says BKN sends Process Actions to AUC directly (AUF gets Text + Actions only) - published L2s (BasicKnowledge, AUserFormation, FinalResponseDemultiplexing, AutonomousUser topology) route them through AUF/FRD; needs an L2 change, not done. - 2026-11-18 (night, N-34 OSD-TMA who/what/where/when; IMPLEMENTATION ONLY, no schema or L2 changed): CVE-VCD (TMA) gives each scene its PROGRAMME time (relative Simple Time) and, with the setting SampleSeconds, one frame a second with BasicVisualObjectProperties.FrameTimes; PAF-FIR for TMA (1PAF-FIR-V1.6-I02, new class FirEachFace) gives EVERY face of every sampled frame as an Instance Identifier with InstanceTime and InstanceSpaceTime (Spatial Attitude: centre of the box in pixels, accuracy half the box, Spatial Qualifier ReferenceFrame Screen / origin the top-left corner / LengthUnit pixel); small faces matched to the registered people by best likeness >= 0.25 and a margin of 0.08 over the next (the gallery's 0.35 is too strict for 30-50 px faces); OSD-MSM records the faces as Basic Visual Objects (VisualObjectType Face, ObjectID the IID) of the Visual Scene of their time, places an utterance where its speaker's face is (BasicSpeechObjectSpaceTime.SpatialAttitude1) and records the pair in AlignedMMObjects (AlignmentCode SpeakerFace); OSD-IDR (TMA) confirms a voice by a face of the same person seen during the utterance; OSD-BVS takes the picture's BasicVisualObjectTime (the visual codec dropped it); the FIR Bounding Box Port is not connected in the TMA L2 - not needed, the IID carries the place. DEFECT FOUND (not fixed in the shared code): the typed TextQualifier of Mpai.Core (AIMs\Core\Qualifiers.cs) writes SubType and Format where TFA-TXQ-V1.5 requires SubTypes and Formats: every Text Object of Whisper ASR (and of other AIMs using the typed model) fails its schema; TMA's MSM writes the schema's names when it records the words; the model should be corrected (it is used by many Modules: to be done by day).

- 2026-11-19 (author: "Yes, build it" - a JSON representation of the Vienna Convention in the software): CAV-TEC V2.0 (draft; V1.1 unchanged) schemas/CAV2/V2.0/data/TrafficRules.json REWRITTEN, Header CAV-TRR-V2.0. Was: Article7..Article34 objects of boolean const true, whose descriptions did not match the Convention (e.g. its Article9 "keep to the side" is Article 10, its Article11 "pedestrians" is Article 20/21, its Article25 "lights" is Article 32). Now: required Header, TrafficRulesID, Sources (instruments, text used, amendments included or not), Jurisdiction (Code, Name, ConventionParty, BasedOn, DomesticLaw), Parameters (by name: Value, Unit, Source Convention/Domestic/Unset, Reference, Verified; TrafficSide Right/Left and UnsignedIntersectionPriority FromRight/FromLeft/NoGeneralRule required), Rules (RuleID VC68-<art>-<par>, Article, Paragraph, Title, Status Binding/Recommended/DomesticOption, Modality Must/MustNot/May, AppliesTo, Context, Behaviour, Parameters, Automation Driving/Vehicle/Operator/Expectation, Text, SatisfiedBy, Amendment), Signs (SignID, Name, Category A-H/Lights/Markings/Works/LevelCrossing, Group, Effect). Data in AIMs/CAV/V2.0/TrafficRules/Data (75 rules: Articles 5-34 of the 1968 text from the UN Treaty Collection + Article 34bis; 170 signs from M2430; profiles RHT, LHT, IT, GB - the IT and GB domestic values not verified). The CAV-TEC V2.0 Traffic Rules web page must follow the new schema (not done). Upload TrafficRules.json. Correction 2026-11-19 evening: the rewrite had dropped DataXMData (PTF V1.0 Data Exchange Metadata) and DescrMetadata (string, max 2048), which the other CAV V2.0 data types keep; both put back as before. Uploaded twice (the second with the correction); web page updated to the schema.

## 2026-11-26 MMM: Accounts with credentials in the implementation - PROPOSALS for MMM-TEC V3.0 (nothing of the web or the schemas changed)
- Personal Profile (MMM-PPR): the implementation adds PersonalProfile.Credential (the password a human registers with; the M-Instance keeps only a salted hash and removes it from the Personal Profile Item) and Data.Organisation. Neither is in the schema; with AccountLevel and PersonaChoice already used there, four fields outside the schema.
- Register is also how a human with an Account ENTERS (the user, 2026-11-26: "Register, in the sense that the person entering the MMM inputs their credentials"): Register of an existing humanID with its Credential -> Ack (200) and new tokens for its Users; wrong Credential -> InsRights; no such Account -> IncID. Register by one of the human's Users (its token) with a PersonaChoice makes a Persona it lacks.
- Account Levels: Visitor (first name, last name, password), Participant and Officer (also email and organisation); the third level renamed Officer (was MPAI Officer). At the end of a Visitor's visit (no request for 60 s) its Persona is removed and offered again.
- The person's Thalia is the person's User: it keeps the Account and registers or enters for the person (Thalia\Core\MmmAccount.cs).
- (same day, later) Every person registers and IS its Account (the name no longer comes from an invitation link); an invitation is the Rights Change the AVS Service gives to the User of the Account whose email was invited (--invite-emails); the Account Item (MMM-ACC) carries Email (not in its schema); an Officer whose organisation is MPAI is called an MPAI Officer.
- (same day) Manager Level: not offered; the Account whose email is the server's --manager is the Manager's (one person), called MMM Manager, covering every other level (Officer's Rights to every place, a seat in any meeting).
- (same day) Linking a device to an Account (proposal for MMM-TEC V3.0): a program acting for the person (Thalia, the person's User) asks the M-Instance for a link and shows a code; the person, entered with its password, approves it in the world; the device gets a key of its own (kept as a hash, like the password) and enters with PersonalProfile.DeviceKey instead of the password (another field outside the schema); the person lists its devices and removes a link at any time. Endpoints /api/link/request, info, approve, status (key given once), devices, remove: not in MMM-TEC.

## 2026-11-27 MMM: H-User and A-User, the role at the first entry, Officer by request, Visitors forgotten - PROPOSALS for MMM-TEC V3.0 (nothing of the web or the schemas changed)
- Two Users per registered human (the user, 2026-11-27): the H-User, driven by the human directly, and the A-User (Autonomous User), commanded by text or speech and acting with autonomy; one of each at most. Which is which follows from the Persona chosen for it: the A-Users' Personae are the military ones of the catalogue (so everybody sees an A-User). The Personal Profile (MMM-PPR) has no field for the kind of User; the M-Instance keeps it (Process.Autonomous, kept between runs). The A-User of <name> is <name>-A; it places its own Persona with its own token.
- A Visitor has one H-User only, no A-User, with a Persona of another class (the sports Personae); other H-Users take neither sports nor military Personae. The Visitor status is transient: at the end of the visit the whole Account goes (Users, Persona, Personal Profile, credentials, devices) and the person registers again next time.
- The role (Account Level) is chosen at the first entry, not at registration: an Account Thalia opens has first name, last name, organisation and email (all four required in Thalia) and no level; the first Register with an AccountLevel sets it, once. Offered from the bottom: Visitor, Participant, Officer (Manager never offered).
- Officer by request: a person choosing Officer who is not designated by the chair enters as a Participant; the request goes to the MMM Manager, who approves or refuses it (with a reason) in the Manager Console (a panel of the world page, Manager only; endpoints /viewer/manager/requests and /viewer/manager/answer); the person is told once (at entry or while in the world). Requests kept between runs. Meetings without Accounts keep the old rule (Officer offered only to the chair and the designated).
- The A-User is offered at every entry until the person answers Yes or Never (Later: next time); Never is kept by the M-Instance and is final for now.
- Thalia: no Account Level setting any more; the role is the MMM's question.

## 2026-11-27 MMM implementation: Rights holders instead of owners, the MPAI Building Service, Rights by Account Level, the A-User takes commands
- CORRECTION OF THE IMPLEMENTATION (not an MMM-TEC change: MMM-TEC has only Rights): an Item of the M-Instance had an "Owner"; it now names the Process holding the Rights to it (Item.Holder; in MM-Capture "Holder"; a kept state with "Owner" is read as Holder).
- The meeting Service of the MMM was named after ABV's AVS; it is now the MPAI Building Service (Process MPAIBuildingService): it holds the Rights to the places of the building (not their owner) and gives Rights by Rights Change. The minutes name it "MPAI Building Service".
- Rights by Account Level (the user, 2026-11-27), held by each User of the Account, H-User and A-User alike, given by the MPAI Building Service (MM-Move To and MM-Animate At each place): public places (Metaverse Square, Atrium, Library, Cafe - Library and Cafe made public) need none; Visitor nothing more; Participant the Member Companies area (Members' wing, Members' rooms 1-4) and, by invitation, the meeting room; Officer also the MPAI meeting rooms and the Offices of MPAI; Manager every place. Only the Manager's H-User (not its A-User) acts on the Service (Manager Console).
- The A-User takes commands (AllApps\MMM\Server\AUserAgent.cs): its own PGM-AUA Module, the world's places; the human commands it from its page. BKN: a Module that knows places and no Items changes no Item.

## 2026-11-27 MMM implementation: the doors are Items
- Each door of world.json is an Item of the M-Instance (OSD-3DO-V1.5, Kind Door) at the place it is entered from, giving access to the place beyond (Item.Leads, an implementation field); the MPAI Building Service holds the Rights to it. Item Properties OpenState (starts Open) and LockState (MMM-IPR-V3.0).
- The rule (the user: anything can be an Item; no decision specific to doors): a User may change an Item that gives access to an M-Location if it may enter that M-Location; a closed such Item stops a Persona on the way in or out (MM-Move, MM-Animate: Clash "... is closed").
- The page draws each door as the M-Instance says, opens a closed door when the person walks at it (if allowed), and C closes/opens the nearest door. An A-User opens the closed doors on its way and knows the doors by name.

## 2026-11-27 MMM: the CAV Proving Ground (the user: start with the CAV Proving Ground; the M-Instance offers new experiences, not a copy of the Universe)
- SCHEMA CHANGE (MMM4/V3.0/data/ItemProperty.json, MMM-IPR-V3.0): four Property Names added to the vocabulary - Signal (Red, Amber, Green, Off: the light a Traffic Light shows), TimeOfDay (0-24 hours: the local time of day of an M-Location), Weather (Clear, Rain, Fog, Snow: of an M-Location), Friction (0-1: road surface / tyre). Not uploaded.
- New M-Location ProvingGround (public), held by the Proving Ground Service, reached through a Gate on the Metaverse Square (Item Gate-ProvingGround, Kind Gate, at MVS, giving access to the Proving Ground; one Item, a side on the Square and a side on the Entrance Plaza). Its conditions are Item Properties of its M-Location (TimeOfDay, Weather, Friction); 12 traffic lights are Items (Kind TrafficLight, Signal) at 6 junctions, cycled by the Service (50 s: NS green 20, amber 3, all red 2, then EW).
- Layout (World\make_proving_ground.py, writes Viewer\world\proving-ground.glb and adds environments/gates to world.json): the Town (grid of 2-lane streets, 3.5 m lanes, blocks with buildings, a park, zebra crossings, stop lines), the Roundabout, the Test Straight (3 lanes, 250 m), the Motorway (2x2 lanes 3.75 m, hard shoulder, median barrier, on-ramp with acceleration lane), the Country Road (winding, trees) to the Car Park. The roads are described exactly in world.json (centre lines, lanes, lane widths). World\meshes.py: the Mesh class moved out of make_world.py (shared).
- Rights: Property Change of an Item is also allowed by a Right (a Rights Item naming a Property Change PA with Nil the Item); the Right to change an Item is given only by the Process holding the Rights to it (Rights Change now checks the Nil of a Property Change PA). The Proving Ground Service gives the Right to change the conditions to Participant, Officer and Manager Users; a Visitor looks.
- Page (Viewer\index.html): the Gate is walked through like a door; in the Proving Ground a panel changes Time, Weather, Road grip (403 without the Right); sky, sun, fog, rain and snow drawn as the M-Instance says; traffic lights lit as their Signal; Shift runs.
- Not yet: the CAV - a Process that drives, i.e. moves a Vehicle Item (the user: a Persona is just an Item, a vehicle is just an Item; never "a vehicle Persona"), hazards on command, ghosts of runs, the CAV's sensors (what its cameras/LiDAR/radar would sense), terrain (the hill of the country road: the ground is flat).
